# Trainer dialogue inventory

Static, conditional reachability; editorial findings are separate from this generated inventory.

## CeladonCity — LORI (TRENDSETTER_F)

- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsetterLori_Defeat` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:299): Now that's a look I remember!$
- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsetterLori_Intro` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:296): The shops have new arrivals.\nMy team has new moves!$
- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsetterLori_Post` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:302): Confidence is always in style.$

## CeladonCity — TAMIA & LEO (TRENDSETTERS)

- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsettersTamiaLeo_Defeat` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:313): Our coordination came apart!$
- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsettersTamiaLeo_Intro` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:310): Our outfits match.\nOur moves work together too!$
- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsettersTamiaLeo_NeedTwo` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:319): Bring two POKéMON and show us\nyour best combination!$
- `CeladonCity_EventScript_TrendsettersTamiaLeo_Post` (data/maps/CeladonCity/scripts.inc:316): A good partner brings out\nyour best.$

## CeladonCity — TERRY (RIVAL_EARLY)

- `CeladonCity_Text_RivalDefeat` (data/maps/CeladonCity/text.inc:19): Hey!\nI was testing you!\lObviously.$
- `CeladonCity_Text_RivalEvolutionIntro` (data/maps/CeladonCity/text.inc:6): {RIVAL}: There you are.\nTook you long enough to reach\lCELADON.\pYou noticed the GYM, right?\nOr did the giant building still\lneed a sign just for you?\pI already got what I needed from\nERIKA. Try not to look impressed.\pBefore you go asking ERIKA for\nfield notes, let's see if your\lteam can keep up with mine.$
- `CeladonCity_Text_RivalEvolutionPostBattle` (data/maps/CeladonCity/text.inc:24): GYM LEADERS aren't just strong.\nThey know how POKéMON change when\lpeople train with them.\pERIKA studies that bond up close.\nTry listening for once.\pIf you beat a GYM LEADER, don't\nget too excited.\pThey're testing you, so they adjust\ntheir teams to how many BADGES you\lhave.$

## CeladonCity_GameCorner — OSWIN (GAMBLER)

- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerOswinAfter` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:154): The machines leave plenty to luck.\nIn battle, I can learn from\pthe choices I make.$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerOswinDefeat` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:151): That was more than luck!$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerOswinIntro` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:147): I am taking a break from the slots.\nCare to test your luck in battle?$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerOswinRematch` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:159): Another battle?\nLet us see if I learned anything!$

## CeladonCity_GameCorner — BASIL (GAMBLER)

- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerBasilAfter` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:170): A winning streak never lasts\nforever. Know when to stop.\pMy POKéMON enjoy the break too.$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerBasilDefeat` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:167): You knew when to make your move!$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerBasilIntro` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:163): I set my last few COINS aside.\nThere is always a POKéMON battle!$
- `CeladonCity_GameCorner_Text_GamblerBasilRematch` (data/maps/CeladonCity_GameCorner/text.inc:175): My POKéMON are ready again.\nLet us have another friendly match!$

## CeruleanCave_1F — STANLY (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_STANLY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4552): My theory doesn't hold water!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_STANLY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4548): These marks show where the water\nonce reached. Shall we compare\pwhat our teams have learned?$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_STANLY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4554): Water leaves evidence long after\nit moves on. I sketch the marks\pbefore the next flood changes\nthem.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_STANLY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4550): I found a higher water mark.\nPerhaps your team has reached\pnew heights too!$

## CeruleanCave_2F — FOSTER (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_FOSTER_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4536): You reached the weakest layer!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_FOSTER_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4532): Look at the bands in this stone!\nEach layer formed differently.\pMy battle plan has layers too!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_FOSTER_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4538): A loose fragment tells only part\nof the story. I record where it\pcame from before picking it up.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_FOSTER_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4534): I've revised my notes and my\nstrategy. Let's compare them\pagain!$

## CeruleanCave_B1F — LARRY (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_LARRY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4544): Another crack in my theory!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_LARRY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4540): Something disturbed these deep\nrocks. I'm tracing the\pfractures. Your team looks like\na force worth studying!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_LARRY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4546): The lake hides the ends of these\nfractures. I won't guess what\plies beneath without evidence.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_RUIN_MANIAC_LARRY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4542): The stone hasn't moved, but my\nideas have. Time to test them!$

## CeruleanCity_Gym — MISTY (LEADER)

- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_ExplainCascadeBadge` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:20): That BADGE marks a real change\nin your journey.\pKeep building your FIELD AIDE\nrecord, and outsider POKéMON\lwill trust you more.\pA puny fish can become a mighty,\nraging POKéMON.\pThat's the beauty of change.\pBring me that GYARADOS.\nThen I'll show you trade evolution\lwith a natural held item.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyDeclineTrade` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:75): No rush.\pBring me GYARADOS when you want\nto see that evolution in motion.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyDefeat` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:51): Wow!\nYou're too much!\pAll right!\pYou can have the CASCADEBADGE to\nshow you beat me.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyGyaradosReminder` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:58): A puny fish can become a mighty,\nraging POKéMON.\pThat's the beauty of change.\pBring me that GYARADOS.\nThen I'll show you trade evolution\lwith a natural held item.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyIntro` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:1): Hey! You made it back!\pThanks for guiding me through\nROCK TUNNEL's currents.\pThe POWER PLANT is stable again,\nand I learned a lot watching how\lyou handled the flow.\pWater is beautiful because it\nchanges without losing itself.\pNow it's time for an official\nGYM challenge.\pMy policy is an all-out offensive\nwith WATER-type POKéMON!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyNotGyarados` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:80): That's not the mighty POKéMON\nthat grows from MAGIKARP.\pBring me GYARADOS and I'll show\nyou something interesting.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyOfferTrade` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:66): Ready to trade GYARADOS?\pSome POKéMON evolve when traded\nwhile holding something natural.\pMy POLIWHIRL is holding a\nKING'S ROCK.\pWill you trade GYARADOS\nfor my POLIWHIRL?$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyTradeComplete` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:86): There it is...\nPOLITOED!\pA POLIWHIRL traded with a\nKING'S ROCK can change into\lsomething completely new.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_MistyTradeDone` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:93): POLITOED is proof that evolution\ncan follow the flow of an item\land a trade.\pKeep watching how beautifully\nPOKéMON can change.$
- `CeruleanCity_Gym_Text_TakeThisWithYou` (data/maps/CeruleanCity_Gym/text.inc:33): You can have the CASCADEBADGE.\pAnd with it, take this board.\nIt will carry you across water.\pFace the water's edge and USE it\nfrom KEY ITEMS in your BAG.$
- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$

## CinnabarIsland_Gym — BLAINE (LEADER)

- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_BlaineDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:13): I have burned down to nothing!\nNot even ashes remain!\pYou have earned the VOLCANOBADGE.$
- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_BlaineIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:1): Hah!\pI am BLAINE, the red-hot LEADER\nof CINNABAR GYM!\pFire is more than destruction.\nProperly handled, it drives\linnovation and progress!\pMy fiery POKéMON are ready to\ntest your next breakthrough!\pHah!\nYou better have BURN HEAL!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_BlainePorygonMachineAvailable` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:43): The machine by my desk is ready.\pGo on!\nTurn research into a POKéMON!$
- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_BlainePostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:38): Fire is the engine of progress!\pTreat it with discipline, and it\ncan light the way to tomorrow.$
- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_BlaineUnlockPorygonMachine` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:28): But a BADGE is only proof of what\nyou have already survived.\pProgress asks what you can build\nnext!\pThe machine by my desk can compile\na POKéMON from research data.\pAnswer its sequence correctly, and\ngenerate PORYGON yourself!$
- `CinnabarIsland_Gym_Text_ExplainVolcanoBadge` (data/maps/CinnabarIsland_Gym/text.inc:23): Hah!\pThe VOLCANOBADGE proves you\nsurvived CINNABAR GYM's heat.$
- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$

## CinnabarVolcano_3F — BLAISE (KINDLER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_BlairDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:5): Burned out!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_BlairIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:1): Deep enough, even the rocks\nstart to glow.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_BlairPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:8): Fire-types love this pressure.\pDown here, even burned ground\nfeels alive again.\pLike the volcano keeps renewing\nits own energy.$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## CinnabarVolcano_3F — ELIAS (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_EliasDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:19): A historic defeat!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_EliasIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:15): These stones remember every\neruption. I study them all!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_EliasPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:22): Volcanic rock tells the story\nof how CINNABAR was formed.$

## CinnabarVolcano_3F — KARINA (EXPERT_F)

- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KarinaDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:31): You did not waver.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KarinaIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:26): A master stance should hold\neven in this heat.\pLet's see if yours does.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KarinaPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/text.inc:34): The volcano teaches the same\nlesson as the dojo.\pBreathe, root yourself, then\nstrike with purpose.$

## CinnabarVolcano_3F — HUGO (EXPERT_M)

- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_ExpertHugoDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:141): I let the heat hurry me!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_ExpertHugoIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:138): Steady breathing. Steady steps.\nKeep your focus in this heat.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_ExpertHugoPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:144): I stop when my breathing turns\nshallow. Experience includes\pknowing when to rest.$

## CinnabarVolcano_3F — FLINT (KINDLER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KindlerFlintDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:162): My flame went out!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KindlerFlintIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:159): Fire rewards a steady hand.\nCan you handle the heat?$
- `CinnabarVolcano_3F_Text_KindlerFlintPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_3F/scripts.inc:165): Control matters more than heat.\nThat goes for battles too.$

## CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F — MASON (SCOUT_M)

- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F_Text_MasonDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F/text.inc:5): Too hot for me!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F_Text_MasonIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F/text.inc:1): This heat is wild!\nLet's see if you can keep up!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F_Text_MasonPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_1F/text.inc:8): The deeper you go, the more the\nair feels like it's pushing back.$

## CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F — GARRETT (HIKER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F_Text_GarrettDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F/text.inc:5): That shook me loose!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F_Text_GarrettIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F/text.inc:1): A volcano's just a mountain\nwith a temper!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F_Text_GarrettPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_2F/text.inc:8): Loose stone, hot air, and hard\nclimbs. That's a real trail.$

## CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F — REN (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F_Text_RenDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F/text.inc:5): Your stance didn't waver!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F_Text_RenIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F/text.inc:1): Train where the air burns.\nThen battle without fear!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F_Text_RenPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_LeftCorridor_3F/text.inc:8): The heat strips away hesitation.\nThat's why I train here.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F — NELLA (SCOUT_F)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F_Text_LenaDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F/text.inc:5): I'm melting here!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F_Text_LenaIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F/text.inc:1): I came prepared for a hike,\nbut not for this heat!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F_Text_LenaPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_1F/text.inc:8): Bring plenty of supplies.\nThis place drains you fast.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F — RONAN (HIKER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F_Text_NolanDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F/text.inc:5): I lost my footing!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F_Text_NolanIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F/text.inc:1): Step carefully!\nThe ground shifts underfoot.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F_Text_NolanPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_2F/text.inc:8): If the cave rumbles, don't wait\naround to admire it.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F — KIRA (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KiraDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:5): Clean movement!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KiraIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:1): Hot ground, narrow path...\nPerfect footwork practice!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KiraPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:8): A battle here teaches balance\nbetter than any dojo floor.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F — COLE (KINDLER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_ColeDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:16): You smothered my spark!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_ColeIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:12): Can you handle a little fire\ninside a volcano?$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_ColePostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:19): The best flames don't flare up.\nThey keep burning steady.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F — KOGA (LEADER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_GymAideTypeAdvice` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:115): JANINE: Father uses POISON types.\pPSYCHIC and GROUND moves are\nstrong against POISON.\pBut GROUND moves cannot hit\nFLYING types. Watch for those!\pBring antidotes for poison, too.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_JanineArrives` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:41): JANINE: Then I made it in time.\pFather, you said togetherness\nwas the point of this trial.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaAfterBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:97): KOGA: Fwahahaha!\nYou have earned the SOULBADGE!\n{PAUSE_MUSIC}{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_OBTAIN_BADGE}{PAUSE 0xFE}{PAUSE 0x56}{RESUME_MUSIC}\pReturn to FUCHSIA GYM\nwhen you are ready.\pThere, the LAVIC STONE will become\ntools worthy of our clan.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaBattleIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:78): KOGA: Then show me the strength\nyou bring to your allies!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:82): Fwahahaha!\nA worthy victory!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaFountain` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:23): KOGA: Fwahahaha!\pYou made it this far.\nTogether, no less.\pThen the lesson has already\ntaken root.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaLiftStone` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:30): KOGA grips the LAVIC STONE\nwith both hands...\p...But it does not move.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaNeedStrength` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:35): KOGA: Hmph!\nEven quenched, it resists me.\pWe need more strength to lift\nthis stone from its resting place.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaOfficialChallenge` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:59): KOGA: Together, we freed the\nstone.\pA community does not weaken\nthe self.\pIt gives strength somewhere to\nstand.\pBack at the GYM, this will become\nthe heart of new ninja tools.\pBut before I return, I want to\nbattle you here.\pJANINE stands beside me as my\nGYM aide.\pThat makes this challenge\nofficial.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaPostJanine` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:86): KOGA: The LAVIC STONE waits.\pWith JANINE here, we may yet\nmove it.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaPostStone` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:91): KOGA: Together, even stubborn\nstone must yield.\pNow, let us make this trial\nofficial.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaReadyToBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:75): Are you ready to battle KOGA?$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KogaTryTogether` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:46): KOGA: Hah!\nYou are right, JANINE.\pThen we shall try together.$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_PullTogether` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:51): KOGA, JANINE, and {PLAYER}\npull with all their strength!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_StonePulledFree` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/text.inc:55): The LAVIC STONE breaks loose\nfrom the volcanic rock!$
- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$

## CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F — EMBER (KINDLER)

- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KindlerEmberDefeat` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/scripts.inc:260): You cooled us down!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KindlerEmberIntro` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/scripts.inc:257): We train where the embers glow.\nLet us light up this battle!$
- `CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F_Text_KindlerEmberPostBattle` (data/maps/CinnabarVolcano_RightCorridor_3F/scripts.inc:263): A small spark can grow strong.\nSo can a patient trainer.$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — PHIL (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_PhilDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:141): Whoa!\nYou've got it!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_PhilIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:135): Stop right there!\pWe train in Master KOGA's gym,\nwherever his work takes him.\pLet me test your technique!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_PhilPostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:145): You impressed me!\pUse your VS SEEKER here when\nyou want to train with us again.$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — EDGAR (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_EdgarDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:156): Awoo!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_EdgarIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:150): I also study the way of the ninja\nwith Master KOGA!\pNinja have a long history of using\nanimals!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_EdgarPostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:159): I still have much to learn.$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — KIRK (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KirkDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:116): I'm done for!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KirkIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:111): I was a magician once upon a time.\pBut I dreamt of becoming a ninja,\nso I joined this GYM.$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KirkPostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:119): Even though I've lost, I will keep\ntraining according to the teachings\lof KOGA, my ninja master.$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — SHAWN (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_ShawnDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:167): You're more skilled than I'd\nthought!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_ShawnIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:162): Master KOGA comes from a long line\nof ninjas.\pWhat did you descend from?$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_ShawnPostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:171): Where there is light, there is\nshadow!\pLight and shadow!\nWhich do you choose?$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — KAYDEN (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KaydenDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:101): What?\nExtraordinary!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KaydenIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:94): Strength isn't the key for POKéMON.\nDo you understand this?\pPOKéMON is about strategy!\pI'll show you how strategy can\nbeat brute strength.$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_KaydenPostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:105): So, you mix brawn with brains?\nGood strategy!\pThat's remarkable for a child\nTRAINER.$

## FuchsiaCity_Gym — NATE (NINJA)

- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_NateDefeat` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:128): You had me fooled!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_NateIntro` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:124): Let's see you beat my special\ntechniques!$
- `FuchsiaCity_Gym_Text_NatePostBattle` (data/maps/FuchsiaCity_Gym/text.inc:131): I like poison and sleep techniques,\nas they linger after battle!$

## FuchsiaForest — TOBIN (BUG_CATCHER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_TOBIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4236): We lost our grip!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_TOBIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4232): I found a bug clinging under a\nwet leaf. It didn't slide off\ponce! My team can hang on too!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_TOBIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4238): I check underneath leaves after\nrain. There's a whole forest you\pmiss by looking down.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_TOBIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4234): We've practised holding on! Try\nshaking our team this time!$

## FuchsiaForest — ROSA (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_ROSA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4365): You went outside my lines!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_ROSA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4357): I sketch leaves to remember\nthe paths. Let me sketch your\pbattle style!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_ROSA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4367): Two trees can look alike from\nfar away. Their leaves make\pbetter landmarks.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_ROSA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4361): I left a page for our\nrematch. Give me something to\pdraw!$

## FuchsiaForest — MARIS (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_MARIS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4204): You took my breath away!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_MARIS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4197): These leaves smell sharp when\nrubbed. My team has a sharp\pside too!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_MARIS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4206): I rub a fallen leaf between\nmy fingers. There is no need\pto strip a living branch.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_MARIS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4201): A fresh sprig for a fresh\nstart. Shall we battle again?$

## FuchsiaForest — QUILL (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_QUILL_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4282): That stopped my clicking!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_QUILL_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4278): Hear that clicking? I record\nwhich bugs answer each other.\pLet's see how your team\nresponds!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_QUILL_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4284): One call can set the whole patch\nbuzzing. I wait quietly to hear\pwho starts it.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_QUILL_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4280): I learned another call. Now I\nwant to hear your battle\pcommands!$

## FuchsiaForest — REED (POKÉMON_RANGER_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_REED_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4413): A dependable team!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_REED_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4409): I check the trees along the\npaths from FUCHSIA. Show me that\pyou and your team can travel\ntogether.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_REED_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4415): I look for fresh damage after\nstrong winds. A familiar path\pstill deserves a fresh look.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_REED_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4411): Your team looks well rested.\nReady for another patrol test?$

## FuchsiaForest — WREN (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_WREN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4397): An adventure for us both!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_WREN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4393): I brought my POKéMON out of the\ncity to explore. New smells can\pbe quite an adventure!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_WREN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4399): I let them choose which scent to\nfollow, then call them back.\pThey learn that exploring\ndoesn't mean being alone.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_WREN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4395): They remembered this path! Let's\nsee what else they remember!$

## FuchsiaForest — DENNY (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_DENNY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4292): You untied my whole plan!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_DENNY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4286): My knots hold firm. Let us\nsee if my strategy does too!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_DENNY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4294): A good knot must come undone\nwhen you need it to. I\ppractice before setting up\ncamp.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_DENNY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4289): I tightened the loose ends.\nTime for another test!$

## MtMoon_1F — ROBBY (HIKER)

- `MtMoon_1F_Text_RobbyDefeat` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:41): Even the cave echoes know I lost.$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_RobbyIntro` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:37): You need to go through this cave\nto get to CERULEAN CITY.$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_RobbyPostBattle` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:44): ZUBAT is tough!\nBut if you can catch one, you'll\lbe able to count on it.$

## MtMoon_1F — JOVAN (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `MtMoon_1F_Text_JovanDefeat` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:18): My POKéMON won't do!$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_JovanIntro` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:14): What!\nDon't sneak up on me!$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_JovanPostBattle` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:21): I have to find stronger POKéMON.\nWhere might they be?$

## MtMoon_1F — KENT (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `MtMoon_1F_Text_KentDefeat` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:29): You found my weak spot faster than\nI found a fossil.$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_KentIntro` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:25): I heard rare fossils are in here.\nWhat about you?$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_KentPostBattle` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:33): I saw some glittering stones,\nbut I couldn't tell what they were.$

## MtMoon_1F — MARCOS (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `MtMoon_1F_Text_MarcosDefeat` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:6): Hah!\nYou hit harder than loose rock!$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_MarcosIntro` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:1): WHOA!\nYou shocked me!\l…Oh, you're just a kid!$
- `MtMoon_1F_Text_MarcosPostBattle` (data/maps/MtMoon_1F/text.inc:10): Kids like you shouldn't be\nwandering around here in the dark.$

## PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab — TERRY (RIVAL_EARLY)

- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakCallPlayerAndRivalOver` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:48): OAK: Both of you, over here.\pThere’s something important\nyou need to hear before\lyou begin.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakCatchMonsForDataTakeThese` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:161): OAK: Seeing POKéMON is useful,\nbut catching them teaches you more.\pTravel with them.\nBattle with them.\pThat is how real understanding\nbegins.\pTake these POKé BALLS with you.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakExplainCatching` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:173): OAK: Pay attention to how the\nGYM LEADERS treat their POKéMON.\pThere is more to learn than\nstrength alone.\pEven familiar routes can shelter\nstrong, fully grown POKéMON.\pYou can flee ordinary wild\nbattles and return when ready.\pI also study rare POKéMON whose\ninstincts exceed ordinary limits.\pI call them APEX POKéMON.\pListen to local accounts and\ncompare three in your LOGBOOK.\pTogether, they may reveal where\nan APEX POKéMON can be found.\pBe ready before you challenge one.\nIf you flee from its battle,\lit will leave for good.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakHaveRequestForYouTwo` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:140): OAK: I have a request for you two.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakPokedexOnDesk` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:143): On the desk here is my\ninvention, the POKéDEX!\pIt records the POKéMON you see\nand catch.\pIt’ll help us understand how\nthey live across KANTO.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_OakTakeTheseWithYou` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:151): OAK: {PLAYER} and {RIVAL}.\nTake these with you.\pRecord what you find and\ncome back wiser.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_ReceivedPokedexFromOak` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:157): {PLAYER} received the POKéDEX\nfrom PROF. OAK.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_RivalDefeat` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:35): WHAT?\nUnbelievable!\lI picked the wrong POKéMON!$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_RivalGoToughenMyMon` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:45): OAK: That’s enough.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_RivalLeaveItToMeGramps` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:54): {RIVAL}: Heh. Fine by me.\pI’ll be finished learning\neverything worth knowing before\lyou’ve even caught up.\pTry not to fall too far behind.$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_RivalLetsCheckOutMons` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:30): {RIVAL}: Wait, {PLAYER}!\nLet's check out our POKéMON!\pCome on, I'll take you on!$
- `PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab_Text_RivalTellSisNotToGiveYouMap` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:61): OAK: We’ll see.\pPower and understanding\nrarely arrive at the same time.\pOff you go, then.\pYour expedition begins now.$
- `Text_RivalVictory` (data/maps/PalletTown_ProfessorOaksLab/text.inc:41): {RIVAL}: Yeah!\nAm I great or what?$

## PewterCity_Gym — BROCK (LEADER)

- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockDeclineTrade` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:73): No rush.\pWhen you're ready, bring\nthe ancient POKéMON back to me.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockDefeat` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:26): I took you for granted...\nand I lost.\pYou understood more than\njust the battle.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockIntro` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:1): So, we meet again.\pBack in MT. MOON, I didn't\nintroduce myself properly.\pI'm BROCK.\nPEWTER CITY's GYM LEADER.\pYou showed a keen eye for\nfossils out there.\pBut finding fragments is only\npart of the story.\pUnderstanding how they connect...\nthat's where true strength lies.\pThere's something I want to show\nyou about evolution.\pBut first-you'll have to\ndefeat me in battle.\pLet's see what you've learned!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockNotEnoughPokemon` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:19): So, we meet again.\pWait, you don't have enough POKéMON\nto battle me.\pCome back when you have\nmore POKéMON in your party.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockNotRelicanth` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:98): That's not the POKéMON\nI was hoping to study.\pLook for one that has lived\nunchanged since ancient times.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockOfferTrade` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:66): Now watch closely.\pSome evolution happens through\ninteraction between TRAINERS.\pWill you trade your ancient\nPOKéMON for my GRAVELER?$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockPostBattle` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:91): Deep below ROCK TUNNEL,\nancient waters still shelter\lPOKéMON from another age.\pFind one and bring it to me.\nThen I'll show you trade evolution.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockTradeComplete` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:78): There it is...\nGOLEM.\pThat's trade evolution.\pA change brought about by\nexchange between TRAINERS.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_BrockTradeDone` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:85): GOLEM is proof that evolution\ncan be shaped by connection.\pRemember that as you continue\nOAK's research.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_ExplainBoulderBadge` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:43): The BOULDERBADGE grants access\nto the CINNABAR LAB.\pThat's where fossils are studied\nand restored.\pBut fossils aren't the only way\nto study the past.\pSome POKéMON never vanished.\nThey still live... unchanged.\pDeep below ROCK TUNNEL,\nancient waters shelter POKéMON\lolder than anything restored.\pFind one. Bring it to me.\pThen I'll show you another kind\nof evolution.$
- `PewterCity_Gym_Text_TakeThisWithYou` (data/maps/PewterCity_Gym/text.inc:32): Wait.\pTake this-\nthe BOULDERBADGE.\pAnd with it, the key to\nthe CINNABAR LAB.$

## PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom — BLAINE, BROCK, ERIKA, GIOVANNI, KOGA, LT. SURGE, MISTY, SABRINA (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_AgathaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:34): AGATHA: OAK sent you out to\nlearn, did he? Let's see whether\pyou can surprise an old hand!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_AreYouReady` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:1): Step into the battle area to face\nyour next League opponent.\pReady for a DOUBLE BATTLE?$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BlaineDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:86): A breakthrough! You burned past\nmy expectations!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BlaineIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:83): BLAINE: Hah! A familiar face\nwith new ideas! Let's put those\pdiscoveries through a proper\nheat test!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrockDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:51): That foundation held firm.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrockIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:48): BROCK: We began with fragments\nand questions. Now show me the\pfoundation you've built since\nMT. MOON.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrunoIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:27): BRUNO: Every step here took\neffort! Put that effort behind\pyour commands. Hoo hah!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_ChampionAwaits` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:10): All four victories are recorded!\pThe north door is now open.\nThe CHAMPION awaits you.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_ErikaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:72): Your care has borne wonderful\nfruit.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_ErikaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:69): ERIKA: How lovely to meet here.\nI can see how your team has\pgrown. May we share another\nlesson?$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_GiovanniDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:100): We have more to learn. A worthy\nresult.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_GiovanniIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:97): GIOVANNI: You taught me what\ncontrol could not achieve.\pToday, my team and I will show\nyou what we have learned.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_KogaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:79): Together, you overcame my\ntechnique!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_KogaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:76): KOGA: Fwahahaha! No volcanic\ntrial today. Only your allies\pand mine. Show me the strength\nof that bond!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LanceIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:41): LANCE: A dragon's strength needs\na trainer who can guide it. Show\pme how you guide your own team.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LoreleiIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:20): LORELEI: The arena is warm, but\nmy team is quite at home. Keep\pyour composure when the cold\ncloses in.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LtSurgeDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:65): Now that's power you can rely\non!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LtSurgeIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:62): LT. SURGE: Look who made the\nLEAGUE! Training's over, kid.\pShow me that voltage under\npressure!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_MistyDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:58): You found a way through every\nwave!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_MistyIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:55): MISTY: We've come a long way\nfrom those currents! My team\pwon't hold back. Let's make some\nwaves!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_PostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:15): Victory {STR_VAR_1} of 4 recorded.\pReturn to the battle area when\nyou are ready for the next match.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_SabrinaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:93): Your will and theirs moved\ntogether.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_SabrinaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:90): SABRINA: The arena is full of\nexpectation. Let it pass. Listen\pto your partners, then answer\nme.$

## PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom — AGATHA, BRUNO, LANCE, LORELEI (ELITE_FOUR)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_AgathaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:37): Hah! There is life in this old\nchallenge yet!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_AgathaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:34): AGATHA: OAK sent you out to\nlearn, did he? Let's see whether\pyou can surprise an old hand!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_AreYouReady` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:1): Step into the battle area to face\nyour next League opponent.\pReady for a DOUBLE BATTLE?$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BlaineIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:83): BLAINE: Hah! A familiar face\nwith new ideas! Let's put those\pdiscoveries through a proper\nheat test!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrockIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:48): BROCK: We began with fragments\nand questions. Now show me the\pfoundation you've built since\nMT. MOON.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrunoDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:30): Your training speaks for itself!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_BrunoIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:27): BRUNO: Every step here took\neffort! Put that effort behind\pyour commands. Hoo hah!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_ChampionAwaits` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:10): All four victories are recorded!\pThe north door is now open.\nThe CHAMPION awaits you.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_ErikaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:69): ERIKA: How lovely to meet here.\nI can see how your team has\pgrown. May we share another\nlesson?$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_GiovanniIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:97): GIOVANNI: You taught me what\ncontrol could not achieve.\pToday, my team and I will show\nyou what we have learned.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_KogaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:76): KOGA: Fwahahaha! No volcanic\ntrial today. Only your allies\pand mine. Show me the strength\nof that bond!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LanceDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:44): Your team answered with\nremarkable strength.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LanceIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:41): LANCE: A dragon's strength needs\na trainer who can guide it. Show\pme how you guide your own team.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LoreleiDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:23): You stayed composed through it\nall.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LoreleiIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:20): LORELEI: The arena is warm, but\nmy team is quite at home. Keep\pyour composure when the cold\ncloses in.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_LtSurgeIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:62): LT. SURGE: Look who made the\nLEAGUE! Training's over, kid.\pShow me that voltage under\npressure!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_MistyIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:55): MISTY: We've come a long way\nfrom those currents! My team\pwon't hold back. Let's make some\nwaves!$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_PostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:15): Victory {STR_VAR_1} of 4 recorded.\pReturn to the battle area when\nyou are ready for the next match.$
- `PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom_Text_SabrinaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_BrunosRoom/text.inc:90): SABRINA: The arena is full of\nexpectation. Let it pass. Listen\pto your partners, then answer\nme.$

## PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom — TERRY (CHAMPION)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_Defeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:37): NO!\nThat can't be!\lYou beat me at my best!\pAfter all that work to become\nthe LEAGUE CHAMP?\pMy reign is over already?\nIt's not fair!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_Intro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:1): {RIVAL}: Hey, {PLAYER}!\pI was looking forward to seeing\nyou, {PLAYER}.\pMy rival should be strong to keep\nme sharp.\pWhile working on my POKéDEX, I\nlooked all over for POKéMON.\pNot only that, I assembled teams\nthat would beat any POKéMON type.\pAnd now…\pI'm the POKéMON LEAGUE CHAMPION!\p{PLAYER}!\nDo you know what that means?\pI'll tell you.\pI am the most powerful TRAINER in\nthe world!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_NotReady` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:46): The INDIGO LEAGUE CHAMPION\nis not receiving challengers yet.$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_OakCongratulations` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:73): OAK: So, you've won!\nSincerely, congratulations!\pYou're the new POKéMON LEAGUE\nCHAMPION!\pYou've grown up so much since you\nfirst left with {STR_VAR_1} to work\lon the POKéDEX.\p{PLAYER}, you have come of age!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_OakImDisappointedRival` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:83): OAK: {RIVAL}…\nI'm disappointed in you.\pI came when I heard you'd beaten\nthe ELITE FOUR.\pBut, when I got here, you had\nalready lost!\p{RIVAL}, do you understand why\nyou lost?\pYou have forgotten to treat your\nPOKéMON with love and trust.\pWithout them, you will never\nbecome a CHAMP again!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_OakPlayer` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:70): OAK: {PLAYER}!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_OakPlayerComeWithMe` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:97): OAK: {PLAYER}.\pYou understand that your victory\nwas not just your own doing.\pThe bond you share with your\nPOKéMON is marvelous.\p{PLAYER}!\nCome with me!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_PostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:61): Why?\nWhy did I lose?\pI never made any mistakes raising\nmy POKéMON…\pDarn it! You're the new POKéMON\nLEAGUE CHAMPION!\pAlthough I don't like to admit it…$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_RematchIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:19): {RIVAL}: Hey, {PLAYER}!\pYou came back, {PLAYER}!\nHahah, that is so great!\pMy rival should be strong to keep\nme sharp.\pWhile working on my POKéDEX, I\nlooked all over for POKéMON.\pNot only that, I assembled teams\nthat would beat any POKéMON type.\pAnd now…\pI'm the POKéMON LEAGUE CHAMPION!\p{PLAYER}!\nDo you know what that means?\pI'll tell you.\pI am the most powerful TRAINER in\nthe world!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_StoriesDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:122): You got us...\nWe gave you everything we had!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_StoriesIntro` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:106): {RIVAL}: There you are, {PLAYER}.\nWe've covered a lot of ground.\pFrom that MANSION to SILPH...\nGIOVANNI wanted control.\pAnd those APEX investigations?\nWild POKéMON finding ways to grow.\pThe GYM LEADERS showed us what\ngrowing alongside a team can mean.\pI still want to be the best.\nYou know that hasn't changed!\pBut my team got me here.\nNow we're taking you on together!\pCome on, {PLAYER}!\nShow us everything you've learned!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_StoriesOakRival` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:138): OAK: {RIVAL}, look at your team.\nThey gave you their best.\pYou listened to them, and you\ntrusted them. That matters.\pYou both brought back discoveries\nand grew alongside your POKéMON.\pBe proud of how far you've come.\nThere is still so much to learn!$
- `PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_StoriesPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:126): {RIVAL}: I thought we had you.\nI really did.\pBut I saw how your team responded.\nYou knew what they could do.\pWe've both learned a lot since\nthat first battle at the lab.\pToday, you were better.\nYou've earned this, {PLAYER}.\pDon't get too comfortable, though.\nMy team and I aren't done growing!$

## PokemonMansion_1F — TED (SCIENTIST)

- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_TedDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_1F/text.inc:5): Ouch!$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_TedIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_1F/text.inc:1): Who are you?\nThere shouldn't be anyone here.$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_TedPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_1F/text.inc:8): A key? I don't know what you're\ntalking about.$

## PokemonMansion_1F — PIKE (BURGLAR)

- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_PIKE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4599): Nothing worth stealing here!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_PIKE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4593): Old labs mean old valuables.\pDon't get in my way!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_PIKE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4601): The best stuff is always behind\nlocked doors.\pThe worst stuff too.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_PIKE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4596): Same halls. Same dust.\pMaybe a better fight this time!$

## PokemonMansion_1F — NILES (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_NILES_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4634): Readings lost!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_NILES_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4628): The equipment is ruined,\nbut the readings linger!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_NILES_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4636): Some instruments failed all at\nonce, as if something woke up\nangry.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_NILES_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4631): Residual energy remains.\pLet's provoke it again!$

## PokemonMansion_2F — ARNIE (BURGLAR)

- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_ArnieDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_2F/text.inc:15): Oh, no!\nMy bag of loot!$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_ArnieIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_2F/text.inc:11): I can't get out!\nThis old place is one big puzzle.$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_ArniePostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_2F/text.inc:19): The switches here open and close\nalternating sets of doors.$

## PokemonMansion_2F — MIREN (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_MIRA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4622): The results oppose me!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_MIRA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4616): This lab held impossible research.\pStand still. I need a comparison!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_MIRA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4624): Power without ethics is still\nscience.\pThat's what makes it dangerous.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_MIRA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4619): Your POKEMON's responses\nmay clarify my notes!$

## PokemonMansion_2F — FLINT (BURGLAR)

- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_FLINT_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4576): Burned again!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_FLINT_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4569): Ashes, old notes, broken locks...\pThis mansion is my kind of\nmuseum!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_FLINT_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4578): The fire didn't take everything.\pSome things survived because\nthey were buried deep.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_FLINT_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4573): Still poking through the ashes?\pLet's see what turns up!$

## PokemonMansion_3F — SIMON (BURGLAR)

- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_SimonDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:14): Ayah!$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_SimonIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:11): This place is, like, huge!$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_SimonPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:17): I wonder where my partner went.$

## PokemonMansion_3F — BRAYDON (SCIENTIST)

- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_BraydonDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:23): Whew!\nOverwhelming!$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_BraydonIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:20): My mentor once lived here.$
- `PokemonMansion_1F_Text_BraydonPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:27): So, you're stuck?\nTry jumping off over there!$

## PokemonMansion_3F — KNOX (BURGLAR)

- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_KNOX_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4588): That scared me straight!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_KNOX_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4582): You heard something too?\pGood. I hate searching alone!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_KNOX_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4590): The walls creak like something\nis still moving inside them.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_KNOX_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4585): If that noise comes back,\nI'm blaming you!$

## PokemonMansion_3F — LENOX (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_LENOX_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4611): My theory collapsed!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_LENOX_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4605): These journals are damaged,\nbut the formulas remain!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_LENOX_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4613): Whatever was created here, it\nwas planned with awful precision.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_LENOX_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4608): I restored another page.\pCare to test the theory?$

## PokemonMansion_3F — TERRY (RIVAL_LATE)

- `PokemonMansion_3F_Text_RivalBattleIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:51): {RIVAL}: Then prove it.\nTry keeping up!$
- `PokemonMansion_3F_Text_RivalDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:55): Tch.\nFine, you're ready enough.$
- `PokemonMansion_3F_Text_RivalNotReady` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:45): {RIVAL}: Hah!\nThought so.\pCome back when you stop looking\nlike you need a permission slip.$
- `PokemonMansion_3F_Text_RivalPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:59): {RIVAL}: Whatever's below,\ndon't slow me down.\pI'm checking the rest of this\nplace.\pTry not to fall apart.$
- `PokemonMansion_3F_Text_RivalReady` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_3F/text.inc:36): {RIVAL}: Still poking around?\pGood. Maybe you're not completely\nuseless after all.\pI don't know what's waiting below,\nbut it isn't nothing.\pSo? Ready to battle me and prove\nyou can handle it?$

## PokemonMansion_B1F — LEWIS (BURGLAR)

- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_LewisDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:5): Awooh!$
- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_LewisIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:1): Uh-oh…\nWhere am I now?$
- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_LewisPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:8): You can find stuff lying around.\nThe residents must've left them.$

## PokemonMansion_B1F — IVAN (SCIENTIST)

- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_IvanDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:16): What was that for?$
- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_IvanIntro` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:12): This place is ideal for a lab.\nLook at all this space.$
- `PokemonMansion_B1F_Text_IvanPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonMansion_B1F/text.inc:19): I like it here.\nIt's conducive to my studies.$

## PokemonMansion_B1F — EGAN (BURGLAR)

- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_EGAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4563): Hands off my haul!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_EGAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4556): This place is abandoned?\pThen nobody will miss what I\npocket!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_EGAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4565): Every cracked floorboard here\nhides a secret.\pOr a trap. Usually both.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_BURGLAR_EGAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4560): Back for more treasure?\pToo bad. I saw it first!$

## PokemonMansion_B1F — ORIN (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_ORIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4647): No conclusion!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_ORIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4640): I came for answers.\pThis mansion only asks louder\nquestions!$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_ORIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4649): There is a difference between\ndiscovery and control.\pThis place forgot that.$
- `EventScript_Mansion_SCIENTIST_ORIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4644): The questions remain.\pMaybe battle will sharpen them!$

## PokemonTower_2F — PATRICIA (CHANNELER)

- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_PatriciaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:17): Whew!\nThe spirit left!$
- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_PatriciaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:13): Be gone!\nMalevolent spirit!$
- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_PatriciaPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:21): The others above…\nThey must have been possessed.$

## PokemonTower_2F — HOPE (CHANNELER)

- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_HopeDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:5): Hwa!\nI'm saved!$
- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_HopeIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:1): Urrg… Awaa…\nHuhu… Graa…$
- `PokemonTower_2F_Text_HopePostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_2F/text.inc:9): The ghosts can be identified\nclearly now.$

## PokemonTower_3F — ANGELICA (CHANNELER)

- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_AngelicaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:76): Groan!\nThe darkness is lifting.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_AngelicaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:72): Give…me…\nyour light…$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_AngelicaPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:80): I feel anemic and weak…$

## PokemonTower_3F — JENNIFER (CHANNELER)

- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_JenniferDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:99): Keee!\nThe astral pull is breaking.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_JenniferIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:95): Ke…ke…ke…\nke…ke…ke!$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_JenniferPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:103): What's going on here?$

## PokemonTower_3F — EMILIA (CHANNELER)

- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_EmiliaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:87): Something left me!\nWas that the spirit?$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_EmiliaIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:83): Urff…\nKwaah!$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_EmiliaPostBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:91): My hair didn't fall out!\nIt was a malevolent spirit!$

## PokemonTower_3F — SABRINA (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_GymAideTypeAdvice` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:106): GYM AIDE: SABRINA uses PSYCHIC\ntypes. Try BUG, GHOST or DARK\lmoves to hit their weakness.\pDARK types are immune to\nPSYCHIC moves, but watch for\lher other attacks!$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_GymGuideArrives` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:34): SABRINA!\nI felt the tower settle.\pIf a battle begins here, I will\nwitness it as your GYM aide.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaAfterBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:65): The MARSHBADGE is yours.\pYou met the tower's pressure and\nanswered it with peaceful control.\pI will return to SAFFRON GYM.\nCome there when you are ready.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaBattleIntro` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:57): In this tower, thought and memory\npress close.\pShow me the shape of your will.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaChoosePokemon` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:14): Very well.\nChoose the POKéMON to focus.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaComeBackWithPokemon` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:18): GASTLY in this tower can learn\nDREAM EATER.\pReturn with one, or another\nPOKéMON that can learn it.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaDeclineBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:52): Then steady yourself.\pWhen your mind is ready, speak\nto me again.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaDefeat` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:62): Your focus did not waver.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaReadyToBattle` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:45): Peace is not proven by silence\nalone.\pLet us see how your focus holds\nin battle.\pAre you ready to challenge me?$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaTaughtDreamEater` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:24): That is DREAM EATER.\pIt draws strength from a sleeping\nfoe through psychic focus.\pYou remained peaceful inside\nthe tower's astral pull.\pThat emotional control is\nenough for an\lofficial challenge here.$
- `PokemonTower_3F_Text_SabrinaTeachDreamEater` (data/maps/PokemonTower_3F/text.inc:1): This circle gathers more than\nmemory.\pIt gathers feeling, fear, grief,\nand the will to remain.\pI came here because the tower's\nastral energy called to me.\pWhile listening to it, I refined\na PSYCHIC-type move.\pWould you like me to teach it?\pYou will need a POKéMON that can\nlearn DREAM EATER.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_ExplainMarshFinder` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:44): The MARSH FINDER senses hidden\nobjects nearby.\pA calm mind can notice what the\neyes alone overlook.$

## PowerPlant — BAILY (ENGINEER)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BAILY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4470): You grounded me!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BAILY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4463): I helped restart this place.\pThat charge still runs through\nmy team!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BAILY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4472): Even when the generators quiet\ndown, the air still crackles.\pIt feels like the power is coming\nfrom the sky itself.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BAILY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4467): The turbines are steady.\pLet's see if you can keep up!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## PowerPlant — BRAXTON (ENGINEER)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BRAXTON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4495): Shorted out!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BRAXTON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4489): Loose wires. Live floors.\pOne careless step and zap!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BRAXTON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4497): I saw lightning gather here\nwithout a cloud overhead.\pNo battery can hold power like\nthat. It just keeps coming.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BRAXTON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4492): Careful where you stand.\pThis floor still bites!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## PowerPlant — BERNIE (ENGINEER)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BERNIE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4483): The spark went out!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BERNIE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4477): Generators hate silence.\pI'll make this room crackle!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BERNIE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4485): Electricity has a temper.\pTreat it rough, and it answers\nback.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_ENGINEER_BERNIE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4480): Hear the crackle again?\pThat's my cue!$

## PowerPlant — CLEM (ELECTRICIAN)

- `EventScript_PowerPlant_ELECTRICIAN_CLEM_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4685): The circuit broke!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_ELECTRICIAN_CLEM_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4677): Hear that hum?\pThe whole plant is charging\nup again!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_ELECTRICIAN_CLEM_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4687): A power plant is never\nreally quiet.\pEvery wire tells you what\nit's carrying.$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_ELECTRICIAN_CLEM_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4681): Back for another surge?\pI've tuned my rhythm to\nthe generators!$

## PowerPlant — JIRO (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_JIRO_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4659): My data scattered!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_JIRO_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4653): These magnets are acting strange.\pYour POKEMON might be the reason!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_JIRO_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4661): Power draws POKEMON in.\pThe stronger the current, the\nstranger the readings.$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_JIRO_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4656): The readings changed again.\pI need another battle sample!$

## PowerPlant — SOREN (SCIENTIST)

- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_SOREN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4671): Overexposed!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_SOREN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4665): Don't touch anything glowing.\pUnless you're ready to battle!$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_SOREN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4673): Some lights in here are warnings.\pSome are invitations. Scientists\nargue which is which.$
- `EventScript_PowerPlant_SCIENTIST_SOREN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4668): The glow is stable now.\pLet's test your team against it!$

## PowerPlant — LUCA (ELECTRICIAN)

- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianLucaDefeat` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:99): You found the live fault!$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianLucaIntro` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:93): I'm tracing a live fault through\nthis whole place.\pKeep clear unless you can handle\na few sparks!$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianLucaPostBattle` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:102): The current's unstable, but the\nwiring tells you where it wants\nto go.$

## PowerPlant — OTIS (ELECTRICIAN)

- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianOtisDefeat` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:130): My circuit is broken!$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianOtisIntro` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:127): A good circuit needs balance.\nSo does a good POKéMON team!$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianOtisPostBattle` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:133): One loose connection is enough\nto bring the whole system down.$

## PowerPlant — WADE (ELECTRICIAN)

- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianWadeDefeat` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:151): That was quite a shock!$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianWadeIntro` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:148): Careful around live wires!\nMy team carries quite a charge.$
- `PowerPlant_Text_ElectricianWadePostBattle` (data/maps/PowerPlant/scripts.inc:154): We keep this place working.\nMy POKéMON help with that too.$

## RockTunnel_1F — LUCAS (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LucasDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:32): I give!\nYour footing's steadier than mine.$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LucasIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:28): Outsiders like you need to show me\nsome respect!$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LucasPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:36): You're talented enough to hike!$

## RockTunnel_1F — OLIVER (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_OliverDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:19): Ease up!\nWhat am I doing?\lWhich way is out?$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_OliverIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:15): Hmm.\nMaybe I'm lost in here…$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_OliverPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:24): That sleeping POKéMON on ROUTE 12\nforced me to take this detour.$

## RockTunnel_1F — LENNY (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LennyDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:4): Doh!\nYou handled the tunnel better than\nI did!$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LennyIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:1): This tunnel goes a long way, kid!$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_LennyPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:9): Watch for ONIX.\nThey appear only occasionally.\pHigh-level ones can put the\nsqueeze on you!$

## RockTunnel_1F — ASHTON (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_AshtonDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:43): Game over!\nThe tunnel swallowed my strategy.$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_AshtonIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:39): POKéMON fight!\nReady, go!$
- `RockTunnel_1F_Text_AshtonPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_1F/text.inc:47): Oh well, I'll get a ZUBAT as I go!$

## RockTunnel_B1F — COOPER (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_CooperDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:30): Shoot!\nI am so jealous!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_CooperIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:26): You have a POKéDEX?\nI want one, too.$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_CooperPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:34): When you finish your POKéDEX,\ncan I have it?$

## RockTunnel_B1F — DUDLEY (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_DudleyDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:18): Oops!\nOutmuscled in my own tunnel!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_DudleyIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:14): Hahaha!\nCan you beat my power?$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_DudleyPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:22): I go for power because I hate\nthinking.$

## RockTunnel_B1F — ALLEN (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_AllenDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:51): I give!\nYou're a better technician!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_AllenIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:47): My POKéMON techniques will leave\nyou crying!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_AllenPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:55): In mountains, you'll often find\nROCK-type POKéMON.$

## RockTunnel_B1F — ERIC (HIKER)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_EricDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:75): You fired back harder than the\nechoes in here.$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_EricIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:72): Hit me with your best shot!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_EricPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:79): I'll raise my POKéMON to beat\nyours, kid.$

## RockTunnel_B1F — STEVIE (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_SteveDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:41): Well, that's that.$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_SteveIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:38): I practise forms by the cave\nechoes. One clean strike should\pmake one clear sound!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_StevePostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:44): If my feet scrape the stone, I\nstart again. Quiet steps leave\pmore strength for the strike.$

## RockTunnel_B1F — WINSTON (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_WinstonDefeat` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:86): Whew…\nI'm exhausted…$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_WinstonIntro` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:83): I sketch the cracks in these\nwalls. They reveal how the\ptunnel formed. Let's compare our\nobservations!$
- `RockTunnel_B1F_Text_WinstonPostBattle` (data/maps/RockTunnel_B1F/text.inc:90): A sketch makes me notice what I\nwould walk past. Battle reveals\pgaps in my thinking too.$

## RocketLeague_Arena — ATTRYCE, JET, KARDINAL, MAKO (ROCKET_ACE_M)

- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArcherIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:30): ARCHER: LANCE relies on dragons.\nI have studied their weakness.\pMy FAIRY team is ready.\nAre you?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArcherPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:67): ARCHER: The next opponent will\nnot be so easily unsettled.\pProceed.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_AreYouReady` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:6): Are you ready?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArianaIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:36): ARIANA: AGATHA has her ghosts.\nI have the darkness they fear.\pCare to step into it?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArianaPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:72): ARIANA: Hmph.\pKeep that edge sharp.\nYou'll need it.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ChampionAwaits` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:20): The ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION\nawaits you in the next room.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_CoinPouchFull` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:137): You can't hold more COINS.\pYou couldn't receive the prize\nCOINS.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_GiovanniAway` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:13): The door is locked.\pGIOVANNI is away on\nimportant business.\pThe ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION\nis not receiving challengers.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_JetDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:187): You stopped us in our tracks!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_JetIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:184): JET: My team keeps its feet on\nthe ground. One clean opening,\pand we hit like a landslide!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_JetPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:111): JET: Fast enough to catch me,\nhuh?\pThen don't slow down now.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_KnellDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:166): You weathered every round!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_KnellIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:163): ATTRYCE: Hear the bell? That's\nour cue. My WATER team keeps\prolling until the final round!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_KnellPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:97): ATTRYCE: You heard the bell\nand kept standing.\pRespect.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_MakoDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:173): You kept your head in deep\nwater.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_MakoIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:170): MAKO: Plenty of challengers look\ncalm on the surface. Let's see\pwhat happens under pressure.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_MakoPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:102): MAKO: Not bad under pressure.\pThe deep end is still ahead.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_NovaIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:156): MOJAY: Empty space? Look closer.\nMy GHOST team is already making\pitself at home under these\nlights!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_NovaPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:92): MOJAY: That hit harder than\nI expected.\pNo shame in recalibrating.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_PetrelIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:24): PETREL: BRUNO trusts his fists.\nI prefer getting inside heads!\pLet me show you what PSYCHIC\nPOKéMON can do.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_PetrelPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:62): PETREL: Go on, then.\pI want to see how long that\nnerve of yours lasts.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ProtonIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:41): PROTON: LORELEI has her ice.\nI have STEEL and a plan to break it!\pLet's see you stop us.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ProtonPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:77): PROTON: Fine, you win.\pDon't expect the next fight to\nbe polite.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_RazorDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:145): You chose a line I hadn't seen.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_RazorIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:142): KARDINAL: You watch the attack.\nI watch the choice before it.\pTry keeping a secret from my\nPSYCHIC team!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_RazorPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:82): KARDINAL: You cut clean through\nmy plan.\pLet's see you do it again.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:121): {PLAYER} received 1,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierOneCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:125): {PLAYER} received 1,500 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierThreeCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:133): {PLAYER} received 3,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierTwoCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:129): {PLAYER} received 2,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_SableIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:177): SABLE: You came looking for\nsomething exotic? My\pordinary-looking team has a few\nsurprises.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_SablePostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:106): SABLE: Shadows favor the\npatient.\pMaybe they favor you too.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:149): SHION: Still judging strength by\nappearances? My FAIRY team\penjoys correcting that mistake.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:87): SHION: Data point recorded.\pYour odds just became more\ninteresting.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:191): VIPER: A little pressure, a\nlittle poison. I prefer a plan\pthat keeps working after the\nfirst hit.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:116): VIPER: You avoided the bite.\pNext time, the venom goes\ndeeper.$

- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_SablePostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:106): SABLE: Shadows favor the\npatient.\pMaybe they favor you too.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:152): An unexpected outcome. I'll\nrevise my prediction.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:149): SHION: Still judging strength by\nappearances? My FAIRY team\penjoys correcting that mistake.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:87): SHION: Data point recorded.\pYour odds just became more\ninteresting.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:194): You cut off my plan before it\nspread!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:191): VIPER: A little pressure, a\nlittle poison. I prefer a plan\pthat keeps working after the\nfirst hit.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:116): VIPER: You avoided the bite.\pNext time, the venom goes\ndeeper.$

## RocketLeague_Arena — ARCHER, ARIANA, PETREL, PROTON (ROCKET_ADMIN)

- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArcherDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:50): So discipline alone was not\nenough.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArcherIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:30): ARCHER: LANCE relies on dragons.\nI have studied their weakness.\pMy FAIRY team is ready.\nAre you?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArcherPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:67): ARCHER: The next opponent will\nnot be so easily unsettled.\pProceed.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_AreYouReady` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:6): Are you ready?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArianaDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:54): Hmph.\nYou are more polished than you look.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArianaIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:36): ARIANA: AGATHA has her ghosts.\nI have the darkness they fear.\pCare to step into it?$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ArianaPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:72): ARIANA: Hmph.\pKeep that edge sharp.\nYou'll need it.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ChampionAwaits` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:20): The ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION\nawaits you in the next room.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_CoinPouchFull` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:137): You can't hold more COINS.\pYou couldn't receive the prize\nCOINS.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_GiovanniAway` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:13): The door is locked.\pGIOVANNI is away on\nimportant business.\pThe ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION\nis not receiving challengers.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_JetIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:184): JET: My team keeps its feet on\nthe ground. One clean opening,\pand we hit like a landslide!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_JetPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:111): JET: Fast enough to catch me,\nhuh?\pThen don't slow down now.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_KnellIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:163): ATTRYCE: Hear the bell? That's\nour cue. My WATER team keeps\prolling until the final round!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_KnellPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:97): ATTRYCE: You heard the bell\nand kept standing.\pRespect.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_MakoIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:170): MAKO: Plenty of challengers look\ncalm on the surface. Let's see\pwhat happens under pressure.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_MakoPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:102): MAKO: Not bad under pressure.\pThe deep end is still ahead.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_NovaIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:156): MOJAY: Empty space? Look closer.\nMy GHOST team is already making\pitself at home under these\nlights!$
