- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_NovaPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:92): MOJAY: That hit harder than\nI expected.\pNo shame in recalibrating.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_PetrelDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:46): Heh... You saw right through\nmy mind games!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_PetrelIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:24): PETREL: BRUNO trusts his fists.\nI prefer getting inside heads!\pLet me show you what PSYCHIC\nPOKéMON can do.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_PetrelPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:62): PETREL: Go on, then.\pI want to see how long that\nnerve of yours lasts.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ProtonDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:58): Tch!\nYou made that take too long.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ProtonIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:41): PROTON: LORELEI has her ice.\nI have STEEL and a plan to break it!\pLet's see you stop us.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ProtonPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:77): PROTON: Fine, you win.\pDon't expect the next fight to\nbe polite.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_RazorIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:142): KARDINAL: You watch the attack.\nI watch the choice before it.\pTry keeping a secret from my\nPSYCHIC team!$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_RazorPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:82): KARDINAL: You cut clean through\nmy plan.\pLet's see you do it again.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:121): {PLAYER} received 1,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierOneCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:125): {PLAYER} received 1,500 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierThreeCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:133): {PLAYER} received 3,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ReceivedTierTwoCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:129): {PLAYER} received 2,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_SableIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:177): SABLE: You came looking for\nsomething exotic? My\pordinary-looking team has a few\nsurprises.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_SablePostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:106): SABLE: Shadows favor the\npatient.\pMaybe they favor you too.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:149): SHION: Still judging strength by\nappearances? My FAIRY team\penjoys correcting that mistake.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_VexPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:87): SHION: Data point recorded.\pYour odds just became more\ninteresting.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:191): VIPER: A little pressure, a\nlittle poison. I prefer a plan\pthat keeps working after the\nfirst hit.$
- `RocketLeague_Arena_Text_ViperPostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_Arena/text.inc:116): VIPER: You avoided the bite.\pNext time, the venom goes\ndeeper.$

## RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom — GIOVANNI (CHAMPION)

- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_CoinPouchFull` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:20): You can't hold more COINS.\pYou couldn't receive the prize\nCOINS.$
- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_GiovanniDefeat` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:7): Excellent.\pYour ambition has weight.$
- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_GiovanniIntro` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:1): GIOVANNI: Four victories in\nsuccession.\pYou have earned the right to face\nthe ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION.$
- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_NotReady` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:25): The ROCKET LEAGUE CHAMPION\nis not waiting here yet.$
- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_PostBattle` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:11): GIOVANNI: A title means little\nwithout your POKéMON's trust.\pKeep earning that trust.$
- `RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom_Text_ReceivedCoins` (data/maps/RocketLeague_ChampionsRoom/text.inc:16): {PLAYER} received 1,000 COINS\nas a ROCKET LEAGUE prize.$

## Route1 — BEN (YOUNGSTER_M)

- `Route1_Text_BenDefeat` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:35): Whoa!\nFieldwork can be tough!$
- `Route1_Text_BenIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:25): This is ROUTE 1!\pPALLET's researchers pass through\nhere all the time.\pI want a battle report too!$
- `Route1_Text_BenPostBattle` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:39): The grass here hides surprises.\nIt pays to watch where you step.$
- `Route1_Text_BenRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:31): I know ROUTE 1 better now.\nThis time I won't freeze up!$

## Route1 — JANICE (YOUNGSTER_F)

- `Route1_Text_JaniceDefeat` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:53): You and your POKéMON move so well\ntogether!$
- `Route1_Text_JaniceIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:43): I like watching the aides from\nPALLET study the grass here.\pLet me see how your POKéMON\nmoves through it!$
- `Route1_Text_JanicePostBattle` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:57): ROUTE 1 feels different when you\nnotice every patch of grass.$
- `Route1_Text_JaniceRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:49): You've walked this road before.\nLet's see how far you've grown!$

## Route1 — ARLO (SCOUT_M)

- `Route1_Text_MiloDefeat` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:70): You found the path through me!$
- `Route1_Text_MiloIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:61): I camp near PALLET because the\noutpost makes this road lively.\pCare for a field test?$
- `Route1_Text_MiloPostBattle` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:73): ROUTE 1 is short, but it teaches\nthe rhythm of roads, grass, and\lbattles.$
- `Route1_Text_MiloRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:66): Back through the outpost road?\nGood. Show me steadier steps!$

## Route1 — LIAM (SCOUT_M)

- `Route1_Text_LiamDefeat` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:87): I stepped right into that!$
- `Route1_Text_LiamIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:78): I keep watch near these ledges.\pThey're a quick way back, but a\nbattle is the better lesson!$
- `Route1_Text_LiamPostBattle` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:90): A good route gives you choices:\ngrass, road, or a ledge home.$
- `Route1_Text_LiamRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:83): You know the ledges now.\nDo you know my team too?$

## Route1 — DIANA (SCOUT_F)

- `Route1_Text_DianaDefeat` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:103): My picnic got scattered!$
- `Route1_Text_DianaIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:94): I packed a picnic for the walk\nbetween PALLET and VIRIDIAN.\pA battle makes the trip brighter!$
- `Route1_Text_DianaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:106): A quiet road should feel kind.\nIt gives you room to notice things.$
- `Route1_Text_DianaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route1/text.inc:99): I saved a spot on the grass.\nLet's make this walk memorable!$

## Route10 — CLARK (HIKER)

- `Route10_Text_ClarkDefeat` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:23): That's steady footing!$
- `Route10_Text_ClarkIntro` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:17): These cliffs make every step\nfeel heavier.\pLet's see how steady your\nfooting is!$
- `Route10_Text_ClarkPostBattle` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:26): The best hikers listen to\nloose stones before they step.$
- `Route10_Text_ClarkRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:176): Back on the cliffs?\pGood. The stones and I\nare ready for you!$

## Route10 — HERMAN (SAILOR)

- `Route10_Text_HermanDefeat` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:36): You kept your balance!$
- `Route10_Text_HermanIntro` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:30): I sail whenever I can, but\nROUTE 10 has its own tide.\pThe cliffs steer the wind\nlike a harbor wall!$
- `Route10_Text_HermanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:39): Follow the wind north and\nyou'll feel the bridge air.\pFollow it south and things\nturn quiet near LAVENDER.$
- `Route10_Text_HermanRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:181): The wind brought you back?\pThen let's battle before\nit changes direction!$

## Route10 — TRENT (HIKER)

- `Route10_Text_TrentDefeat` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:64): That cleared my head!$
- `Route10_Text_TrentIntro` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:59): The air near a cave mouth\nchanges before the path does.\pBreathe deep, then battle!$
- `Route10_Text_TrentPostBattle` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:67): ROCK TUNNEL air is damp,\ncold, and full of echoes.$
- `Route10_Text_TrentRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:190): Breathe in that cave air.\pNow let's see if your team\nhas grown stronger!$

## Route10 — MARK (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route10_Text_MarkDefeat` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:7): You read that current\nbetter than I did!$
- `Route10_Text_MarkIntro` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:1): The water by the POWER PLANT\npulls in sudden bursts.\pIf you can read currents,\nyou can read battles!$
- `Route10_Text_MarkPostBattle` (data/maps/Route10/text.inc:11): When storms pass over the POWER\nPLANT, the river keeps buzzing.\pIt's like nature is feeding it\npower without end.$
- `Route10_Text_MarkRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:170): Back by the POWER PLANT\ncurrent?\pThen let's see who reads\nthe water faster!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## Route10 — DILLON (TUBER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_TUBER_DILLON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5587): You took the bend faster!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_TUBER_DILLON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5580): I can see the bridge to\nROUTE 25 from up here!\pRace me before the shore bends!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_TUBER_DILLON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5589): ROUTE 10 feels like a shortcut,\nbut it twists more than you think.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_TUBER_DILLON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5584): Back near the bridge path?\pThen it's race time again!$

## Route10 — HALEY (SWIMMER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_HALEY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5040): You stayed sharp!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_HALEY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5033): The water beside ROCK TUNNEL\nis colder than it looks.\pA cold current makes you sharp!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_HALEY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5042): The cave keeps the shade here.\nEven sunny days feel cool.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_HALEY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5037): Back for another cold swim?\pI'll make this battle sharp!$

## Route10 — GAVIN (FISHERMAN)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_FISHERMAN_KYLE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4934): You reeled me in!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_FISHERMAN_KYLE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4927): I cast my line where the\nriver quiets near LAVENDER.\pQuiet water still has bite!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_FISHERMAN_KYLE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4936): The closer you get to LAVENDER,\nthe quieter the water feels.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_FISHERMAN_KYLE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4931): Back by the quiet water?\pGood. My line's already set!$

## Route10 — SUSIE (SWIMMER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_SUSIE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5372): That woke me up!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_SUSIE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5364): South of here, LAVENDER\ngets so still.\pI swim hard to shake off\nthat quiet!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_SUSIE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5374): It's strange.\pThe water moves, but the road\nstill feels calm near LAVENDER.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE10_SWIMMER_SUSIE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5369): Need to shake off the quiet?\pA battle will do it!$

## Route10 — MIKA (TUBER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10TuberMika_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6195): You splashed right past me!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10TuberMika_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6186): The bridge up north makes\nthis place feel secret.\pLike a beach hiding under\nall the cliffs!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10TuberMika_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6197): If you follow the water north,\nit feels like CERULEAN is\ljust around the bend.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10TuberMika_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6191): You found the hidden beach\nfeeling again!\pLet's battle!$

## Route10 — PIERCE (FISHERMAN)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10FishermanPierce_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6154): You felt the line twitch!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10FishermanPierce_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6146): The POWER PLANT hums through\nthe water if you listen.\pThe fish notice it before we do!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10FishermanPierce_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6156): Some days the current changes\nright when the plant surges.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10FishermanPierce_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6150): Hear that hum again?\pThen the fish are moving,\nand so are we!$

## Route10 — NOLA (SWIMMER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SwimmerNola_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6180): You beat my timing!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SwimmerNola_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6173): I time my laps between the\nPOWER PLANT and ROCK TUNNEL.\pOne buzzes, one echoes!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SwimmerNola_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6182): It's a good route for training.\pThe water never lets you keep\none rhythm for long.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SwimmerNola_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6177): I heard the echo and the hum.\pThat means it's rematch time!$

## Route10 — ARLEN (SAILOR)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SailorArlen_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6167): You held the channel!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SailorArlen_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6159): A sailor can learn plenty\naway from the sea.\pROUTE 10's narrow waterway\nteaches control!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SailorArlen_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6169): VERMILION has open harbor air.\pHere, the cliffs squeeze every\ngust into a command.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route10SailorArlen_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6164): Back in this narrow channel?\pGood. Tight water, tight battle!$

## Route11 — EVAN (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_EVAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4812): You scattered my swarm!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_EVAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4804): Bugs love the warm grass beside\nthe harbor road.\pI came to watch them, but a\nbattle works too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_EVAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4814): VERMILION's wind warms the grass,\nand warm grass brings bugs.\pThat's fieldwork!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_EVAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4809): The bugs are buzzing louder.\pThey know it's rematch time!$

## Route11 — CHAPLIN (POKÉMANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_POKEMANIAC_CHAPLIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4836): I would've won had you\nchallenged me before they shut\lthemselves away.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_POKEMANIAC_CHAPLIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4832): I study MR. MIME, but they've\nall withdrawn into DIGLETT'S\pCAVE! Perhaps a battle will\nclear my head.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_POKEMANIAC_CHAPLIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4840): The MR. MIME went into DIGLETT\nCAVE and erected barriers.\pIt's like they built their own\nlittle community and closed the\lworld out.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_POKEMANIAC_CHAPLIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4834): Still no sign of the wild MR.\nMIME. My own partner is eager to\psee you again, though!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## Route11 — MARK (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_MARK_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4826): You spotted my weak point!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_MARK_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4818): People hurry to the gate and\nmiss all the tiny movements.\pA true bug fan sees everything!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_MARK_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4828): The best things on ROUTE 11\nare small enough to hide\lunder one leaf.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_MARK_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4822): You came back slowly enough\nto notice me this time.\pGood. Rematch!$

## Route11 — LOGAN (POKÉMON_RANGER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_LOGAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5457): Good control. Well done.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_LOGAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5448): ROUTE 11 links city traffic\nto wild cave habitat.\pI make sure travelers respect\nboth sides.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_LOGAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5459): The safest routes are the ones\nwhere people notice the wild\lplaces beside them.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_LOGAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5453): You crossed without disturbing\nthe grass or cave mouth.\pThat earns a rematch.$

## Route11 — BETH (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_BETH_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4701): Strong roots, stronger trainer!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_BETH_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4692): The DIGLETT stir the soil\nunder this route.\pThat makes the grass and flowers\ngrow with unusual strength.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_BETH_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4703): If the ground trembles lightly,\nit may just be DIGLETT\lworking below.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_BETH_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4697): The soil is loose again today.\pMy POKéMON feel lively.\nShall we battle?$

## Route11 — MARCOS (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_RUIN_MANIAC_MARCOS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4798): That shook my theory loose!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_RUIN_MANIAC_MARCOS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4789): DIGLETT'S CAVE cuts beneath\nKANTO like an old passage.\pI study the stones it pushes\nup to the surface!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_RUIN_MANIAC_MARCOS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4800): The stones near DIGLETT'S CAVE\ntell you where the tunnel\lhas widened over time.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE11_RUIN_MANIAC_MARCOS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4794): Back to inspect the cave mouth?\pThen let's compare field notes\nthrough battle!$

## Route11 — ALLISON (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALLISON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5414): You kept steadier than us!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALLISON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5406): POKéMON raised near DIGLETT'S\nCAVE get used to sudden rumbling.\pMine won't flinch easily!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALLISON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5416): A road can train temperament.\pNoise, wind, footsteps,\ntremors-it all matters.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALLISON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5410): The ground rumbled twice today.\pMy team took it calmly.\nReady for a rematch?$

## Route11 — LINA (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_LINA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4191): Even the breeze paused.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_LINA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4182): The flowers here smell faintly\nof sea air from VERMILION.\pLet me show you what they\nbring out in my POKéMON.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_LINA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4193): Plants near busy roads grow\ntougher than you expect.\pThey learn to live with noise.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_AROMA_LADY_LINA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4187): The breeze is carrying that\nsalt-sweet scent again.\pA rematch would suit it.$

## Route11 — REMY (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_REMI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4351): You know the route now!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_REMI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4342): I scout the path between\nVERMILION and the eastern gate.\pIf you can cross it cleanly,\nshow me!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_REMI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4353): This road looks simple, but it\nfeeds into caves, gates, and\lthe long way to LAVENDER.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_REMI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4347): Back from the east gate?\pGood. Let's test how much\nground you've covered.$

## Route12 — NED (FISHERMAN)

- `Route12_Text_NedDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:7): That was the big one!$
- `Route12_Text_NedIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:1): The water gets quiet south\nof LAVENDER.\pQuiet water makes every tug\nfeel huge!$
- `Route12_Text_NedPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:10): Some folks hurry over the bridge.\pFISHERMEN know this route is\nbetter when you wait.$
- `Route12_Text_NedRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:244): The water went quiet again.\pThat means either a bite...\nor a rematch!$

## Route12 — CHIP (FISHERMAN)

- `Route12_Text_ChipDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:21): You broke the line!$
- `Route12_Text_ChipIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:15): On ROUTE 12, patience is\nas important as bait.\pLet's see if you can wait out\na battle too.$
- `Route12_Text_ChipPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:24): The bridge shadows draw all sorts\nof POKéMON beneath them.$
- `Route12_Text_ChipRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:249): I waited by the same shadow.\pLooks like patience brought\nyou back.$

## Route12 — HANK (FISHERMAN)

- `Route12_Text_HankDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:58): You read me clean!$
- `Route12_Text_HankIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:52): Sportfishing means reading the\nwater, the wind, and your\lPOKéMON.\pCan you read a battle?$
- `Route12_Text_HankPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:61): A bite can come from either side\nof the bridge.\pThat's why I keep looking around.$
- `Route12_Text_HankRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:264): I read the wind.\nI read the water.\pNow I'll read your rematch.$

## Route12 — ANDREW (FISHERMAN)

- `Route12_Text_AndrewDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:84): You landed it!$
- `Route12_Text_AndrewIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:78): The farther south you go,\nthe more the route opens up.\pLet's see what your team can\ncatch before ROUTE 13!$
- `Route12_Text_AndrewPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:87): ROUTE 12 is long enough that\nthe water changes mood before\lyou reach the bottom.$
- `Route12_Text_AndrewRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:274): Back before ROUTE 13?\pGood timing.\nThe water just changed mood.$

## Route12 — GIA & JES (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route12_Text_GiaDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:128): GIA: We lost the pace!$
- `Route12_Text_GiaIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:123): GIA: JES, match my pace!\pThis bridge is our training lane,\nand we're not slowing down!$
- `Route12_Text_GiaNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:135): GIA: JES and I battle together.\pCome back when you have two\nPOKéMON ready.$
- `Route12_Text_GiaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:131): GIA: The route is narrow,\nbut it teaches teamwork.$
- `Route12_Text_GiaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:283): GIA: JES, eyes forward!\pThe rematch starts on my count.$
- `Route12_Text_JesDefeat` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:111): JES: Our rhythm slipped!$
- `Route12_Text_JesIntro` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:105): JES: GIA and I train together\non this long bridge.\pIf our rhythm is right,\nwe can't lose!$
- `Route12_Text_JesNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:118): JES: GIA and I battle as a pair.\pBring two POKéMON if you want\nto face us properly.$
- `Route12_Text_JesPostBattle` (data/maps/Route12/text.inc:114): JES: Training beside the water\nhelps us keep pace together.$
- `Route12_Text_JesRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:279): JES: GIA, keep the bridge pace!\pThis time we'll stay in rhythm.$

## Route12 — DAVE (TUBER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_TUBER_DAVE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5562): You splashed ahead!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_TUBER_DAVE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5555): Everyone calls this the fishing\nroute, but I found a splash lane!\pRace me before the anglers notice!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_TUBER_DAVE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5564): The best part of ROUTE 12 is\nhow long it stays by the water.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_TUBER_DAVE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5559): Back to my splash lane?\pThen we race-battle again!$

## Route12 — DAWN (SWIMMER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_SWIMMER_DAWN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5028): You held against the current!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_SWIMMER_DAWN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5020): The bridge cuts the wind,\nbut the water still pushes hard.\pI train where the current argues\nwith the posts!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_SWIMMER_DAWN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5030): Swimming beside a bridge teaches\nyou to notice every little pull.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE12_SWIMMER_DAWN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5025): The current's arguing again.\pPerfect timing for a rematch!$

## Route12 — LUCA (TUBER_M)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberLuca_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6284): My splash barely reached you!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberLuca_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6277): The grown-ups fish all day,\nbut I say the bridge is for\lsplashing too!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberLuca_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6286): If you stand still too long here,\nsomeone starts fishing beside you.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberLuca_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6281): Back to splash by the bridge?\pThen let's battle!$

## Route12 — PIA (TUBER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberPia_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6296): I counted that as a loss!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberPia_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6289): I counted every bridge post\nfrom LAVENDER down here.\pThen I got bored and battled!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberPia_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6298): ROUTE 12 is so long that even\ncounting bridge posts feels like\la journey.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TuberPia_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6293): I lost count again.\pA rematch sounds more fun!$

## Route12 — EBB (FISHERMAN)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanEbb_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6208): Soft water, hard loss.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanEbb_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6201): I fish where LAVENDER's quiet\nsettles onto the water.\pEven the splashes sound softer.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanEbb_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6210): Some FISHERMEN like chatter.\pI like listening for the float\nto dip.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanEbb_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6205): Back to the quiet water?\pKeep your voice low and battle!$

## Route12 — REED (FISHERMAN)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanReed_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6221): You pulled me from cover!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanReed_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6214): Down here, the bridge shadows\nmake perfect hiding spots.\pFor fish and for battles!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanReed_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6223): The longer the bridge,\nthe more shadows underneath.\pThat means more places to cast.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12FishermanReed_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6218): Back under the bridge shadows?\pLet's see what bites first.$

## Route12 — CORA (SWIMMER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerCora_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6246): You read the current!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerCora_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6239): The anglers watch the surface.\pI train below it, where the\ncurrent really moves.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerCora_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6248): Don't judge this route from the\nbridge alone.\pThe water has its own road.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerCora_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6243): Back to test the current?\pDive into this rematch!$

## Route12 — MARIS (SWIMMER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerMaris_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6260): Clean turn!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerMaris_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6252): I swim laps between the bridge\nposts.\pMiss one turn and the current\nlaughs at you!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerMaris_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6262): The bridge makes a perfect lane\nif you know how to use it.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SwimmerMaris_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6257): Back between the posts?\pThen keep your turns sharp!$

## Route12 — BRIGGS (SAILOR)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SailorBriggs_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6234): You steered through my defence!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SailorBriggs_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6227): A sailor can read open water.\pROUTE 12 teaches the opposite:\ntight water, tight turns!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SailorBriggs_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6236): VERMILION HARBOR feels wide\nafter you train here awhile.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12SailorBriggs_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6231): Back in the narrow channel?\pGood. Hold your course!$

## Route12 — VERA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TriathleteVera_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6272): You kept the pace!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TriathleteVera_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6265): Route training means adapting:\nbridge, water, bridge again.\pShow me your pace!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TriathleteVera_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6274): ROUTE 12 is endurance training\ndisguised as a shortcut south.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_Route12TriathleteVera_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6269): Back for another long-route\npace test?$

## Route13 — SEBASTIAN (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route13_Text_SebastianDefeat` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:6): The crosswind carried you instead!$
- `Route13_Text_SebastianIntro` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:1): The wind off SILENCE BRIDGE makes\nmy bird POKéMON restless.\pThey want a battle!$
- `Route13_Text_SebastianPostBattle` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:9): Bird POKéMON love this route.\pThe rough ground keeps people slow,\nbut the sky stays wide open.$
- `Route13_Text_SebastianRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:287): The wind's up again.\pMy bird POKéMON remember how you\nhandled it last time!$

## Route13 — SHEILA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route13_Text_SheilaDefeat` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:91): You kept a better pace!$
- `Route13_Text_SheilaIntro` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:86): Loose stones, broken grass,\nawkward turns...\pPerfect endurance training!$
- `Route13_Text_SheilaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:94): ROUTE 13 punishes sloppy footwork.\pIf your stride is uneven, your\nbattle rhythm goes with it.$
- `Route13_Text_SheilaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:318): Back for another rough-ground run?\pGood. My team's pace has improved!$

## Route13 — MARTIN (HIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_HIKER_ALAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5616): You stayed steady!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_HIKER_ALAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5608): The rocks here are smaller than\nmountain stone, but just as mean.\pWatch your footing!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_HIKER_ALAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5618): A HIKER can learn plenty here.\pThe bridge is flat, but this road\nmakes every step count.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_HIKER_ALAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5612): Back over the broken ground?\pThen let's see if your stance\nhas improved!$

## Route13 — PERRY (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route13_Text_PerryDefeat` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:67): You cut through the wind!$
- `Route13_Text_PerryIntro` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:62): This bend catches every gust from\nthe bridge.\pMy birds know how to ride it!$
- `Route13_Text_PerryPostBattle` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:70): A bird keeper learns to read the\nair before giving a command.\pROUTE 13 changes by the minute.$
- `Route13_Text_PerryRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:308): I studied the gusts since our\nlast battle.\pThis time, we'll ride them better!$

## Route13 — JARED (BIKER)

- `Route13_Text_JaredDefeat` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:104): Tch!\nMy wheels slipped!$
- `Route13_Text_JaredIntro` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:99): These ruts are rough on tires.\pIf you can handle them, you can\nhandle a battle!$
- `Route13_Text_JaredPostBattle` (data/maps/Route13/text.inc:108): ROUTE 13's no smooth ride.\pThat's why I like it.$
- `Route13_Text_JaredRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:322): I learned these ruts better.\pTry keeping up this time!$

## Route13 — JOHNSON (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_SCOUT_JOHNSON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5642): You picked the better line!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_SCOUT_JOHNSON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5634): A SCOUT checks the safest line\nthrough rough country.\pBattle me before you choose yours!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_SCOUT_JOHNSON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5644): North is SILENCE BRIDGE.\pSouth, the road gets rough again.\nA good SCOUT marks both.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_SCOUT_JOHNSON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5638): You found your way back through\nthe rough grass.\pGood. Now prove you learned it!$

## Route13 — KOJI (BIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_BIKER_KOJI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5629): Skidded out!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_BIKER_KOJI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5622): This track is all ruts and grit.\pThat's perfect for a BIKER who\nwants better control!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_BIKER_KOJI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5631): Smooth roads make lazy riders.\pROUTE 13 keeps me honest.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_BIKER_KOJI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5626): I took the rough line twice today.\pMy tires and my team are ready!$

## Route13 — TESSA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_TRIATHLETE_MISSY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5656): You had the stronger finish!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_TRIATHLETE_MISSY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5648): I pace myself between bridge\nplanks and broken ground.\pCan your POKéMON keep rhythm?$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_TRIATHLETE_MISSY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5658): Endurance is about adapting.\pOn ROUTE 13, the ground changes\nbefore your breathing does.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE13_TRIATHLETE_MISSY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5652): I tightened my pace on the bend.\pLet's see if your team can match\nit again!$

## Route14 — GERALD (BIKER)

- `Route14_Text_GeraldDefeat` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:113): Wipeout!\nYou found the clean line.$
- `Route14_Text_GeraldIntro` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:108): We ride out here because the road\nstays wild.\pNo clean lanes, no easy turns!$
- `Route14_Text_GeraldPostBattle` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:117): ROUTE 14 bites back if you get\ncareless.\pThat's what makes it worth riding.$
- `Route14_Text_GeraldRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:369): I've learned every rut between\nhere and FUCHSIA.\pTry taking my line now!$

## Route14 — DONALD (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route14_Text_DonaldDefeat` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:62): Your call was clearer!$
- `Route14_Text_DonaldIntro` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:57): I follow bird calls between\nROUTE 13 and FUCHSIA.\pThis stretch has the best echoes!$
- `Route14_Text_DonaldPostBattle` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:65): The trees break up the wind here.\pBird POKéMON learn quick turns\nbetween the gusts.$
- `Route14_Text_DonaldRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:349): The calls are echoing again.\pThat means my birds are ready\nfor a rematch!$

## Route14 — ISAAC (BIKER)

- `Route14_Text_IsaacDefeat` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:99): Tch!\nYou didn't shake loose!$
- `Route14_Text_IsaacIntro` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:94): This road's rough enough to rattle\nyour teeth.\pLet's see if it rattles your team!$
- `Route14_Text_IsaacPostBattle` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:103): I like roads that fight back.\pSmooth pavement makes everyone\nthink they're tough.$
- `Route14_Text_IsaacRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:364): You came back over this rough road?\pFine. Let's see if you still hold\nsteady!$

## Route14 — CARTER (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route14_Text_CarterDefeat` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:6): Not enough lift!$
- `Route14_Text_CarterIntro` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:1): The rough road opens into big sky\nout here.\pMy bird POKéMON can feel it!$
- `Route14_Text_CarterPostBattle` (data/maps/Route14/text.inc:9): Bird keepers like open roads.\pYou can watch the ground and the\nsky at the same time.$
- `Route14_Text_CarterRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:330): My birds read the open road\nbetter now.\pLet's see if you do too!$

## Route14 — LOLA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_LOLA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5669): My stride fell apart!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_LOLA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5662): This road keeps changing underfoot.\pThat's the best way to train a\nsteady stride!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_LOLA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5671): ROUTE 14 is tougher than it looks.\pIf your feet drift, your commands\ndrift with them.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_LOLA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5666): Back for another uneven run?\pGood. My rhythm is sharper now!$

## Route14 — DESTIN (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_SCOUT_DESTIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5682): You read the path better!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_SCOUT_DESTIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5675): A SCOUT marks where the grass\nbreaks and where the path bends.\pBattle me before you pass!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_SCOUT_DESTIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5684): This road funnels travelers toward\nFUCHSIA.\pA good SCOUT watches who arrives\ntired and who arrives ready.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_SCOUT_DESTIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5679): You found my marker again.\pThat deserves another battle!$

## Route14 — BRICE (HIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_HIKER_BRICE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5697): You stepped cleaner!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_HIKER_BRICE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5689): A HIKER doesn't need a mountain\nto test his boots.\pThis rough road will do!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_HIKER_BRICE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5699): ROUTE 14 is good training.\pThe stones are low, but they still\nmake you pay attention.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_HIKER_BRICE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5693): My boots know this road now.\pLet's see if your POKéMON know it\nas well!$

## Route14 — IRENE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_IRENE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5711): You had the better kick!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_IRENE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5703): I use the long straightaways for\nspeed and the rough patches for\lcontrol.\pNow I need battle practice!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_IRENE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5713): ROUTE 14 is a good endurance road.\pIt never lets you settle into one\neasy rhythm.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE14_TRIATHLETE_IRENE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5708): I ran the straightaway twice today.\pLet's see if that extra pace shows!$

## Route15 — EDWIN (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route15_Text_EdwinDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:32): They spotted you, but couldn't\nstop you!$
- `Route15_Text_EdwinIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:27): Bird POKéMON love watching the\nroad open toward FUCHSIA.\pMine spotted you first!$
- `Route15_Text_EdwinPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:36): The closer you get to FUCHSIA,\nthe more the sky feels busy.\pBirds, travelers, trainers...\neveryone's moving.$
- `Route15_Text_EdwinRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:395): My birds spotted you again.\pThey remember the way you battled!$

## Route15 — OLIVIA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route15_Text_OliviaDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:74): You broke my rhythm!$
- `Route15_Text_OliviaIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:69): I train where the road gets busy,\nthen quiet, then busy again.\pThat rhythm is perfect!$
- `Route15_Text_OliviaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:77): A TRIATHLETE has to adapt.\pROUTE 15 gives you road, grass,\nand city edge all at once.$
- `Route15_Text_OliviaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:409): I found a better rhythm on this\nroad.\pLet's see if you can break it\nagain!$

## Route15 — ALEX (BIKER)

- `Route15_Text_AlexDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:114): You know them well enough!$
- `Route15_Text_AlexIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:109): Coming from FUCHSIA?\nThen you should know rough roads.\pShow me!$
- `Route15_Text_AlexPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:117): BIKERS don't need smooth streets.\pGive us gravel, ruts, and room\nto move.$
- `Route15_Text_AlexRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:424): You still know how to handle this\nroad?\pProve it again!$

## Route15 — GRACE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route15_Text_GraceDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:61): You had the faster finish!$
- `Route15_Text_GraceIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:56): I use this long road for finishing\nsprints.\pDon't blink when I take off!$
- `Route15_Text_GracePostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:64): The final run into FUCHSIA feels\ngreat when your breathing is\lsteady.$
- `Route15_Text_GraceRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:404): Back for another finishing sprint?\pGood. I saved my best pace for\nyou!$

## Route15 — RON & MYA (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route15_Text_MyaDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:155): MYA: RON, watch your footing!\nThis route punishes drift.$
- `Route15_Text_MyaIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:150): MYA: This road toughens balance\nand timing.\pHelp me train my little brother?$
- `Route15_Text_MyaNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:164): MYA: Do you want to challenge us?\nYou'll need two POKéMON, though.$
- `Route15_Text_MyaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:159): MYA: We'll keep using ROUTE 15.\pIf RON can handle this road,\nhe can handle anything.$
- `Route15_Text_MyaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:438): MYA: RON's footing improved.\pHelp me test him on this road\nagain?$
- `Route15_Text_RonDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:173): RON: Oh, no...\nI slipped again!$
- `Route15_Text_RonIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:168): RON: My sister says this road\nbuilds character.\pI think it builds sore feet.$
- `Route15_Text_RonNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:182): RON: Did you want to battle with\nmy sister and me?\pYou need two POKéMON, then.$
- `Route15_Text_RonPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:177): RON: FUCHSIA is so close.\pMaybe if I run there fast enough,\ntraining will be over.$
- `Route15_Text_RonRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:443): RON: My sister says I'm better\nat rough-road battles now.\pPlease don't prove her wrong...$

## Route15 — CORDELL (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_SCOUT_CORDELL_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5725): You found it!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_SCOUT_CORDELL_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5717): The gate to FUCHSIA is close,\nbut the rough grass still matters.\pA SCOUT checks the last stretch!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_SCOUT_CORDELL_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5727): People relax when a city is close.\pThat's when they miss loose stones,\nbent grass, and hidden turns.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_SCOUT_CORDELL_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5721): Back on the last stretch?\pThen show me you remember the\nsafe line!$

## Route15 — KINDRA (SCOUT_F)

- `Route15_Text_KindraDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:7): You checked it better!$
- `Route15_Text_KindraIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:1): FUCHSIA is close, but don't rush\nthe last stretch.\pA SCOUT checks the rough grass\nbefore stepping through!$
- `Route15_Text_KindraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:10): Between the forest path, the road,\nand FUCHSIA's gate, ROUTE 15 has\lmore choices than it first shows.$
- `Route15_Text_KindraRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:385): I marked a better line through\nthe grass.\pWant to test it with a rematch?$

## Route15 — CLARK (HIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_CLARK_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5740): You outpaced me!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_CLARK_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5731): A road doesn't need a mountain\nto work your legs.\pROUTE 15 has enough rough ground\nfor me!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_CLARK_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5742): A HIKER notices where the land\nchanges.\pNear FUCHSIA, the road starts to\nfeel softer but no less tricky.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_CLARK_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5736): I've walked this road again.\pMy boots and my POKéMON are\nsettled in now!$

## Route15 — TRENT (HIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_TRENT_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5755): Solid footing!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_TRENT_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5747): The stones here sit low, but they\nstill twist an ankle.\pStep carefully, then battle hard!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_TRENT_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5757): ROUTE 15 is a reminder that rough\nterrain isn't always tall terrain.\pSometimes it's right underfoot.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_HIKER_TRENT_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5751): You came back over the low stones.\pGood. Let's see if your team\nkept its footing!$

## Route15 — LUKAS (BIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIKER_LUKAS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5769): You took the inside line!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIKER_LUKAS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5761): This road gets tight near FUCHSIA.\pA BIKER has to make every turn\ncount!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIKER_LUKAS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5771): ROUTE 15 teaches control.\pToo much speed and you're in the\ngrass before you know it.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIKER_LUKAS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5765): I tightened my turns since last\ntime.\pLet's see if you can keep up!$

## Route15 — CHESTER (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route15_Text_ChesterDefeat` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:48): They were right to be alert!$
- `Route15_Text_ChesterIntro` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:42): My birds get jumpy near the\nforest edge.\pSomething about this route keeps\nthem alert!$
- `Route15_Text_ChesterPostBattle` (data/maps/Route15/text.inc:51): Bird keepers listen for changes.\pOn ROUTE 15, the wind sounds\ndifferent near the trees.$
- `Route15_Text_ChesterRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:399): My birds heard your steps before\nI did.\pThey're ready this time!$

## Route15 — MITCH (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MITCH_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5783): Sharp eyes weren't enough!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MITCH_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5775): My bird POKéMON watch travelers\narrive from FUCHSIA.\pThey picked you out!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MITCH_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5785): Bird POKéMON like this route.\pThe forest edge gives cover, and\nthe road gives them sightlines.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MITCH_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5779): My birds picked you out again.\pThey know a strong TRAINER when\nthey see one!$

## Route15 — MARLON (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MARLON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5796): You ruled the sky!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MARLON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5789): The sky opens just enough here\nfor my birds to stretch out.\pLet's battle under it!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MARLON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5798): The trees make the air twist.\pA good bird keeper teaches quick\nturns before high climbs.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE15_BIRD_KEEPER_MARLON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5793): The sky's clear again.\pMy birds want another go!$

## Route16 — LAO (BIKER)

- `Route16_Text_LaoDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:6): Tch.\nYou kept your line.$
- `Route16_Text_LaoIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:1): CYCLING ROAD starts here.\pYou either control the slope,\nor the slope controls you.$
- `Route16_Text_LaoPostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:10): CELADON's polished streets end\nfast out here.\pThat's why we hang around.\nThe road has a rougher pulse.$
- `Route16_Text_LaoRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:448): Back at the top of CYCLING\nROAD?\pLet's see if you control the\nslope better this time.$

## Route16 — CAMRON (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route16_Text_CamronDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:56): That warmed me up too much!$
- `Route16_Text_CamronIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:51): I warm up before the downhill\nrun.\pA battle gets the blood moving.$
- `Route16_Text_CamronPostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:59): A tough team helps when the\nroad is crowded.\pEveryone wants space before\nthe slope takes over.$
- `Route16_Text_CamronRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:467): I warmed up for the downhill\nrun again.\pA rematch should finish the job!$

## Route16 — LUKE (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route16_Text_LukeDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:31): You didn't wobble!$
- `Route16_Text_LukeIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:26): At the top of the slope,\neveryone acts brave.\pLet's see if you are.$
- `Route16_Text_LukePostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:34): The road south pulls at you.\pFirst lesson: don't fight the\nslope. Steer through it.$
- `Route16_Text_LukeRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:458): Still brave at the top of the\nslope?\pGood. Prove it again!$

## Route16 — RUBEN (BIKER)

- `Route16_Text_RubenDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:71): You rode right through!$
- `Route16_Text_RubenIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:65): West of CELADON, the air\nchanges.\pSmells like rubber, grass,\nand trouble.$
- `Route16_Text_RubenPostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:74): ROUTE 16 is a staging line.\pOnce you roll south, CYCLING\nROAD takes over.$
- `Route16_Text_RubenRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:472): Smell that?\pRubber, grass, and another\nbattle on ROUTE 16.$

## Route16 — LEA & JED (TRENDSETTERS)

- `Route16_Text_JedDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:95): JED: That was a proper battle!$
- `Route16_Text_JedIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:89): JED: LEA and I are watching\ntrainers choose between CELADON\land the slope.\pShow us a proper battle!$
- `Route16_Text_JedNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:104): JED: This is a DOUBLE BATTLE.\pBring two POKéMON, then show us\nyour teamwork.$
- `Route16_Text_JedPostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:98): JED: CELADON makes for a fine\ndate, but this view is better.\pYou can feel KANTO stretching\nout from here.$
- `Route16_Text_JedRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:477): JED: Back to choose between\nCELADON and the slope?\pShow LEA and me another proper\nbattle first!$
- `Route16_Text_LeaDefeat` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:115): LEA: That echoed down the road!$
- `Route16_Text_LeaIntro` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:109): LEA: The city noise fades right\nhere.\pLet's make some battle noise\ninstead!$
- `Route16_Text_LeaNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:124): LEA: We need a pair against\na pair.\pCome back with two POKéMON,\nokay?$
- `Route16_Text_LeaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route16/text.inc:118): LEA: Trainers come from CELADON\nwith clean shoes.\pThey leave ROUTE 16 ready for\ndust and downhill speed.$
- `Route16_Text_LeaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:483): LEA: The city got quiet again.\pLet's make ROUTE 16 echo with\nanother battle!$

## Route16 — JULIA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_LASS_JULIA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5068): You didn't slow down at all!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_LASS_JULIA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5059): CELADON is right there, but\neveryone speeds off toward\lCYCLING ROAD.\pSlow down and battle me first!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_LASS_JULIA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5070): ROUTE 16 feels like a doorway.\pOne side is city streets, the\nother is rushing downhill.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_LASS_JULIA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5064): Back at the edge of CELADON?\pGood. I saved another battle\nbefore you hit the slope!$

## Route16 — TALIA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_SCOUT_ARIANA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5082): Your team is ready for the slope!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_SCOUT_ARIANA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5074): A good rider checks the ground\nbefore a long ride.\pLet's check your team, too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_SCOUT_ARIANA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5084): The best place to rest is before\nthe road gets steep.\pAfter that, your wheels make\nmost of the choices.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE16_SCOUT_ARIANA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5078): Still training at the top of\nCYCLING ROAD?\pThen I'll check your team again!$

## Route17 — VIRGIL (BIKER)

- `Route17_Text_VirgilDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:36): Clean pass!$
- `Route17_Text_VirgilIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:31): Heading down toward FUCHSIA?\pThen you need road sense as much\nas speed.$
- `Route17_Text_VirgilPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:39): The best rides are the ones\nwhere you never panic.\pROUTE 17 rewards calm hands.$
- `Route17_Text_VirgilRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:498): Heading down toward FUCHSIA\nagain?\pThen prove your road sense again.$

## Route17 — ISAIAH (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route17_Text_IsaiahDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:22): Whoo!\nYou held steady!$
- `Route17_Text_IsaiahIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:16): Holding steady on this road\ntakes muscle.\pLet's see how strong your stance\nis!$
- `Route17_Text_IsaiahPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:26): I train here because the road\nnever lets you coast forever.\pEven stopping is a workout.$
- `Route17_Text_IsaiahRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:493): Still holding steady on this\ndownhill road?\pShow me that stance again!$

## Route17 — RAUL (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route17_Text_RaulDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:7): I lost my grip!$
- `Route17_Text_RaulIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:1): People drop all kinds of things\nwhen the slope gets fast.\pBattle me before you check the\nroadside!$
- `Route17_Text_RaulPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:10): You can find good stuff lying\naround on CYCLING ROAD.\pThe trick is stopping safely\nbefore you pick it up.$
- `Route17_Text_RaulRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:488): Back to search the roadside?\pBattle me before you check what\nthe slope shook loose.$

## Route17 — NIKOLAS (BIKER)

- `Route17_Text_NikolasDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:62): Grounded out!$
- `Route17_Text_NikolasIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:57): A sharp spark keeps riders awake\non the long downhill.\pLet VOLTORB jolt you!$
- `Route17_Text_NikolasPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:65): I caught VOLTORB before the\nPOWER PLANT reopened. It likes\pthis road's constant motion as\nmuch as I do.$
- `Route17_Text_NikolasRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:508): VOLTORB's charged from all this\nmotion.\pReady for another jolt?$

## Route17 — JAMAL (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route17_Text_JamalDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:87): Whew!\nGood workout!$
- `Route17_Text_JamalIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:82): This road is my exercise track.\pA battle halfway down keeps the\nlegs honest!$
- `Route17_Text_JamalPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:91): Going downhill sounds easy until\nyou have to keep control.\pThat's where the workout hides.$
- `Route17_Text_JamalRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:518): Back for another halfway-down\nworkout?\pGood. My legs were getting lazy!$

## Route17 — JAXON (BIKER)

- `Route17_Text_JaxonDefeat` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:113): Smooth handling!$
- `Route17_Text_JaxonIntro` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:108): Nice BIKE!\pHow's it handle when the road\nstarts dragging you south?$
- `Route17_Text_JaxonPostBattle` (data/maps/Route17/text.inc:116): The slope makes it hard to steer.\pIf your hands get tense, the\nwhole ride gets worse.$
- `Route17_Text_JaxonRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:528): Still riding that BIKE?\pShow me how it handles in a\nrematch!$

## Route17 — KAY (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_LASS_KAY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5986): You flew past me!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_LASS_KAY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5978): I came out from CELADON to watch\nthe bikes fly past.\pWatching made me want a battle!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_LASS_KAY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5988): CYCLING ROAD looks intimidating,\nbut everyone finds a pace.\pEven watching has a rhythm.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_LASS_KAY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5982): Back on the fast road?\pGood. I was hoping to see your\nteam fly past again!$

## Route17 — LEAH (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_SCOUT_LEAH_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6000): You made good use of the stop!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_SCOUT_LEAH_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5992): A good rider marks places to\npause on a long route.\pThis one has room for a battle!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_SCOUT_LEAH_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6002): ROUTE 17 is long, but it is not\nempty.\pGood rest points turn a hard road\ninto a manageable one.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_SCOUT_LEAH_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5996): Back at my marked rest point?\pThen you're rested enough for\nanother battle!$

## Route17 — OTIS (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route17_EventScript_Milo_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5900): You passed me!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Milo_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5896): I ride beside the BIKERS until\nmy legs ache. My team still has\penergy for you!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Milo_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5902): This route is long enough for a\nrace and narrow enough for a\nbattle.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Milo_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5898): Chain checked, tyres ready. One\nbattle before my next ride!$

## Route17 — TOBY (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route17_EventScript_Tess_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5946): Lost count!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Tess_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5939): I count BIKERS as they pass.\pYou're trainer number too many\nnot to challenge!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Tess_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5948): The best part of CYCLING ROAD\nis watching everyone find their\nown pace.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Tess_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5943): I counted you coming back.\pThat means I get another battle!$

## Route17 — JUNE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route17_EventScript_June_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5877): There goes my rhythm!$
- `Route17_EventScript_June_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5871): My food is strapped to the bike,\nbut the wind keeps pulling at\pthe bag! Let's stop for a match.$
- `Route17_EventScript_June_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5879): I eat before the last climb. An\nempty stomach makes for heavy\ppedals.$
- `Route17_EventScript_June_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5873): Back on the windy stretch?\pGood. My lunch can wait for\none more battle!$

## Route17 — MILES (BIKER)

- `Route17_EventScript_Miles_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5889): You drowned out my wheels!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Miles_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5881): I mark the middle stretch by\nhow loud the wheels get.\pYou're right on time for battle!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Miles_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5891): Halfway down, riders either\nsettle in or lose focus.\pI battle the ones who still\nlook sharp.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Miles_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5885): Heard your wheels coming.\pLet's make the middle stretch\nloud again!$

## Route17 — WADE (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route17_EventScript_Wade_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5972): You took the bend!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Wade_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5965): I guard this bend because people\ntake it too fast.\pBattle me, then brake harder!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Wade_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5974): The bend teaches patience.\pRush it and the road chooses\nyour line for you.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Wade_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5969): Back at the bend?\pGood. Brake, battle, then roll.$

## Route17 — ORSON (BIKER)

- `Route17_EventScript_Orson_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5933): Solid braking!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Orson_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5924): The lower slope gets faster\nthan people expect.\pIf you can stop for battle,\nyou're doing fine.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Orson_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5935): Anyone can roll downhill.\pA good rider chooses exactly\nwhere to stop.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Orson_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5929): Managed to stop on the lower\nslope again?\pThen you can stop for a rematch.$

## Route17 — VAUGHN (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route17_EventScript_Vaughn_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5960): Clean enough!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Vaughn_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5952): The road gets narrow here.\pThat means we settle things\nquick and clean.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Vaughn_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5962): A long road needs clear lanes.\pWithout them, everyone eats dust.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Vaughn_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5956): Back in my lane?\pThen we settle it quick and\nclean again.$

## Route17 — NOEL (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route17_EventScript_Mina_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5919): You kept up!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Mina_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5914): Near the bottom, everyone stops\npedalling. That's when I put on\pa final burst!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Mina_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5921): FUCHSIA feels close from here,\nbut ROUTE 17 still has teeth.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Mina_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5916): You came back to the lower road?\pThen don't relax yet. Race me!$

## Route17 — NICO (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route17_EventScript_Nico_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5910): Outpaced!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Nico_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5906): Running and swimming can wait.\nToday I'm working on my cycling!\pTry to match our cadence!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Nico_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5912): I keep my pedals turning evenly\nover the rough patches. A steady\peffort gets me farther.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Nico_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5908): I've ridden the downhill stretch\nagain. Let's test my team's\pfinish!$

## Route17 — ERIN (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route17_EventScript_Erin_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5864): You saved plenty!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Erin_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5855): I picked a rest spot near the\nbottom of CYCLING ROAD.\pIf you still have energy, battle\nme before ROUTE 18!$
- `Route17_EventScript_Erin_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5866): By this point, everyone starts\nthinking about FUCHSIA.\pI like making them pause one\nlast time.$
- `Route17_EventScript_Erin_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5860): Back near the end of the slope?\pGood. Let's see if you saved\nenough energy for me!$

## Route17 — DAX (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_DAX_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6011): You kept pace!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_DAX_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6007): I practise keeping my wheels on\na clean line. My POKéMON train\pfor precise moves too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_DAX_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6013): A TRIATHLETE learns fast legs are\nuseless without calm hands.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_DAX_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6009): Another downhill ride is done.\nTime to test that steady\pcadence!$

## Route17 — NINA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_NINA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6022): Fast finish!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_NINA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6016): I time every lap from CELADON\nto FUCHSIA.\pLet's see your split!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_NINA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6024): The road changes under your\nwheels.\pThat's what makes it training.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_ROUTE17_TRIATHLETE_NINA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6020): A new riding time to beat! Can\nmy team finish as strongly?$

## Route18 — JACOB (BIKER)

- `Route18_Text_JacobDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:34): Road's yours!$
- `Route18_Text_JacobIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:29): You want through to FUCHSIA?\pShow me you can handle the last\nstretch of the road.$
- `Route18_Text_JacobPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:37): The downhill rush from CELADON\nends here by FUCHSIA.\pAfter this, the pace finally\nbelongs to your feet again.$
- `Route18_Text_JacobRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:549): Passing through to FUCHSIA again?\pThen show me you still handle\nthe last stretch.$

## Route18 — RAMIRO (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route18_Text_RamiroDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:20): Your nerve held longer!$
- `Route18_Text_RamiroIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:15): Cyclists barrel down from the\nnorth, so hold your line.\pSame goes for battling.$
- `Route18_Text_RamiroPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:23): This little road catches all the\nmomentum from the slope.\pStand careless and it knocks\nyou sideways.$
- `Route18_Text_RamiroRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:544): Hold your line again.\pThe road is short, so this battle\nhas to be sharp!$

## Route18 — WILTON (BIKER)

- `Route18_Text_WiltonDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:7): Still running hot!$
- `Route18_Text_WiltonIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:1): CYCLING ROAD throws everyone\nout here with hot wheels.\pLet's see if your team cooled\ndown yet!$
- `Route18_Text_WiltonPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:10): FUCHSIA gets quiet fast, but\nROUTE 18 still has the pulse\lof CYCLING ROAD.$
- `Route18_Text_WiltonRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:538): Back where CYCLING ROAD throws\neveryone out?\pLet's see if your team is still\nrunning hot!$

## Route18 — LENA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route18_Text_LenaDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:48): You were quick too!$
- `Route18_Text_LenaIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:43): I came out from FUCHSIA to watch\nthe cyclists race by.\pA battle is even more exciting!$
- `Route18_Text_LenaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:51): The city feels close here, even\nwith the trees around us.\pYou can hear FUCHSIA in the\nquiet between bikes.$
- `Route18_Text_LenaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:84): Back from FUCHSIA already?\pGood. I wanted another battle\nwith the road rushing by!$

## Route18 — PAXTON (ROUGHNECK)

- `Route18_Text_PaxtonDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:63): Lesson learned!$
- `Route18_Text_PaxtonIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:57): A short road still teaches you\nplenty if you pay attention.\pLesson one: battle before you\nrelax.$
- `Route18_Text_PaxtonPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:66): I like training where town paths\nand open road meet.\pYou get careful trainers and\nreckless riders in one place.$
- `Route18_Text_PaxtonRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:89): Ready for lesson two?\pA short road can still teach\nyou twice.$

## Route18 — RINA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route18_Text_RinaDefeat` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:77): Momentum gone!$
- `Route18_Text_RinaIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:72): I packed light.\pROUTE 18 is all momentum,\nso you don't want loose bags!$
- `Route18_Text_RinaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:80): You can feel the whole road\npointing toward FUCHSIA.$
- `Route18_Text_RinaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route18/text.inc:94): Still packed light?\pThen you're ready for another\nmomentum battle!$

## Route19 — RICHARD (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route19_Text_RichardDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:6): Now I'm really warmed up!$
- `Route19_Text_RichardIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:1): I warm up on the FUCHSIA shore\nbefore taking on the current.\pA battle should do it!$
- `Route19_Text_RichardPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:9): ROUTE 19 looks calm from land,\nbut the water pulls hard once\lyou're out in it.$
- `Route19_Text_RichardRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:554): Back at the FUCHSIA shore?\pGood. I could use another warm-up\nbefore facing the current.$

## Route19 — TONY (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route19_Text_TonyDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:69): Caught in the undertow!$
- `Route19_Text_TonyIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:64): The sea between FUCHSIA and\nSEAFOAM is never still.\pGood. Neither am I!$
- `Route19_Text_TonyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:72): Every wave here seems to push\nyou toward SEAFOAM.\pYou have to decide for yourself\nwhether to follow it.$
- `Route19_Text_TonyRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:577): The waves are pushing south,\nand you're back for more.\pLet's see who drifts first!$

## Route19 — MATTHEW (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route19_Text_MatthewDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:32): That wake spun me round!$
- `Route19_Text_MatthewIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:26): I love swimming south from\nFUCHSIA.\pThe whole sea opens up once\nthe shore disappears.$
- `Route19_Text_MatthewPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:35): The ferry cuts a clean line\nthrough here.\pI try to stay out of its wake.$
- `Route19_Text_MatthewRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:563): The sea is open again!\pLet's battle before the ferry wake\nthrows off my rhythm.$

## Route19 — DAVID (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route19_Text_DavidDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:55): Sent drifting!$
- `Route19_Text_DavidIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:50): I tried diving near the rocks,\nbut the current shoved me back.\pBattling on the surface is safer.$
- `Route19_Text_DavidPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:58): If you want fish-shaped POKéMON,\nbring a ROD.\pIf you want trouble, just splash\naround out here.$
- `Route19_Text_DavidRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:572): Still staying on the surface?\pSmart. The rocks and currents\nare rougher than they look.$

## Route19 — ALICE (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route19_Text_AliceDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:94): Splashed out!$
- `Route19_Text_AliceIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:90): Swimming's great!\nFerry spray in your face isn't!$
- `Route19_Text_AlicePostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:97): The route is shorter by ship,\nbut swimming lets you feel the\lsea change under you.$
- `Route19_Text_AliceRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:588): Swimming's great!\pGetting sprayed by the ferry\ntwice is less great!$

## Route19 — CONNIE (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route19_Text_ConnieDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:119): Now I'm exhausted…$
- `Route19_Text_ConnieIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:114): I swam down from FUCHSIA with\nmy friends.\pI'm tired, but not done!$
- `Route19_Text_ConniePostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:122): The shore feels far away once\nyou're surrounded by waves.\pThat's why I never swim this\nroute alone.$
- `Route19_Text_ConnieRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:597): I saved enough energy for one\nmore battle.\pAfter that, I'm floating back\ntoward FUCHSIA.$

## Route19 — LIA & LUC (SWIM_SIBS)

- `Route19_Text_LiaDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:147): LIA: The sea got away from us!$
- `Route19_Text_LiaIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:141): LIA: I'm keeping an eye on LUC.\nROUTE 19's current is strong.\pBut battling together will help\nhim learn the sea!$
- `Route19_Text_LiaNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:156): LIA: We only battle together.\pDon't you have two POKéMON?$
- `Route19_Text_LiaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:150): LIA: LUC is braver when he can\nsee FUCHSIA behind us.\pNext time, maybe he'll lead us\ntoward SEAFOAM.$
- `Route19_Text_LiaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:603): LIA: LUC is reading the waves\nbetter now.\pBattle us again and you'll see!$
- `Route19_Text_LucDefeat` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:165): LUC: We got swept back!$
- `Route19_Text_LucIntro` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:160): LUC: My big sis says the current\nis easier if we move together.\pOur POKéMON do that too!$
- `Route19_Text_LucNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:174): LUC: I battle with LIA!\pDon't you have two POKéMON?$
- `Route19_Text_LucPostBattle` (data/maps/Route19/text.inc:168): LUC: I want to swim all the way\nto SEAFOAM someday.\pLIA says I have to read the\nwaves first.$
- `Route19_Text_LucRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:608): LUC: I watched the current like\nLIA told me!\pNow I can battle better too!$

## Route2 — GARRET (BUG_CATCHER)

- `Route2_Text_GarretDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:133): You spotted my strategy!$
- `Route2_Text_GarretIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:123): BUG POKéMON drift out from\nVIRIDIAN FOREST onto this road.\pI watch the grass for movement!$
- `Route2_Text_GarretPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:136): ROUTE 2 is perfect for bug study.\pForest shade on one side,\nopen grass on the other.$
- `Route2_Text_GarretRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:128): I've watched the grass since our\nlast battle.\pThis time, my bugs will move first!$

## Route2 — JONAH (BUG_CATCHER)

- `Route2_Text_JonahDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:147): My bugs lost their footing!$
- `Route2_Text_JonahIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:141): The forest entrance is close. If\nyou can handle my bugs, you can\phandle the path ahead!$
- `Route2_Text_JonahPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:150): The trees make ROUTE 2 feel calm,\nbut the grass is always busy.$
- `Route2_Text_JonahRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:144): You've crossed beneath those\ntrees before. Let's see if my\pbugs can still slow you down!$

## Route2 — SHANE (SCOUT_M)

- `Route2_Text_ShaneDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:164): You know the trail well!$
- `Route2_Text_ShaneIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:154): I set camp where the road bends\nbetween VIRIDIAN and PEWTER.\pEvery traveler passes this shade!$
- `Route2_Text_ShanePostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:167): A shaded route keeps your team\nfresh.\pThat's why I like camping here.$
- `Route2_Text_ShaneRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:159): Back through ROUTE 2?\pThen let's see what the forest road\nhas taught you!$

## Route2 — NANCY (SCOUT_F)

- `Route2_Text_NancyDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:182): That was a lively picnic!$
- `Route2_Text_NancyIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:172): I picked this spot for lunch.\pYou can hear the forest without\nstepping under the trees.$
- `Route2_Text_NancyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:185): ROUTE 2 is long, but the forest\nbreaks it into little resting spots.$
- `Route2_Text_NancyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:177): I've had another picnic and another\ntraining session.\pReady for both?$

## Route2 — ORIN (BUG_CATCHER)

- `Route2_Text_OrinDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:200): They cooled off too fast!$
- `Route2_Text_OrinIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:189): The best bugs are just outside\nthe forest, where they warm up\lin the grass!$
- `Route2_Text_OrinPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:203): Morning light hits the route before\nit reaches the deep forest.\pBUG POKéMON notice that.$
- `Route2_Text_OrinRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:194): The sun warmed up my whole team\ntoday.\pReady for another forest-edge\nbattle?$

## Route2 — ROWAN (SCOUT_M)

- `Route2_Text_RowanDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:218): You kept your bearings!$
- `Route2_Text_RowanIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:208): A good forest route gives you\ncover, quiet, and a clear path.\pLet me test your trail sense!$
- `Route2_Text_RowanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:221): Keep one eye on the grass and one\neye on the treeline.\pThat's forest-route travel.$
- `Route2_Text_RowanRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:213): You found the path once already.\pNow let's see if you can read\nthe trail under pressure!$

## Route2 — LYRA (SCOUT_F)

- `Route2_Text_LyraDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:236): I need a snack break!$
- `Route2_Text_LyraIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:226): I brought snacks for the forest\nedge.\pBattles make them taste better!$
- `Route2_Text_LyraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:239): The forest entrance is a good place\nto rest before heading north.$
- `Route2_Text_LyraRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:231): I packed extra snacks this time.\pA rematch on ROUTE 2 deserves\nproper supplies!$
