## Route2 — MILA (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `Route2_Text_MiloDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:253): That's worth recording!$
- `Route2_Text_MiloIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:243): POKéMON behave differently where\nwoods meet open road.\pI'm studying that boundary.$
- `Route2_Text_MiloPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:256): Forest POKéMON are less shy near\nROUTE 2 once they know the path is\lsafe.$
- `Route2_Text_MiloRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:248): Your team changed my notes on\nforest-edge behavior.\pLet's gather another sample!$

## Route2 — ELLIS (BUG_MANIAC)

- `Route2_Text_EllisDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:271): My secret spot failed me!$
- `Route2_Text_EllisIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:261): Inside the forest is obvious.\pThe real bug secrets are here,\nright at the edge!$
- `Route2_Text_EllisPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:274): Bugs cross from bark to grass and\nback again.\pThat's why I watch the edge.$
- `Route2_Text_EllisRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:266): I found a new secret at the edge.\pThis one is called: don't lose\nthe rematch!$

## Route2 — IVY (POKÉMON_RANGER_F)

- `Route2_Text_IvyDefeat` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:290): You travel with care.$
- `Route2_Text_IvyIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:279): I patrol ROUTE 2 because it links\nVIRIDIAN, PEWTER, and the forest.\pShow me you can travel safely!$
- `Route2_Text_IvyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:293): A route this useful needs looking\nafter.\pForest paths can change quickly.$
- `Route2_Text_IvyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route2/text.inc:284): Good to see you travelling ROUTE 2\nagain.\pShow me your team is still ready\nfor changing paths!$

## Route20 — MELISSA (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route20_Text_MelissaDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:157): Too cold!$
- `Route20_Text_MelissaIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:152): CINNABAR's volcano warms the sea\nwest of here.\pBut around SEAFOAM? Brrr!$
- `Route20_Text_MelissaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:160): CINNABAR and SEAFOAM feel like\nopposites on one route.\pWarm rock to the west, cold cave\nwater in the middle.$
- `Route20_Text_MelissaRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:661): Warm CINNABAR, cold SEAFOAM,\nand one more battle between them!$

## Route20 — NORA (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route20_Text_NoraDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:130): Lost the shine!$
- `Route20_Text_NoraIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:125): The water here polishes stones\nand shells until they shine.\pMy POKéMON battle just as bright!$
- `Route20_Text_NoraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:133): CLOYSTER like the cold currents\naround SEAFOAM.\pNo wonder pearls feel at home\nnear this island.$
- `Route20_Text_NoraRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:651): The water's still shining today.\pLet's see if my battle can shine\nbrighter than last time!$

## Route20 — DEAN (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route20_Text_DeanDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:74): Form broke!$
- `Route20_Text_DeanIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:69): Check out my open-water form!\pNo pool lane can teach you what\nRoute 20 does.$
- `Route20_Text_DeanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:77): I should train my POKéMON to\nhandle cross-currents too.$
- `Route20_Text_DeanRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:636): My open-water form is sharper\nthan before.\pRoute 20 keeps improving me!$

## Route20 — BARRY (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route20_Text_BarryDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:7): Splashed flat!$
- `Route20_Text_BarryIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:1): The water gets restless around\nSEAFOAM.\pIf you can battle here, you can\nkeep steady anywhere!$
- `Route20_Text_BarryPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:10): The channel runs from CINNABAR\nto the open water by FUCHSIA.\pThat's a lot of sea to cross.$
- `Route20_Text_BarryRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:613): Back in SEAFOAM water?\pGood. Let's see if you can keep\nsteady twice!$

## Route20 — SHIRLEY (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route20_Text_ShirleyDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:20): Current got me!$
- `Route20_Text_ShirleyIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:15): SEAFOAM looks peaceful until\nyou try swimming around it.\pThe current has teeth!$
- `Route20_Text_ShirleyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:23): There are caves under SEAFOAM.\pYou can feel the cold water\nmoving through them.$
- `Route20_Text_ShirleyRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:618): The current still has teeth.\pSo do I!$

## Route20 — TYLER (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route20_Text_TylerDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:33): Drifted too far!$
- `Route20_Text_TylerIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:28): I like floating where the waves\nsplit around SEAFOAM.\pBattle me before I drift off!$
- `Route20_Text_TylerPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:36): Fish POKéMON gather where the\ncurrents slow.\pEverything else has to work to\nhold its place.$
- `Route20_Text_TylerRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:622): Back around SEAFOAM?\pLet's see if I can hold my line\nbetter this time!$

## Route20 — DARRIN (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route20_Text_DarrinDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:86): Ouch!\nTorpedoed!$
- `Route20_Text_DarrinIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:81): The water near SEAFOAM bites back.\nThat's why I like it!\pLet's see if you bite back too.$
- `Route20_Text_DarrinPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:90): If you can hold a course here,\nyou can swim almost anywhere.$
- `Route20_Text_DarrinRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:641): The water near SEAFOAM still\nbites back.\pSo let's bite back harder!$

## Route20 — ROGER (SAILOR)

- `Route20_Text_RogerDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:99): Overboard!$
- `Route20_Text_RogerIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:94): I keep watch where the ferry\nroute meets the swimmers.\pThat means I watch battles too!$
- `Route20_Text_RogerPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:102): SEAFOAM is a landmark, but the\nwater around it is the real map.$
- `Route20_Text_RogerRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:646): The ferry lane is clear.\pThat leaves plenty of room for\nour rematch!$

## Route20 — MISSY (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route20_Text_MissyDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:145): Missed the turn!$
- `Route20_Text_MissyIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:139): Swim, bike, run.\pThis channel is my open-water\ntraining course, and SEAFOAM is\nmy turn marker!$
- `Route20_Text_MissyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:148): SEAFOAM makes a perfect turning\npoint for a triathlon route.$
- `Route20_Text_MissyRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:656): One lap around SEAFOAM wasn't\nenough.\pA rematch is part of training!$

## Route20 — MARINER (SAILOR)

- `Route20_Text_MarinerDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:111): Agh!$
- `Route20_Text_MarinerIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:106): I heard SEAFOAM got fractured\nwhen the cold arrived all at\lonce and reshaped the caves.$
- `Route20_Text_MarinerPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:114): You broke me into pieces too!\pSEAFOAM was split by a sudden\nfreeze. The caves still hold\lthat cold.\pThe two entrances meet in the\ncavern below. A deeper stairway\lleads to an icy lake.\pTake your CASCADE BOARD if you\nwant to cross that water.$
- `Route20_Text_MarinerRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:665): Currents change before you notice.\pLet's see if your battle course\nhas changed too!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## Route20 — LUIS (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route20_Text_LuisDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:47): The cold got into my strategy!$
- `Route20_Text_LuisIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:42): SEAFOAM's cold current keeps\ntrying to push me back.\pA battle ought to warm us up!$
- `Route20_Text_LuisPostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:50): The current here is rough, but\nit teaches you how to keep your\nline in open water.$
- `Route20_Text_LuisRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:627): Back in the cold current?\pGood. I could use another\nwarm-up battle!$

## Route20 — IRENE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route20_Text_IreneDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:60): I missed my stroke!$
- `Route20_Text_IreneIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:55): The sea is rough enough to make\nevery stroke count.\pThat's perfect training for me!$
- `Route20_Text_IrenePostBattle` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:63): A triathlon route around SEAFOAM\ndoesn't forgive lazy turns.\pThe island tells you exactly\nwhere your stamina ends.$
- `Route20_Text_IreneRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:632): SEAFOAM is still my turn marker.\pTry to keep pace this time!$

## Route20 — AMIRA (TUBER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_AMIRA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5487): Splashed again!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_AMIRA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5479): The grown-ups swim far out,\nbut I like the SEAFOAM shore.\pIt's still my battle spot!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_AMIRA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5489): The water is colder here than\nnear FUCHSIA.\pI can feel SEAFOAM breathing\nunder the waves.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_AMIRA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5483): Back near SEAFOAM?\pThen my shore training gets\nanother test!$

## Route20 — NOAH (TUBER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_NOAH_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5501): My timing slipped!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_NOAH_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5494): I counted the ferry waves!\pIf I can time them, I can time\na battle too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_NOAH_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5503): Big swimmers go all the way to\nCINNABAR.\pI'm practising one wave at a\ntime.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_NOAH_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5498): The ferry made more waves!\pLet's battle before they settle.$

## Route20 — NIA & FINN (SWIM_SIBS)

- `Route20_Text_GorebyssTradeDone` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:218): NIA: How is GOREBYSS doing?\pIt's amazing what a simple\ntrade can do for CLAMPERL!$
- `Route20_Text_GorebyssTradeIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:206): NIA: You beat our team!\pI have another CLAMPERL holding\na DEEP SEA SCALE.\pTrade me a CLAMPERL, and mine\nwill become GOREBYSS for you!$
- `Route20_Text_GorebyssTradeThanks` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:213): NIA: There it is! GOREBYSS!\pThanks for the CLAMPERL.\nI'll take good care of it!$
- `Route20_Text_HuntailTradeDone` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:235): FINN: How is HUNTAIL doing?\pIt's amazing what a simple\ntrade can do for CLAMPERL!$
- `Route20_Text_HuntailTradeIntro` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:223): FINN: You beat our team!\pI have another CLAMPERL holding\na DEEP SEA TOOTH.\pTrade me a CLAMPERL, and mine\nwill become HUNTAIL for you!$
- `Route20_Text_HuntailTradeThanks` (data/maps/Route20/text.inc:230): FINN: There it is! HUNTAIL!\pThanks for the CLAMPERL.\nI'll take good care of it!$
- `Route20_Text_NiaFinnDefeat` (data/maps/Route20/scripts.inc:90): Our two paths met their match!$
- `Route20_Text_NiaFinnIntro` (data/maps/Route20/scripts.inc:87): Our CLAMPERL took different paths.\nSee how they battle together!$
- `Route20_Text_NiaFinnNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route20/scripts.inc:96): Bring two ready POKéMON\nso we can battle as a pair!$
- `Trade_Text_DoYouHaveMonWantToTradeForMon` (data/text/ingame_trade.inc:42): Hi!\nDo you have a {STR_VAR_1}?\pWant to trade it for my\n{STR_VAR_2}?$
- `Trade_Text_ThatsTooBad` (data/text/ingame_trade.inc:48): That's too bad.$
- `Trade_Text_ThisIsNoMon` (data/text/ingame_trade.inc:51): …This is no {STR_VAR_1}.\pIf you get one, trade it with me.$

## Route21_North — RONALD (FISHERMAN)

- `Route21_North_Text_RonaldDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:6): Line snapped!$
- `Route21_North_Text_RonaldIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:1): Fish gather where PALLET's quiet\nwater starts to deepen.\pKeep still, or battle me!$
- `Route21_North_Text_RonaldPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:9): Most people rush south toward\nCINNABAR.\pI like the north end. It rewards\npatience.$
- `Route21_North_Text_RonaldRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:670): The north water is quiet again.\pLet's see if the battle bites\nbefore the fish do!$

## Route21_North — WADE (FISHERMAN)

- `Route21_North_Text_WadeDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:20): My timing missed the wake!$
- `Route21_North_Text_WadeIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:15): The ferry stirs up the water\nwhen it leaves PALLET.\pThat's when I cast my line!$
- `Route21_North_Text_WadePostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:23): A little wake can bring fish up\nfrom deeper water.\pThe trick is knowing when the\nsea is about to move.$
- `Route21_North_Text_WadeRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:675): The ferry wake brought the fish\nup again.\pMaybe it brought you back for a\nrematch too!$

## Route21_North — SPENCER (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route21_North_Text_SpencerDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:34): Swept south!$
- `Route21_North_Text_SpencerIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:29): ROUTE 21 starts gentle, then\nkeeps pulling south.\pI'll match that current!$
- `Route21_North_Text_SpencerPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:37): From here you can still imagine\nPALLET behind you.\pA few strokes later, it's only\nyou and the water.$
- `Route21_North_Text_SpencerRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:681): Still swimming south from PALLET?\pGood. Let's see if the current\nmade you stronger!$

## Route21_North — ANYA (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route21_North_Text_AnyaDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:75): The tide turned!$
- `Route21_North_Text_AnyaIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:64): This stretch links PALLET's quiet\nshore to the open southern sea.\pLet's see how you handle the\nfirst pull of the route.$
- `Route21_North_Text_AnyaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:78): The north water feels gentler,\nbut it still leads to CINNABAR.\pEvery current here knows where\nit's going.$
- `Route21_North_Text_AnyaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:70): Back on ROUTE 21?\pLet's see if the current has made\nyou stronger!$

## Route21_North — LIL & IAN (SWIM_SIBS)

- `Route21_North_Text_IanDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:227): IAN: We got pulled apart!$
- `Route21_North_Text_IanIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:222): IAN: LIL keeps us steady when\nthe route starts pulling south.\pI'll show you how we battle!$
- `Route21_North_Text_IanNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:235): IAN: LIL and I battle together.\pBring two POKéMON before you\nchallenge us, okay?$
- `Route21_North_Text_IanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:230): IAN: PALLET feels far away once\nyou're in the middle of Route 21.\pThat's why we practise together.$
- `Route21_North_Text_IanRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:722): IAN: LIL says we're steadier now.\pI want to prove it in a rematch!$
- `Route21_North_Text_LilDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:211): LIL: The current won that one!$
- `Route21_North_Text_LilIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:205): LIL: We're practising the swim\nfrom PALLET.\pIAN says we have to battle as a\nteam too.$
- `Route21_North_Text_LilNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:218): LIL: We only battle together.\pBring two POKéMON, won't you?$
- `Route21_North_Text_LilPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:214): LIL: The sea feels less scary\nwhen IAN swims beside me.$
- `Route21_North_Text_LilRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:717): LIL: IAN and I practised staying\ntogether in the current.\pLet's try another battle!$

## Route21_North — ALEXIS (TUBER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_ALEXIS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5472): You tipped my float!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_ALEXIS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5463): I came down from PALLET to play\nwhere the water starts getting\ldeep.\pI'm not scared of ROUTE 21!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_ALEXIS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5474): The grown-ups swim farther south,\nbut I like staying near PALLET.\pThis part of ROUTE 21 is big\nenough for me.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_ALEXIS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5468): Back where PALLET's beach turns\ninto a real sea route?\pThen I want another battle!$

## Route21_North — CLAUDE (FISHERMAN)

- `Route21_South_Text_ClaudeDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:154): There goes the bite!$
- `Route21_South_Text_ClaudeIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:149): Hey, don't scare away the fish!\pThe CINNABAR current makes them\nbite in strange patterns.$
- `Route21_South_Text_ClaudePostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:157): The water rolls differently near\nCINNABAR.\pFish know it. Fishermen are still\ntrying to learn.$
- `Route21_South_Text_ClaudeRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:706): The CINNABAR current is strange\ntoday.\pIf the fish won't bite, maybe\nyou'll battle!$

## Route21_North — NOLAN (FISHERMAN)

- `Route21_South_Text_NolanDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:168): Hook slipped!$
- `Route21_South_Text_NolanIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:163): Keep me company till I get a hit.\pOr better yet, battle me while\nthe line is quiet.$
- `Route21_South_Text_NolanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:171): Oh wait!\nI got a bite! Yeah!\pRoute 21 makes you wait, but it\ndoesn't forget you.$
- `Route21_South_Text_NolanRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:712): Still waiting on a big catch.\pA rematch will keep the line from\nfeeling too quiet.$

## Route21_North — JACK (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route21_South_Text_JackDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:114): Dragged under!$
- `Route21_South_Text_JackIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:109): I caught my POKéMON in the long\nwater north of CINNABAR.\pThey know this route's rhythm!$
- `Route21_South_Text_JackPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:117): Route 21 changes as you go south.\pThe water near CINNABAR feels\nwarmer and heavier.$
- `Route21_South_Text_JackRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:691): My POKéMON know this whole\nstretch north of CINNABAR now.\pReady to test them again?$

## Route21_North — NICOLE (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route21_South_Text_NicoleDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:187): I lost my rhythm in the current.$
- `Route21_South_Text_NicoleIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:177): CINNABAR's warm water meets the\ncooler sea here.\pThat's my favorite training spot,\nand my favorite battle spot!$
- `Route21_South_Text_NicolePostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:190): The island changes the current.\nYou can feel it before you see it.\pThat's how you know CINNABAR is\nclose.$
- `Route21_South_Text_NicoleRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:183): The water's different every time.\pSo is my battling!$

## Route21_North — JEROME (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route21_South_Text_JeromeDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:127): Lost my pace!$
- `Route21_South_Text_JeromeIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:122): I'm timing my swim from PALLET\nall the way to CINNABAR.\pBattle me without breaking pace!$
- `Route21_South_Text_JeromePostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:130): This route is long enough to\nturn confidence into fatigue.\pThat's why I train here.$
- `Route21_South_Text_JeromeRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:696): PALLET to CINNABAR is a long\ntraining line.\pA rematch keeps the pace honest!$

## Route21_North — ROLAND (SWIMMER_M)

- `Route21_South_Text_RolandDefeat` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:140): The breeze dropped!$
- `Route21_South_Text_RolandIntro` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:135): Feel that sea breeze?\pCINNABAR is close enough to\nchange the air!$
- `Route21_South_Text_RolandPostBattle` (data/maps/Route21_North/text.inc:143): The sun hits different near\nvolcanic rock.\pNo wonder swimmers slow down\nbefore CINNABAR.$
- `Route21_South_Text_RolandRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:701): CINNABAR's breeze is blowing\nstrong again.\pLet's ride it into a rematch!$

## Route22 — JEFF (SCOUT_M)

- `Route22_Text_JeffDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:16): Lost my footing!$
- `Route22_Text_JeffIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:5): I camp on ROUTE 22 because the\nroad feels wilder than VIRIDIAN.\pLet's see how you handle rough\nground!$
- `Route22_Text_JeffPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:19): This route is the first place west\nof VIRIDIAN that feels properly\lrugged.\pThat's why SCOUTS learn a lot\nhere.$
- `Route22_Text_JeffRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:11): Back from the city road?\pGood. ROUTE 22 should test your\nfooting again!$

## Route22 — ALICIA (SCOUT_F)

- `Route22_Text_AliciaDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:37): You noticed my opening!$
- `Route22_Text_AliciaIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:26): The LEAGUE gate makes this road\nfeel serious.\pA SCOUT still has to notice the\nsmall paths too!$
- `Route22_Text_AliciaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:40): People stare north toward the\nLEAGUE, but ROUTE 22 spreads out\lbefore that.\pThere's always more than one way\nto cross rough terrain.$
- `Route22_Text_AliciaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:32): I checked the rocks, grass, and\ngate road again.\pNow I want to check your team!$

## Route22 — JAREN (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route22_Text_JarenDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:58): I took that corner too sharply!$
- `Route22_Text_JarenIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:47): This broken-up road is perfect\nfor hard turns.\pLet's see if your battle style\ncan corner like that!$
- `Route22_Text_JarenPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:61): Flat roads make lazy riders.\pROUTE 22 keeps your hands tight\nand your eyes open.$
- `Route22_Text_JarenRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:53): I know every bend between\nVIRIDIAN and the LEAGUE gate.\pTry keeping up again!$

## Route22 — ROBERT (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route22_Text_RobertDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:76): My wings dipped!$
- `Route22_Text_RobertIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:66): Birds ride the wind coming down\nfrom the LEAGUE cliffs.\pMy team trains in that air!$
- `Route22_Text_RobertPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:79): The closer you get to the LEAGUE,\nthe stronger the wind feels.\pBird POKéMON love a road that\nmakes them work.$
- `Route22_Text_RobertRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:71): The wind changed since last time.\pLet's see if our battle changes\nwith it!$

## Route22 — JEREMY (HIKER)

- `Route22_Text_JeremyDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:95): Rolled downhill!$
- `Route22_Text_JeremyIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:85): You can feel the land rising\ntoward the LEAGUE gate.\pA HIKER notices every slope!$
- `Route22_Text_JeremyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:98): ROUTE 22 is rough, but not wild\nwithout purpose.\pIt prepares your legs before the\nLEAGUE road gets serious.$
- `Route22_Text_JeremyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:90): Back on the west road?\pThen let's climb into another\nbattle!$

## Route22 — FREYA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route22_Text_TriathleteFLandDefeat` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:114): I need to pace myself!$
- `Route22_Text_TriathleteFLandIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:104): I run from VIRIDIAN to the\nLEAGUE gate and back.\pRough terrain builds endurance!$
- `Route22_Text_TriathleteFLandPostBattle` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:117): Strong legs, steady breath,\nand a good partner POKéMON.\pThat's how you cross rough\nterrain.$
- `Route22_Text_TriathleteFLandRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route22/text.inc:109): Another run, another rematch.\pLet's see whose pace holds on\nROUTE 22!$

## Route23 — WAYNE (HIKER)

- `Route23_Text_WayneDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:16): Your footing held.$
- `Route23_Text_WayneIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:6): Every step north of here weighs\nmore than the last.\pShow me your legs can carry your\nBADGES.$
- `Route23_Text_WaynePostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:19): ROUTE 23 is not here to hurry you.\pIt gives you time to understand\nwhat you have earned.$
- `Route23_Text_WayneRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:12): Back on the final road?\pThen climb with purpose again.$

## Route23 — ORVILLE (BIRD_KEEPER)

- `Route23_Text_ChesterDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:34): The silence won.$
- `Route23_Text_ChesterIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:24): Birds go quiet near VICTORY ROAD.\pEven they know this place asks\nfor respect.$
- `Route23_Text_ChesterPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:37): A loud trainer stands out here.\pA prepared trainer listens first.$
- `Route23_Text_ChesterRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:29): The air is still again.\pMy birds are ready to test your\nresolve.$

## Route23 — ERNEST (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `Route23_Text_ErnestDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:51): You meant it.$
- `Route23_Text_ErnestIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:41): No showing off on this road.\pIf you battle on ROUTE 23, battle\nlike you mean it.$
- `Route23_Text_ErnestPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:54): The LEAGUE gate changes people.\pEven rough trainers learn to keep\ntheir voice down here.$
- `Route23_Text_ErnestRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:46): You came back with the same steady\nlook.\pGood. Let's battle again.$

## Route23 — ZEEK (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `Route23_Text_ZeekDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:69): Focus held.$
- `Route23_Text_ZeekIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:59): The road narrows your thoughts\nuntil only the next step matters.\pBattle me with that focus.$
- `Route23_Text_ZeekPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:72): I used to think strength was all\nnoise.\pROUTE 23 taught me it can be\nquiet too.$
- `Route23_Text_ZeekRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:64): Same road. Same pressure.\pLet's see if your focus still\nholds.$

## Route23 — COREY (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `Route23_Text_CoreyDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:87): You belong.$
- `Route23_Text_CoreyIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:78): People pass the guards and start\nwalking like champions already.\pThis road checks that attitude.$
- `Route23_Text_CoreyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:90): The BADGES open the path.\pThey don't carry you up it.\nThat's still on you.$
- `Route23_Text_CoreyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:83): Still walking like you belong?\pGood. Prove it again.$

## Route23 — MALIK (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `Route23_Text_MalikDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:105): Nothing left but respect.$
- `Route23_Text_MalikIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:95): The last stretch before VICTORY\nROAD strips away excuses.\pLet's see what's left.$
- `Route23_Text_MalikPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:108): If you can keep moving after every\nguard, every climb, every battle...\pthen VICTORY ROAD will know your\nname.$
- `Route23_Text_MalikRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route23/text.inc:100): No excuses this time either.\pJust the road, your POKéMON,\nand mine.$

## Route23 — EVAN & ZOE (ACES)

- `Route23_Text_EvanZoeDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:224): We'll take that lesson to the\nLEAGUE!$
- `Route23_Text_EvanZoeIntro` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:221): We are training for the LEAGUE.\nCan you handle both of us?$
- `Route23_Text_EvanZoeNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:230): Bring two ready POKéMON\nso we can battle as a pair!$
- `Route23_Text_EvanZoePostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:227): A good partner spots things\nyou might miss on your own.$

## Route23 — NORA (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFNoraDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:248): A lesson worth learning!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFNoraIntro` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:245): The LEAGUE is our goal.\nEvery battle brings us closer!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFNoraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:251): I keep track of every loss.\nThere is always more to learn.$

## Route23 — VERA (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFVeraDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:269): You found a way through!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFVeraIntro` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:266): A strong team needs variety.\nLet me show you what I mean!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFVeraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:272): Different strengths make\na team worth relying on.$

## Route23 — LENA (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFLenaDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:290): That was a proper challenge!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFLenaIntro` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:287): I train for tough opponents.\nYou look like a good test!$
- `Route23_Text_AceTrainerFLenaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:293): A challenge shows you where\nyour training needs more work.$

## Route23 — TERRY (RIVAL_LATE)

- `Route23_Text_RivalConversation` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:340): {RIVAL}: All eight BADGES, huh?\nLet me see those!\pEvery GYM taught us something.\nSometimes after a few losses!\pI used to just shout orders.\nNow I pay attention to my team.\pRemember SLICK, my AIPOM?\nWe trained in FUCHSIA FOREST.\pOne level with DOUBLE HIT, and\nSLICK became an APEX AMBIPOM!\pHOTSHOT got a METAL COAT from\nthe lab on CINNABAR ISLAND.\pOne trade later, my SCYTHER\ncame back as SCIZOR!\pBut you know I'm still aiming\nfor the top, {PLAYER}!\pLet's see what you and your\nPOKéMON learned. Here we go!$
- `Route23_Text_RivalDefeat` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:360): You've earned those BADGES!$
- `Route23_Text_RivalPostBattle` (data/maps/Route23/scripts.inc:363): {RIVAL}: You trusted your team\nto pull that off. I could tell.\pWe've come a long way since\nthat first battle at the lab.\pGet through VICTORY ROAD.\nI'll see you at the LEAGUE!$

## Route24 — CALE (FISHERMAN)

- `Route24_Text_CaleBridgeRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:89): Back for another run across\nNUGGET BRIDGE?\pGood. I'll test your rhythm\nagain.$
- `Route24_Text_CaleDefeat` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:95): Clean catch!$
- `Route24_Text_CaleIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:82): Welcome to NUGGET BRIDGE!\pFive TRAINERS stand between you\nand the prize at the end.\pI'll cast the first line.\nReady?$
- `Route24_Text_CalePostBattle` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:98): The bridge challenge starts calm,\nbut each step north adds pressure.\pKeep moving and don't let the\nwater distract you.$

## Route24 — NADIA (TUBER_F)

- `Route24_Text_NadiaDefeat` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:115): Still nervous!$
- `Route24_Text_NadiaIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:104): The bridge looks huge when you're\nsmall.\pThat's why I battle here. It makes\nme feel brave!$
- `Route24_Text_NadiaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:118): CERULEAN is right there, but this\nbridge feels like its own place.\pEverybody gets a little tougher\nby the far side.$
- `Route24_Text_NadiaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:110): I crossed the bridge once already!\pNow I can battle you without\ngetting nervous.$

## Route24 — INEZ (SCOUT_F)

- `Route24_Text_KindraDefeat` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:136): Bridge checked!$
- `Route24_Text_KindraIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:124): A good SCOUT checks every plank\nbefore crossing.\pA great SCOUT checks every\nTRAINER too!$
- `Route24_Text_KindraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:139): ROUTE 24 links CERULEAN to the\npaths beyond the cape.\pIt is short, but everyone remembers\ncrossing it.$
- `Route24_Text_KindraRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:130): I've checked the whole bridge\nagain.\pYou're still the most interesting\npart of it!$

## Route24 — BECKY (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route24_Text_BeckyDefeat` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:154): You beat my bridge time!$
- `Route24_Text_BeckyIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:145): I run bridge laps when the water\nis bright.\pA quick battle keeps my pace sharp!$
- `Route24_Text_BeckyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:157): The bridge is perfect training.\pFlat planks, sea air, and just\nenough room to sprint.$
- `Route24_Text_BeckyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:150): Another lap, another battle.\pLet's see whose pace holds longer!$

## Route24 — NELL (SWIMMER_F)

- `Route24_Text_NellDefeat` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:173): That made a splash!$
- `Route24_Text_NellIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:162): I swim beneath the bridge and hear\nevery battle overhead.\pLet's see if yours sounds strong\nup close!$
- `Route24_Text_NellPostBattle` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:176): NUGGET BRIDGE belongs to the\nTRAINERS above and the water below.\pYou handled both sides well.$
- `Route24_Text_NellRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route24/text.inc:168): The water carried your footsteps\nback to me.\pThat means rematch time!$

## Route25 — TESS (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `Route25_Text_TessDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:207): Healthy growth!$
- `Route25_Text_TessIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:196): The cape has open air, soft grass,\nand plenty of passing TRAINERS.\pThat's excellent social training\nfor young POKéMON.$
- `Route25_Text_TessPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:210): A busy route can be gentle too.\pROUTE 25 teaches POKéMON to stay\ncalm around strangers.$
- `Route25_Text_TessRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:202): My POKéMON know the cape path\nbetter now.\pLet's show you how they've grown!$

## Route25 — MIRA (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `Route25_Text_MiraDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:224): Wide awake now!$
- `Route25_Text_MiraIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:215): I bring my POKéMON here because\nthe bridge breeze keeps them alert.\pLet's see if yours are alert too!$
- `Route25_Text_MiraPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:227): ROUTE 25 is good for confidence.\pEvery battle here feels like a\nstep toward the cape.$
- `Route25_Text_MiraRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:220): The breeze is back, and so are you.\pPerfect conditions for a rematch!$

## Route25 — TOMMY (FISHERMAN)

- `Route25_Text_FishermanTommyDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:138): The current got me!$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTommyIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:130): A bridge over good water?\nThat's an invitation to cast!$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTommyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:141): Fish gather where the shore changes\nshape.\pA sandy bank like this is worth\nchecking twice.$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTommyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:134): Back for another cast?\nLet's see what bites!$

## Route25 — TYLOR (FISHERMAN)

- `Route25_Text_FishermanTylorDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:155): The tide favoured you!$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTylorIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:147): The sea breeze reaches all the way\nup here now.$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTylorPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:158): This bridge turned my quiet spot\ninto a proper route.\pCan't complain when the bites are\nthis good.$
- `Route25_Text_FishermanTylorRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:151): The tide's turned.\nThis one's mine!$

## Route25 — MILES (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route25_Text_TriathleteMLandDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:173): You won that interval!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteMLandIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:164): This bridge is perfect for interval\ntraining! Keep up!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteMLandPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:176): The best part of a long run is\nfeeling the wind over the water.$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteMLandRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:168): Back on the long bridge?\pGood. I've been timing my laps\nfor this rematch!$

## Route25 — RINA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route25_Text_TriathleteFLandDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:189): You found another gear!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteFLandIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:180): No slowing down on the bridge!\nBattle at full stride!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteFLandPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:192): A straight path, sea air, and a\ngood battle. That's training.$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteFLandRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:184): You made it back across the span!\pLet's see if you still have speed\nleft for me.$

## Route25 — DALIA (AROMA_LADY)

- `Route25_Text_DaliaDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:243): Scattered petals!$
- `Route25_Text_DaliaIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:232): The flowers here lean toward the\nsea breeze.\pEven plants seem curious about\nwhere this route leads.$
- `Route25_Text_DaliaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:246): A cape garden grows differently.\pSalt air, bright water, and steady\nfootsteps all leave a mark.$
- `Route25_Text_DaliaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:238): The flowers are leaning again.\pThey must have noticed you coming\nback for a rematch.$

## Route25 — JOANA (AROMA_LADY)

- `Route25_Text_JoanaDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:262): Lovely and strong!$
- `Route25_Text_JoanaIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:251): I came for the flowers, but stayed\nfor the view.\pA battle with the cape behind us\nsounds lovely!$
- `Route25_Text_JoanaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:265): From here you can feel CERULEAN\nbehind you and the open routes\nahead.$
- `Route25_Text_JoanaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:257): The view is lovely again.\pLet's make the battle just as\nmemorable.$

## Route25 — FLINT (FISHERMAN)

- `Route25_Text_FlintBridgeRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:30): The current shifted again.\pLet's see if your battle rhythm\nshifted too!$
- `Route25_Text_FlintDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:35): That cast went wide!$
- `Route25_Text_FlintIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:25): The cape water changes depth fast.\pA FISHERMAN learns to read those\nshifts before casting.$
- `Route25_Text_FlintPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:38): ROUTE 25 looks peaceful, but the\nwater keeps changing under it.\pThat's why the fishing stays good.$

## Route25 — DANA (FISHERMAN)

- `Route25_Text_DanaDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:281): You saw through it!$
- `Route25_Text_DanaIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:270): The posts under this bridge give\nfish places to hide.\pA smart FISHERMAN checks every\nshadow.$
- `Route25_Text_DanaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:284): People look across bridges.\nFISHERMEN look underneath them.$
- `Route25_Text_DanaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:276): Same bridge, new shadows.\pLet's see what kind of battle\nhides here today.$

## Route25 — JUSTIN (SCOUT_M)

- `Route25_Text_JustinDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:298): You read the path!$
- `Route25_Text_JustinIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:288): A SCOUT should know when a short\npath becomes a long journey.\pThis bridge did that to ROUTE 25.$
- `Route25_Text_JustinPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:301): ROUTE 25 used to feel like an end.\pNow it feels like a crossing.$
- `Route25_Text_JustinRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:293): Still checking the long route?\pGood. A SCOUT respects a path\nthat changed.$

## Route25 — GWEN (SCOUT_F)

- `Route25_Text_GwenDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:315): Well rested!$
- `Route25_Text_GwenIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:305): I mark rest spots for TRAINERS\ncrossing from CERULEAN to ROUTE 10.\pThis one is also good for battles!$
- `Route25_Text_GwenPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:318): Long connections need quiet places\nwhere TRAINERS can reset their\npace.$
- `Route25_Text_GwenRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:310): You found my rest spot again.\pThat means you're ready for\nanother battle.$

## Route25 — VALERIE (SCOUT_F)

- `Route25_Text_ValerieDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:333): No surprise there!$
- `Route25_Text_ValerieIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:323): The cape path twists just enough\nto hide what comes next.\pA SCOUT learns to enjoy that.$
- `Route25_Text_ValeriePostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:336): A route with turns invites you to\nslow down and look closely.$
- `Route25_Text_ValerieRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:328): You came back around the twist.\pLet's see what changed since your\nlast pass.$

## Route25 — RILEY (SCOUT_M)

- `Route25_Text_RileyDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:351): You didn't miss a step!$
- `Route25_Text_RileyIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:340): I patrol the grass near the bridge\nbecause people rush through it.\pRush past me and you'll miss the\nbattle!$
- `Route25_Text_RileyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:354): Fast routes still have details.\pA hidden item, a patch of grass,\na TRAINER waiting sideways.$
- `Route25_Text_RileyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:346): You didn't rush past this time.\pGood. Let's make the rematch\ncount.$

## Route25 — BLAKE (AROMA_LADY)

- `Route25_Text_BlakeDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:369): Freshly beaten!$
- `Route25_Text_BlakeIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:359): The breeze carries flower scents\nfarther over water.\pThat's why ROUTE 25 feels so fresh.$
- `Route25_Text_BlakePostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:372): Flowers near the shore grow hardy.\pThey bend often, but they don't\nbreak easily.$
- `Route25_Text_BlakeRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:364): The air is fresh again.\pLet's see if your battling feels\nfresh too.$

## Route25 — BETHANY (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `Route25_Text_BethanyDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:387): Clear as a bell!$
- `Route25_Text_BethanyIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:377): This far from town, POKéMON learn\nto listen to their TRAINER.\pToo much wind and water otherwise!$
- `Route25_Text_BethanyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:390): Good raising is a conversation.\pROUTE 25 gives POKéMON plenty\nto respond to.$
- `Route25_Text_BethanyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:382): My POKéMON have been listening\nbetter every day.\pLet me hear how yours answer!$

## Route25 — REED (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `Route25_Text_TriathleteReedDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:398): You ran rings around us!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteReedIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:395): A long bridge makes a great track!\nCare for a training break?$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteReedPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:401): We pace ourselves on the long run.\nThere is room to pass beside us!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteReedRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:404): Another lap, another battle!$

## Route25 — LILA (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `Route25_Text_TriathleteLilaDefeat` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:410): Time to catch our breath!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteLilaIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:407): Sea air, steady feet, strong team!\nLet us show you our pace!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteLilaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:413): My POKéMON set the pace.\nI just try to keep up!$
- `Route25_Text_TriathleteLilaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route25/text.inc:416): Ready for another training break?$

## Route3 — GREG (HIKER)

- `Route3_Text_GregDefeat` (data/maps/Route3/text.inc:46): You found your footing!$
- `Route3_Text_GregIntro` (data/maps/Route3/text.inc:42): The trail gets rockier from here.\nStep carefully!$
- `Route3_Text_GregPostBattle` (data/maps/Route3/text.inc:49): MT. MOON is rougher than this.\nBring sturdy POKéMON.$
- `Route3_Text_GregRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:30): Back on the rocky trail?\pGood. Let's see if your footing\nhas gotten even stronger!$

## Route3 — JOSH (YOUNGSTER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_JOSH_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5824): I slipped up!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_JOSH_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5817): MT. MOON is ahead, so I'm training\non every rocky patch I can find!\pBattle me!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_JOSH_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5826): The closer you get to MT. MOON,\nthe tougher the road feels.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_JOSH_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5821): I trained on the rocks again!\pThis time, I won't lose my footing!$

## Route3 — JANAE (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ROBIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5160): You didn't stumble at all!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ROBIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5152): The road to MT. MOON makes\neveryone hurry.\pSlow down and battle me first!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ROBIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5162): The mountain looks close from here,\nbut ROUTE 3 still tests your pace.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ROBIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5156): Still rushing toward MT. MOON?\pThen I'll stop you for one more\nbattle!$

## Route3 — MIA (YOUNGSTER_F)

- `Route3_Text_YoungsterMiaDefeat` (data/maps/Route3/scripts.inc:27): I need more practice!$
- `Route3_Text_YoungsterMiaIntro` (data/maps/Route3/scripts.inc:24): Josh and I came out to train!\nWant to battle me too?$
- `Route3_Text_YoungsterMiaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route3/scripts.inc:30): We take turns picking\nwhere to explore next.$
- `Route3_Text_YoungsterMiaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route3/scripts.inc:33): Josh says I've improved my\ntiming. Help me prove it!$

## Route4 — TIMMY (YOUNGSTER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_TIMMY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5836): I still stumbled!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_TIMMY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5829): I tripped on a GEODUDE earlier,\nbut I'm not going home!\pROUTE 4 made me tougher!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_TIMMY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5838): The rocks here are rough, but they\nmake you pay attention.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_TIMMY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5833): I watched my step this time!\pNow watch out for my POKéMON!$

## Route4 — ANGELICA (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_CRISSY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5014): After all that searching!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_CRISSY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5005): I came to MT. MOON looking for\nmushroom POKéMON.\pThey like damp corners, but I like\nbattles too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_CRISSY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5016): Mushroom POKéMON hide well.\pThe trick is watching where the\nmountain stays cool and damp.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_CRISSY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5010): I checked the mountain again.\pNo mushrooms this time, so you're\nmy best find!$

## Route4 — WINSTON (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_RICKY_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4947): That belongs in my field notes!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_RICKY_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4939): I came out of MT. MOON covered\nin dust and questions.\pLet's see what your team uncovered!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_RICKY_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4949): Fossils, stones, old tunnels...\nROUTE 4 is where the mountain's\lstories spill into daylight.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_RICKY_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4943): Back from the mountain again?\pGood. Every pass through MT. MOON\nreveals something new!$

## Route4 — CAITLIN (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CAITLIN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5226): You made the exit look easy!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CAITLIN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5218): The road opens up after MT. MOON,\nbut don't relax too soon.\pA good SCOUT checks the exit too!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CAITLIN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5228): After a cave, open sky feels huge.\pThat's when tired TRAINERS make\nmistakes.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CAITLIN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5222): Coming from CERULEAN or MT. MOON?\pEither way, I'll check how ready\nyou are!$

## Route4 — ELLA (YOUNGSTER_F)

- `Route4_Text_YoungsterEllaDefeat` (data/maps/Route4/scripts.inc:87): You caught me off guard!$
- `Route4_Text_YoungsterEllaIntro` (data/maps/Route4/scripts.inc:84): Timmy spotted you first,\nbut I want a battle too!$
- `Route4_Text_YoungsterEllaPostBattle` (data/maps/Route4/scripts.inc:90): My POKéMON and I are getting\nbraver with every battle.$
- `Route4_Text_YoungsterEllaRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route4/scripts.inc:93): My team is braver on this rocky\nroad now. Let's try again!$

## Route5 — VANCE (BUG_CATCHER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_VANCE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4784): They got startled after all!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_VANCE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4776): The grass here is close to the\ncity, but bugs still love it.\pI'll prove they belong here!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_VANCE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4786): Even near SAFFRON, wild POKéMON\nfind little places to live.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_VANCE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4780): My bugs got used to the city\nnoise.\pNow they're ready for you!$

## Route5 — KATELYN (POKÉMON_RANGER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_KATELYN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5442): Careful and strong. Good.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_KATELYN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5431): I watch ROUTE 5 because city\ntravelers and wild POKéMON share\lthe same narrow path.\pShow me you can pass through\nwith care!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_KATELYN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5444): The DAY CARE, the tunnel, the\ncity gate...\pBusy places need gentle footsteps.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_KATELYN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5437): You returned without trampling\nthe grass.\pGood. Now let's see your battle\ndiscipline!$

## Route5 — HEIDI (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_HEIDI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5282): You picked the winning path!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_HEIDI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5274): I like this road because it gives\nyou choices.\pCERULEAN, SAFFRON, or the tunnel-\nwhich way are you heading?$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_HEIDI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5284): A good route is more than a line.\nIt's a set of choices.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_HEIDI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5279): You chose to come back here?\pThen I choose another battle!$

## Route5 — IRMA (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4771): Your team bloomed beautifully!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4763): The flowers here soften the road\nbetween CERULEAN and SAFFRON.\pA fragrant battle, perhaps?$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4773): ROUTE 5's little flowers survive\nbecause people leave them be.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4767): The scent has changed since our\nlast battle.\pSo have we, I hope.$

## Route5 — FINN (BUG_CATCHER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_FINN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4218): They grew faster than me!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_FINN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4210): BUG POKéMON gather near the\nDAY CARE's quiet grass.\pI came to watch them grow!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_FINN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4220): Small POKéMON teach patience.\pYou notice more when you stop\nrushing between cities.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_FINN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4214): The bugs around the DAY CARE\nseem livelier today.\pLet's see if I am too!$

## Route5 — OSCAR (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_OSCAR_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4307): You stayed sharper than me!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_OSCAR_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4299): I camp here because ROUTE 5 is\nquiet for a road near SAFFRON.\pA calm route makes a sharp team!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_OSCAR_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4309): Between CERULEAN and SAFFRON,\nthis place gives TRAINERS room\lto breathe.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_OSCAR_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4303): Back from the city roads?\pGood. Let's see if ROUTE 5's\nquiet helped your focus!$

## Route5 — MAX (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_MAE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4335): My lunch break lost momentum!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_MAE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4327): I brought lunch for the walk to\nthe UNDERGROUND PATH.\pWant a battle before I head down?$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_MAE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4337): The UNDERGROUND PATH makes KANTO\nfeel smaller.\pYou pop up somewhere new before\nyou know it.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PICNICKER_MAE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4331): I packed extra this time.\pA tunnel trip and a rematch both\nneed supplies!$

## Route5 — NIA (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_NIA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4387): That's healthy growth!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_NIA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4379): The DAY CARE teaches you that\nPOKéMON grow in their own time.\pLet's see how your team has grown!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_NIA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4389): Some POKéMON need training.\nSome need time.\pGood TRAINERS learn both.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_NIA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4383): I checked on the DAY CARE again.\pNow I want to check on your\nteam's growth too!$

## Route5 — HOLLIS (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_HOLLIS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4257): My theory needs work!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_HOLLIS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4248): Everyone talks about the DAY CARE,\nbut I watch the bugs nearby.\pBreeding, molting, changing-\nit's all connected!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_HOLLIS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4259): Eggs, cocoons, evolution...\pROUTE 5 is a quiet place for\nbig changes.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_HOLLIS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4253): I found another pattern in the\ngrass by the DAY CARE.\pA rematch should test it!$

## Route5 — JACKSON (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_JACKSON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4272): I misread the vibrations!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_JACKSON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4263): The UNDERGROUND PATH shakes the\nsoil more than people think.\pBUG POKéMON feel those tiny\nvibrations!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_JACKSON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4274): Routes aren't only what you see.\pSometimes the important movement\nis under your feet.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_JACKSON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4268): I felt footsteps below the route.\pWas that you coming back for a\nrematch?$

## Route5 — PRESTON (PSYCHIC_M)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_PRESTON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6746): My focus scattered!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_PRESTON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6737): Close to SAFFRON,\nyou can feel thoughts\ngetting louder.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_PRESTON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6749): SAFFRON's PSYCHICS\ntrain the mind before\nthe POKEMON.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_PRESTON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6742): Let's quiet the noise\nwith another battle.$

## Route6 — DAN (TUBER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_DAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5549): Splashdown!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_DAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5539): I can see VERMILION from here,\nbut I don't want to wait for\lthe beach.\pLet's battle right here!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_DAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5551): This route is like a countdown.\pEvery step south gets you closer\nto VERMILION's water.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_DAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5544): Still between SAFFRON and the\nharbour?\pGood! I need another practice\nbattle before swimming.$

## Route6 — IRIS (TUBER_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_IRIS_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5053): I got sand in my strategy!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_IRIS_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5045): I was going to VERMILION for\nthe beach, but this sand is\lgood enough for a warm-up!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_IRIS_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5055): ROUTE 6 smells like the sea when\nthe wind comes from VERMILION.\pThat's how I know I'm close!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_TUBER_IRIS_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5049): You came back before I reached\nthe beach?\pThen we'll warm up again!$

## Route6 — KYLE (FISHERMAN)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_FISHERMAN_CAROL_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5240): You didn't take the bait!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_FISHERMAN_CAROL_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5232): People hurry to the UNDERGROUND\nPATH and forget to watch the\lwater beside them.\pThat's where the good bites are!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_FISHERMAN_CAROL_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5242): A quiet shore beside a busy road\nis perfect for fishing.\pThe footsteps keep the fish\ncurious.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_FISHERMAN_CAROL_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5237): The water's different today.\pMaybe the battle will be too!$

## Route6 — TYRON (SAILOR)

- `EventScript_WatersEdge_SAILOR_TYRON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4526): You caught the wind first!$
- `EventScript_WatersEdge_SAILOR_TYRON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4517): VERMILION's harbour wind reaches\nall the way up this road.\pA SAILOR can feel a battle in\nthe breeze!$
- `EventScript_WatersEdge_SAILOR_TYRON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4528): Land routes have currents too.\pWatch how TRAINERS flow between\nthe city and the sea.$
- `EventScript_WatersEdge_SAILOR_TYRON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4522): Back from SAFFRON or VERMILION?\pEither way, ROUTE 6 owes us\nanother battle!$

## Route6 — LUCIAN (PSYCHIC_M)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_LUCIAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6762): That future slipped\naway.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_LUCIAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6754): The path to SAFFRON\nsharpens every sense.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_LUCIAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6766): Power like this belongs\naround SAFFRON. The city\nhums with it.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_LUCIAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6758): I foresaw another\nbattle here.$

## Route7 — CHAD (YOUNGSTER_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_CHAD_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5517): Whoa, that's city-level strong!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_CHAD_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5508): I train here because CELADON's\nright there and SAFFRON's right\lthere too!\pTwo cities means twice the battles!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_CHAD_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5519): I want to battle TRAINERS from\nboth cities.\pThat way I'll get strong twice\nas fast!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_CHAD_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5513): You came back from one city or\nthe other, right?\pThen show me what you learned!$

## Route7 — CAMERON (PSYCHIC_M)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_CAMERON_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4511): Your focus held firm.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_CAMERON_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4502): SAFFRON's thoughts press outward,\neven here on ROUTE 7.\pCan you keep your focus at the\ncity's edge?$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_CAMERON_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4513): Between CELADON's calm and\nSAFFRON's noise, ROUTE 7 is a\lthin place for concentration.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_CAMERON_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4507): I felt you return before I saw\nyou.\pLet's test that pressure again.$

## Route7 — CLAIRE (TRENDSETTER_F)

- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterClaire_Defeat` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:705): You gave me a bright idea!$
- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterClaire_Intro` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:702): A new look, a new team!\nCare for a quick battle?$
- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterClaire_Post` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:708): Shopping can wait.\nThat battle gave me an idea!$

## Route7 — ELIO (TRENDSETTER_M)

- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterElio_Defeat` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:719): You set today's trend!$
- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterElio_Intro` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:716): Celadon sets the trends.\nMy team sets the pace!$
- `Route7_EventScript_TrendsetterElio_Post` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:722): Great style means finding\nwhat works for you.$

## Route7 — RUBY (YOUNGSTER_F)

- `Route7_Text_YoungsterRubyDefeat` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:740): We still have lots to learn!$
- `Route7_Text_YoungsterRubyIntro` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:737): Chad and I train outside town.\nLet me show you our progress!$
- `Route7_Text_YoungsterRubyPostBattle` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:743): There are strong trainers here!\nI want to keep up with them.$
- `Route7_Text_YoungsterRubyRematchIntro` (data/maps/Route7/scripts.inc:746): Chad and I tried a new training\nplan. Want to see my results?$

## Route8 — HEATHER (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4757): What a vivid bloom!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4748): The flowers thin out as you get\ncloser to LAVENDER.\pThat makes each scent feel more\nprecious, don't you think?$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4759): Near LAVENDER, even flowers seem\nto grow more gently.\pIt suits this road.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4753): You returned along the quiet road.\pGood. Let's see what has bloomed\nin your team.$

## Route8 — ALIZE (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALIZE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5400): Your team stayed calmer!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALIZE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5391): POKéMON raised near busy roads\nlearn to stay calm.\pMine trained beside SAFFRON's\ntraffic!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALIZE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5402): A good BREEDER watches where a\nPOKéMON grows up.\pRoad sounds can shape temperament.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ALIZE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5396): My POKéMON have heard every step\non this road.\pThey know yours too. Rematch?$

## Route8 — DREW (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_DREW_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4869): You had plenty left!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_DREW_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4861): I watch the UNDERGROUND PATH\nentrance for tired travelers.\pIf you still have energy, prove it!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_DREW_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4871): The tunnel saves time, but ROUTE 8\nteaches patience.\pYou need both around SAFFRON.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_DREW_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4865): You came back through the tunnel\ntraffic?\pThen you must be ready!$

## Route8 — EMILIA (CHANNELER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ALI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4999): Your bond shines brightly.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ALI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4990): I am returning to LAVENDER to\npray for departed POKéMON.\pA quiet battle helps me prepare\nmy mind for the journey.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ALI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5001): LAVENDER lies east of here.\pWe remember departed POKéMON\nby caring for those beside us.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_ALI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4995): The road to LAVENDER gives me\ntime to reflect.\pShall we share another battle?$

## Route8 — MARTHA (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_MARTHA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5333): I blinked first!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_MARTHA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5324): The road gets quiet before\nLAVENDER.\pThat's when a SCOUT should pay\nthe most attention.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_MARTHA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5335): When a place feels still, people\ndrop their guard.\pROUTE 8 taught me not to.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_MARTHA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5329): You came back from LAVENDER?\pThen you know why I keep watch\nout here.$

## Route8 — BRYCE (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_BRYCE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4855): You kept steady!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_BRYCE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4846): I set camp halfway between the\ncity noise and LAVENDER's quiet.\pIt's the perfect place to test\nyour nerve.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_BRYCE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4857): Some roads are loud. Some roads\nare lonely.\pROUTE 8 manages to be both.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_BRYCE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4851): Back on the long road?\pThen let's see if your nerve\nheld up.$

## Route8 — CELIA (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CELIA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5255): You read the route better!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CELIA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5247): I mark the paths people miss when\nthey rush to the UNDERGROUND PATH.\pYou noticed me, so battle me!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CELIA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5257): Shortcuts are useful, but looking\naround is how you find hidden\nplaces.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_CELIA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5251): You found this side path again.\pA good SCOUT respects a sharp\nmemory!$

## Route8 — JOHAN (PSYCHIC_M)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_JOHAN_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6731): You cut through the noise!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_JOHAN_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6725): The noise from SAFFRON makes it\nhard to hear a single thought.\pCan you focus on one command?$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_JOHAN_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6734): I close my eyes and picture this\nroad. The footsteps tell me\pwhich way people are heading.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_PSYCHIC_JOHAN_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6728): I recognised your steps this\ntime. Let's see if I can predict\pyour moves!$

## Route9 — ALAN (HIKER)

- `Route9_Text_AlanDefeat` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:92): Hahahaha!\pYou beat me fair!$
- `Route9_Text_AlanIntro` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:87): Hahahaha!\pThis ridge wakes up the legs and\nthe lungs. Bring it on!$
- `Route9_Text_AlanPostBattle` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:96): Hahahaha!\pUs hearty hikers laugh so the\nmountains know we're coming.$
- `Route9_Text_AlanRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:163): Hahahaha!\nI'll win this time!$

## Route9 — BRENT (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `Route9_Text_BrentDefeat` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:78): I lost my stance!$
- `Route9_Text_BrentIntro` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:73): The climb to ROCK TUNNEL is my\ntraining ground!\pKeep your stance low and steady!$
- `Route9_Text_BrentPostBattle` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:81): A strong stance keeps you steady\non uneven ground.\pThe mountain path punishes sloppy\nsteps.$
- `Route9_Text_BrentRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:160): I've practised my stance on\nloose stone. Try to knock me off\pbalance now!$

## Route9 — CORA (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `Route9_Text_ConnerDefeat` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:107): You hit harder!$
- `Route9_Text_ConnerIntro` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:101): This mountain path makes every\nbattle hit harder!\pLoose stones, tight turns-\nperfect balance training!$
- `Route9_Text_ConnerPostBattle` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:110): Train on rough ground and your\nbalance improves fast.\pThat's why I like ROUTE 9.$
- `Route9_Text_ConnerRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:167): I've sharpened my turns on this\nrocky path. Watch my team's\pfootwork!$

## Route9 — BRICE (HIKER)

- `Route9_Text_BriceDefeat` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:64): You found better footing!$
- `Route9_Text_BriceIntro` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:58): The rocks here teach you where\nto put your feet.\pLet's see if your battling has\nthe same footing!$
- `Route9_Text_BricePostBattle` (data/maps/Route9/text.inc:67): ROCK TUNNEL is tougher than this\nroad.\pIf ROUTE 9 tires you out, rest\nbefore heading in.$
- `Route9_Text_BriceRematchIntro` (data/text/trainers.inc:156): Hahaha!\nA little toughie, as always!$

## Route9 — JOVAN (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_EDDIE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5601): Your team made better history!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_EDDIE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5592): These rocks are younger than the\nones in ROCK TUNNEL.\pStill, they have stories.\nBattle me and I'll tell you!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_EDDIE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5603): The surface stones here hint at\nthe tunnel ahead.\pThat's why RUIN MANIACS never\nwalk a road casually.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_YOUNGSTER_EDDIE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5597): Back to study the ridge?\pGood. A rematch is the best field\nnote.$

## Route9 — LUCAS (HIKER)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_NORA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5130): My legs held, but my team didn't!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_NORA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5122): I hike this road to toughen up\nbefore ROCK TUNNEL.\pDon't expect me to take it easy!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_NORA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5132): Cerulean feels far behind once\nthe road turns rocky.\pThat's when the journey starts\nfeeling real.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_LASS_NORA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5126): Back from the tunnel road?\pThen show me how much stronger\nyour legs got!$

## Route9 — ISAAC (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_ISAAC_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4896): You stayed sure-footed!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_ISAAC_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4888): A SCOUT checks the ledges before\nanyone gets careless.\pROUTE 9 has plenty to check!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_ISAAC_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4898): This route looks open, but the\nrocks guide where people move.\pA good SCOUT reads the ground.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_CAMPER_ISAAC_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4892): Back on the ridge road?\pGood. Let's see if you remembered\nthe footing.$

## Route9 — ALMA (SCOUT_F)

- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_ALMA_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5187): That shook the basket!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_ALMA_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5179): I packed for a picnic, but ROUTE 9\nturned it into a hike.\pA battle break sounds perfect!$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_ALMA_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5189): You can still enjoy a rough road.\pYou just need sturdy shoes and\na steady POKéMON.$
- `EventScript_RouteTrainer_PICNICKER_ALMA_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:5183): Back for another rocky picnic?\pThen let's battle before the food\ngets dusty.$

## SSAnne_1F_Room2 — ANN (LADY)

- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_AnnDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:17): Oh, no!\nI went around the world for these!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_AnnIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:13): I collected these POKéMON\nfrom all around the world!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_AnnPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:21): You hurt my poor POKéMON!\pI demand that you heal them at a\nPOKéMON CENTER!$

## SSAnne_1F_Room2 — TYLER (TRENDSETTER_M)

- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_TylerDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:5): Oh!\nThat was not proper at all!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_TylerIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:1): Father says a proper trainer\naccepts every challenge.$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room2_Text_TylerPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room2/text.inc:9): I shall have to train harder\nbefore the next voyage.$

## SSAnne_1F_Room4 — BRI & REMI (TRENDSETTERS)

- `SSAnne_1F_Room4_EventScript_TrendsettersBriRemi_Defeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room4/scripts.inc:26): You stole our grand entrance!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room4_EventScript_TrendsettersBriRemi_Intro` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room4/scripts.inc:23): A grand voyage needs\na grand entrance!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room4_EventScript_TrendsettersBriRemi_NeedTwo` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room4/scripts.inc:32): Bring two POKéMON and show us\nyour best combination!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room4_EventScript_TrendsettersBriRemi_Post` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room4/scripts.inc:29): Our next stop?\nThe shops in Celadon!$

## SSAnne_1F_Room5 — ARTHUR (GENTLEMAN)

- `SSAnne_1F_Room5_Text_ArthurDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room5/text.inc:5): Humph! You rude child!\nYou have no sense of courtesy!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room5_Text_ArthurIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room5/text.inc:1): You insolent pup!\nHow dare you barge in!$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room5_Text_ArthurPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room5/text.inc:9): I wish to be left alone!\nGet out!$

## SSAnne_1F_Room7 — THOMAS (GENTLEMAN)

- `SSAnne_1F_Room7_Text_ThomasDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room7/text.inc:6): My, my friends…$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room7_Text_ThomasIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room7/text.inc:1): I am but a solitary traveler…\pMy sole companions and friends are\nPOKéMON I caught on my journeys…$
- `SSAnne_1F_Room7_Text_ThomasPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_1F_Room7/text.inc:9): You should be nice to friends!$

## SSAnne_2F_Room2 — DALE (TRENDSETTER_M)

- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_DaleDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:17): A costly lesson!$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_DaleIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:13): A fine voyage calls for a fine\nbattle, wouldn't you say?$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_DalePostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:20): The ship's party was splendid.\pThe battles afterward have been\nrather lively, too.$

## SSAnne_2F_Room2 — BROOKS (GENTLEMAN)

- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_BrooksDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:5): Good match!\nAh, I feel young again!$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_BrooksIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:1): Competing against the young keeps\nme youthful.$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room2_Text_BrooksPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room2/text.inc:9): Fifteen years ago, I would\nhave won!$

## SSAnne_2F_Room4 — LAMAR (GENTLEMAN)

- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_LamarDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:5): I must salute you!$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_LamarIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:1): Which do you find more worthy,\na strong or a rare POKéMON?$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_LamarPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:8): Personally, I prefer strong and\nrare POKéMON.$

## SSAnne_2F_Room4 — DAWN (TRENDSETTER_F)

- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_DawnDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:16): Take it easy!$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_DawnIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:12): I don't believe I saw you at the\nparty?$
- `SSAnne_2F_Room4_Text_DawnPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_2F_Room4/text.inc:19): Oh, I adore your strong POKéMON!\nOh, how I envy you for them!$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room1 — BLAIR (LADY)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_BarnyDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:17): Oh!\nHow uncouth of me!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_BarnyIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:13): Even below deck, one should keep\ntheir battling form polished.$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_BarnyPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:21): I came below deck for quiet.\pInstead, I found quite the capable\ntrainer.$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room1 — PHILLIP (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_PhillipDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:5): Argh!\nBeaten by a kid!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_PhillipIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:1): Matey, you're walking the plank if\nyou lose!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room1_Text_PhillipPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room1/text.inc:9): When we're out at sea, jellyfish\nPOKéMON sometimes drift by.$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room2 — HUEY (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room2_Text_HueyDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room2/text.inc:4): Okay, you're not bad.$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room2_Text_HueyIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room2/text.inc:1): Even us sailors have POKéMON, too!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room2_Text_HueyPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room2/text.inc:7): We caught all our POKéMON while\nwe were out at sea.$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room3 — DYLAN (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room3_Text_DylanDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room3/text.inc:4): Argh!\nLost it!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room3_Text_DylanIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room3/text.inc:1): I like feisty kids like you!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room3_Text_DylanPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room3/text.inc:8): Sea POKéMON live in deep water.\nYou'll need a ROD to fish them up!$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room4 — DUNCAN (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_DuncanDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:17): Your spirit sank me!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_DuncanIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:13): Come on, then!\nMy sailor's pride is at stake!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_DuncanPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:20): Did you see the FISHING GURU in\nVERMILION CITY?$

## SSAnne_B1F_Room4 — LEONARD (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_LeonardDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:5): Right!\nGood battle, mate!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_LeonardIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:1): You know what they say about\nsailors and battling!$
- `SSAnne_B1F_Room4_Text_LeonardPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_B1F_Room4/text.inc:9): Hahaha!\nWant to be a sailor, mate?$

## SSAnne_Deck — TREVOR (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_TrevorDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:29): I was just careless!$
- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_TrevorIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:25): Ahoy, there!\nAre you seasick?$
- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_TrevorPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:32): My pa said there are 100 kinds of\nPOKéMON. I think there are more.$

## SSAnne_Deck — EDMOND (SAILOR)

- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_EdmondDefeat` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:18): You're impressive!$
- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_EdmondIntro` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:14): Hey, matey!\pLet's do a little jig!$
- `SSAnne_Deck_Text_EdmondPostBattle` (data/maps/SSAnne_Deck/text.inc:21): How many kinds of POKéMON do you\nthink there are in this big world?$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — HITOSHI (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HitoshiDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:67): Oof!\nI give up!$
