- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HitoshiIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:62): Hwaah!\pYou're trespassing in our\nFIGHTING DOJO!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HitoshiPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:71): The prime fighters across the land\ntrain here.$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — HANA (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HidekiDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:40): Judge!\nOne point!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HidekiIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:36): I hear you're good!\nShow me!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_HidekiPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:44): Our MASTER is a pro fighter.\nBe prepared to lose!$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — AARON (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_AaronDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:54): Yow!\nStubbed fingers!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_AaronIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:48): Hiyah!\nI have no fear for anything tough!\pI shatter boulders for training\nevery day!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_AaronPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:58): The only thing that frightens us is\npsychic power!$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — MIKA (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_MikeDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:29): I bow to that technique!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_MikeIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:25): Hoargh!\nTake your shoes off!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_MikePostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:32): You wait 'til you see our MASTER!\nI'm a small fry compared to him!$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — KOICHI (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_ChoosePrizedFightingMon` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:13): Indeed, I have lost!\pBut, I beseech you, do not take\nour emblem as your trophy!\pIn return, I will give you a prized\nFIGHTING POKéMON!\pChoose whichever one you like!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_MasterKoichiDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:9): Hwa!\nArrgh! Beaten!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_MasterKoichiIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:1): Grunt!\pI am the KARATE MASTER!\nI am the LEADER here!\pYou wish to challenge us?\nExpect no mercy!\pFwaaa!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_StayAndTrainWithUs` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:21): Grunt!\nStay and train at karate with us!$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — CARLY (EXPERT_M)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CarlyDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:80): A clean finish.$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CarlyIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:75): Black belts master technique.\pExperts master when to use it.\nShow me your judgment.$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CarlyPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:83): Power without timing is only\nnoise.\pA true martial artist makes one\nmotion answer everything.$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — MARA (EXPERT_F)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_ExpertMaraDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/scripts.inc:165): You spotted the opening first!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_ExpertMaraIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/scripts.inc:162): Watch your opponent carefully.\nEach move teaches you something.$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_ExpertMaraPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/scripts.inc:168): I ask my students what they\nnoticed before I give advice.\pThey often see what I miss.$

## SaffronCity_Dojo — REN & MEI (SPARRING_DUO)

- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CrushKinAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:144): We grew up training side by side.\nNow our POKéMON do the same!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CrushKinDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:141): You found our opening!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CrushKinIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:135): We train together as siblings!\nOur POKéMON follow our rhythm.\pShow us how your team works\nin a DOUBLE BATTLE!$
- `SaffronCity_Dojo_Text_CrushKinNeedTwo` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Dojo/text.inc:148): Bring two POKéMON that can battle.\nWe always train as a pair!$

## SaffronCity_Gym — SABRINA (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_ExplainMarshBadgeTakeThis` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:34): A calm mind earns trust from\neven strong outsider POKéMON.\pKeep completing PROF. OAK's\nsubquests to strengthen that bond.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_ReceivedChinglingFromSabrina` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:183): {PLAYER} received CHINGLING\nfrom SABRINA!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaChinglingAdvice` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:187): CHINGLING already feels close\nto you. Nurture that friendship.\pWhen its bond is strong enough,\nit can evolve into CHIMECHO\lby leveling up at night.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:14): This loss shocks me!\nBut, a loss is a loss.\pI admit, I didn't work hard enough\nto win.\pYour victory has earned you the\nMARSHBADGE.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaGiftChingling` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:177): SABRINA: Your calm presence\nearned this CHINGLING's trust.\pI would like you to take it\nwith you on your journey.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:1): I had a vision of your arrival.\pI am SABRINA.\nSAFFRON CITY's GYM LEADER.\pYou kept your mind steady within\nPOKéMON TOWER's astral pull.\pI have had psychic powers since\nI was a child.\pIt started when a spoon I\ncarelessly tossed, bent.\pI dislike battling, but if you wish,\nI will show you my powers!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaPostBattle` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:22): Psychic power isn't something that\nonly a few people have.\pEveryone has psychic power.\nPeople just don't realize it.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SabrinaPostFieldVictory` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:28): POKéMON TOWER's astral pressure\nhas settled.\pThe MARSHBADGE is proof that your\nmind held firm within it.$
- `Text_GiveNicknameToThisMon` (data/event_scripts.s:713): Do you want to give a nickname to\nthis {STR_VAR_1}?$
- `Text_MonSentToBoxBillsBoxFull` (data/text/pc_transfer.inc:19): BOX “{STR_VAR_3}” on\nBILL'S PC was full.\p{STR_VAR_2} was transferred to\nBOX “{STR_VAR_1}.”$
- `Text_MonSentToBoxInBillsPC` (data/text/pc_transfer.inc:7): {STR_VAR_2} was transferred to\nBILL'S PC.\pIt was placed in \nBOX “{STR_VAR_1}.”$
- `Text_MonSentToBoxInSomeonesPC` (data/text/pc_transfer.inc:1): {STR_VAR_2} was transferred to\nSomeone's PC.\pIt was placed in \nBOX “{STR_VAR_1}.”$
- `Text_MonSentToBoxSomeonesBoxFull` (data/text/pc_transfer.inc:13): BOX “{STR_VAR_3}” on\nSomeone's PC was full.\p{STR_VAR_2} was transferred to\nBOX “{STR_VAR_1}.”$
- `Text_NoMoreRoomForPokemon` (data/text/pc_transfer.inc:36): There's no more room for POKéMON!\pThe POKéMON BOXES are full and\ncan't accept any more!$

## SaffronCity_Gym — ORIN (PSYCHIC_M)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_OrinAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:118): A calm mind can learn\neven from defeat.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_OrinDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:115): My thoughts ran ahead of my\nteam!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_OrinIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:111): Focus your thoughts.\nNow, focus your team!$

## SaffronCity_Gym — ALDEN (PSYCHIC_M)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_AldenAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:129): The shortest path is not\nalways the clearest one.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_AldenDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:126): You found the clear way through!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_AldenIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:122): These rooms test direction.\nI test concentration.$

## SaffronCity_Gym — SILAS (PSYCHIC_M)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SilasAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:140): Prediction still needs practice.\nBring your VS SEEKER next time.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SilasDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:137): I didn't predict that finish!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SilasIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:133): Can you read my next move?\nLet us find out.$

## SaffronCity_Gym — MIRA (PSYCHIC_F)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_MiraAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:151): Our training continues\nwhile SABRINA is away.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_MiraDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:148): My concentration slipped!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_MiraIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:144): SABRINA values a steady mind.\nShow me yours in battle.$

## SaffronCity_Gym — SELENE (PSYCHIC_F)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SeleneAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:162): Trust makes it easier\nto understand one another.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SeleneDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:159): Your team heard you clearly!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_SeleneIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:155): Close out the noise.\nListen to your POKéMON.$

## SaffronCity_Gym — LYRA (PSYCHIC_F)

- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_LyraAfter` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:173): Come back with your VS SEEKER.\nThere is always more to learn.$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_LyraDefeat` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:170): A clear and decisive answer!$
- `SaffronCity_Gym_Text_LyraIntro` (data/maps/SaffronCity_Gym/text.inc:166): A clear mind and a loyal team.\nThat is my kind of strength.$

## SilphCo_10F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_10F_Text_GruntDefeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_10F/text.inc:5): I'm stunned!$
- `SilphCo_10F_Text_GruntIntro` (data/maps/SilphCo_10F/text.inc:1): Welcome to 10F!\nSo good of you to join me!$
- `SilphCo_10F_Text_GruntPostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_10F/text.inc:8): Nice try, but the boardroom is up\none more floor.$

## SilphCo_11F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt1Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:116): Gaah!\nDemolished!$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt1Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:112): Halt! Do you have an appointment\nwith my BOSS?$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt1PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:120): Watch your step…\nMy BOSS likes his POKéMON tough!$

## SilphCo_11F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_F)

- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt2Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:105): Don't…\nPlease!$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt2Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:101): Stop right there!\nDon't you move!$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_Grunt2PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:109): So, you want to see my BOSS?$

## SilphCo_11F — GIOVANNI (BOSS)

- `SilphCo_11F_Text_GiovanniChallenge` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:78): I will not allow sentiment to\ninterrupt absolute power.$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_GiovanniDefeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:82): No…\nThe plan was flawless!$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_GiovanniIntro` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:63): GIOVANNI: You're the child from\nPOKEMON MANSION...\pSo, you followed me from\nCINNABAR ISLAND.\pSILPH's MASTER BALL is no mere\nproduct.\pIt is control made mechanical.\nCommand made perfect.\pWith its plans, TEAM ROCKET will\nmanufacture the means to reclaim\lMEWTWO.\pCome forth.\nI'll deal with you now.$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_GiovanniPostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:86): GIOVANNI: So SILPH's MASTER BALL\nwill remain out of reach.\pNo matter.\pA BALL is only one way to claim\npower.\pSILPH's signal research will\ncomplete my device.\pI will call MEWTWO to ROUTE 7.\pOnce it answers, no LEAGUE,\nno child, and no bond will stand\lin my way.\pTEAM ROCKET, withdraw.\nWe have what we need.$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_GiovanniScoffs` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:60): GIOVANNI: Hmph.$
- `SilphCo_11F_Text_PresidentInterruptsGiovanni` (data/maps/SilphCo_11F/text.inc:53): PRESIDENT: GIOVANNI, stop this!\pThe MASTER BALL was never meant\nto enslave POKEMON.\pIf you force that power on MEWTWO,\nyou'll bring ruin on us all.$

## SilphCo_2F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_F)

- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt2Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:40): I goofed!$
- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt2Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:36): Hey, kid!\nWhat are you wandering here for?$
- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt2PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:43): SILPH CO. will be associated with\nTEAM ROCKET!$

## SilphCo_2F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt1Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:27): Tough!$
- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt1Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:24): No kids are allowed in here!$
- `SilphCo_2F_Text_Grunt1PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_2F/text.inc:30): Diamond-shaped tiles are teleport\nblocks.\pThey're transporters for this\nhigh-tech building.$

- `SilphCo_3F_Text_GruntIntro` (data/maps/SilphCo_3F/text.inc:9): Quit messing with us, kid!$
- `SilphCo_3F_Text_GruntPostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_3F/text.inc:15): A hint? You can open doors with\na CARD KEY!$

## SilphCo_4F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_4F_Text_Grunt1PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_4F/text.inc:16): Fwahahaha!\nMy BOSS has been after this place!$

## SilphCo_5F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_F)

- `SilphCo_5F_Text_Grunt2Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_5F/text.inc:48): Cough…\nCough…$
- `SilphCo_5F_Text_Grunt2Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_5F/text.inc:45): Show TEAM ROCKET a little respect!$
- `SilphCo_5F_Text_Grunt2PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_5F/text.inc:52): Which reminds me.\pKOFFING evolves into WEEZING!$

## SilphCo_5F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_5F_Text_Grunt1Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_5F/text.inc:14): Boom!$
- `SilphCo_5F_Text_Grunt1Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_5F/text.inc:10): I heard a kid was wandering\naround.$

## SilphCo_7F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_F)

- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt2Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:98): Fine!\nI lost!$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt2Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:94): A child intruder?\nThat must be you!$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt2PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:102): Go on home before my BOSS gets\nticked off!$

## SilphCo_7F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt3Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:64): Lights out!$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt3Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:60): Uh-oh!\nI smell a little mouse!$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_Grunt3PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:67): You won't find my BOSS by just\nscurrying around!$

## SilphCo_7F — TERRY (RIVAL_LATE)

- `SilphCo_7F_Text_RivalDefeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:139): Tch!\nYou might actually make it\lto the top floor.$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_RivalIntro` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:112): {RIVAL}: I figured out why\nthey're here before you did,\lobviously.\pSILPH isn't just money.\nIt's capture tech.\pA MASTER BALL that can catch\nanything...\pSomeone powerful would burn down\nSAFFRON for that.\pSee BOSS? The lab reconstructed\nmy KABUSTAR from fossil pieces.\pI chose DOME and HASTE fragments.\nPretty good combination, huh?\pGENIUS evolved into ESPEON\nwhile we trained one morning.\pNo stone. Just trust.\nGuess I earned it, huh?\pYou're going upstairs, right?\nReady to battle me and prove\lyou won't fold when it matters?$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_RivalNotReady` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:134): {RIVAL}: Figures.\pTry not to spend all day hiding\nbehind me.$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_RivalPostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:150): Fine. You can handle yourself.\pBut don't get excited.\nI already knew this was bigger\lthan a robbery.\pWhoever is pulling TEAM ROCKET's\nstrings wants control.\pNot money. Not badges.\nControl.\pI'm finding my own way through.\nTry not to trail too far behind.\pSmell ya!$
- `SilphCo_7F_Text_RivalWhatKeptYou` (data/maps/SilphCo_7F/text.inc:106): {RIVAL}: Ha!\nTook you long enough, {PLAYER}.\pDon't tell me the ROCKETS\nactually slowed you down.$

## SilphCo_8F — GRUNT (TEAM_ROCKET_M)

- `SilphCo_8F_Text_Grunt1Defeat` (data/maps/SilphCo_8F/text.inc:10): Not enough grit!$
- `SilphCo_8F_Text_Grunt1Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_8F/text.inc:7): That's as far as you'll go!$
- `SilphCo_8F_Text_Grunt1PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_8F/text.inc:13): If you don't turn back…\nI'll call for backup!$

- `SilphCo_8F_Text_Grunt2PostBattle` (data/maps/SilphCo_8F/text.inc:36): I'll leave you up to my siblings.$

- `SilphCo_9F_Text_Grunt1Intro` (data/maps/SilphCo_9F/text.inc:11): Your POKéMON seem to adore you,\nkid!$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — ALTON (POKÉFAN_M)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanAltonDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:153): They got plenty of attention!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanAltonIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:148): I come down here when SAFFRON's\ncrowds get too loud.\pMy {PKMN} still like attention!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanAltonPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:156): A tunnel full of trainers feels\nfriendlier than a busy street.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — MARLO (POKÉFAN_F)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanMiraDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:165): That was a warm little battle.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanMiraIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:160): Everyone above hurries past each\nother toward SAFFRON.\pDown here, we can actually chat.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanMiraPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:168): I like hearing footsteps before\nI see who is coming.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — NOLAN (POKÉMANIAC)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacNolanDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:176): A rare loss for my records!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacNolanIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:172): Shortcuts collect stories.\nThat makes this tunnel valuable.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacNolanPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:179): I catalogue every trainer who\npasses under SAFFRON.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — VANCE (SUPER_NERD)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdVanceDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:200): My figures were off!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdVanceIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:195): The air pressure shifts near each\nstairwell.\pLet's test battle acoustics!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdVancePostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:203): A cross tunnel changes sound in\nways a straight one never could.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — HUGO (POKÉFAN_M)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanHugoDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:225): That team has spirit!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanHugoIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:219): Four exits, one tunnel.\nEvery trainer brings a\ndifferent partner.\pShow me yours!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanHugoPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:228): I keep notes on the partners\npeople choose underground.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — DIRK (POKÉMANIAC)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacDirkDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:272): My rumor fizzled out!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacDirkIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:268): Rare {PKMN} rumors travel faster\nunderground than above.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacDirkPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:275): SAFFRON's streets talk business.\nDown here, people talk {PKMN}.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — RICH (POKÉFAN_M)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanRichDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:249): Your POKEMON stole the show!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanRichIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:244): I came for the shortcut and\nstayed for the parade of\nPOKEMON!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanRichPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:252): Every entrance brings another\nteam to admire.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — LESLIE (SUPER_NERD)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdLeslieDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:212): Open circuit!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdLeslieIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:207): SAFFRON sits above us like a giant\ncircuit board.\pCare to complete a battle circuit?$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_SuperNerdLesliePostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:215): Every entrance feeds a different\nflow of trainers underground.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — RICARDO (POKÉMANIAC)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacRicardoDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:188): I'll file that under defeat.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacRicardoIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:183): People drop odd things when they\nduck underground.\pI collect battles, too!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacRicardoPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:191): The path above belongs to traffic.\nThis path belongs to finds.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — ELLIOT (POKÉFAN_F)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanElliotDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:236): That was worth the wait!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanElliotIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:232): Rare teams pass under SAFFRON.\nLet me see yours up close!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanElliotPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:239): Aboveground everyone hurries.\nDown here, I can admire a\ngood team.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — SOL (POKÉFAN_F)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanSolDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:261): I heard that loud and clear!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanSolIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:256): Every echo down here sounds\nlike another POKEMON cry.\pBattle me before I guess wrong!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokefanSolPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:264): The tunnel makes every team\nfeel a little mysterious.$

## UndergroundPath_Tunnel — HIDEO (POKÉMANIAC)

- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacHideoDefeat` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:283): Your story beats mine!$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacHideoIntro` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:279): I haunt this tunnel for odd teams\nand stranger battle stories.$
- `UndergroundPath_Tunnel_Text_PokemaniacHideoPostBattle` (data/maps/UndergroundPath_Tunnel/scripts.inc:286): A real fanatic knows the best\nfinds are below the obvious path.$

## VermilionCity_Gym — LT. SURGE (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_ExplainThunderBadge` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:35): That pass unlocks vitamins and\nbattle items at POKéMON MARTS.\pPower isn't handed to you.\nUse those tools and train until\lyour POKéMON can hold it.\pBring me a RAICHU with real\nvoltage.\pThen I'll show you evolution\nthrough serious output.$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeDeclineElectabuzzTrade` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:54): No pressure, kid!\pCome back when you're ready to\nhandle that kind of power.$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeDefeat` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:85): Now that's a shocker!\pYou're the real deal, kid!\pFine, then, take the THUNDERBADGE!$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeElectabuzzTradeComplete` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:63): Hah!\nNow that's a power surge!\pTrading with an ELECTIRIZER\nturned ELECTABUZZ into ELECTIVIRE!$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeElectabuzzTradeDone` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:69): That ELECTIVIRE was built for\nserious output.\pKeep it charged, kid!$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeIntro` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:1): So, you made it back, kid!\pI'm LT. SURGE.\nVERMILION CITY's GYM LEADER.\pYou handled VOLT TACKLE training\nwithout flinching.\pThat takes nerve!\pPower isn't handed to you.\nYou train until you can hold it!\pBut a GYM battle is combat!\nNo holding back!\pI tell you, kid, electric POKéMON\nsaved me during the war!\pNow show me you can stand against\nreal voltage!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeNotRaichu` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:59): That's not RAICHU, kid!\pBring me the real lightning rod.$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgeOfferElectabuzzTrade` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:46): Hey, kid!\pYou've got a RAICHU with real\nvoltage, right?\pI've got an ELECTABUZZ holding\nan ELECTIRIZER.\pTrade me your RAICHU for it?$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_LtSurgePostBattle` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:17): A little word of advice, kid!\pElectricity is sure powerful!\pBut training is how you make\nthat power obey!\pBut, it's useless against GROUND-\ntype POKéMON!$
- `VermilionCity_Gym_Text_TakeThisWithYou` (data/maps/VermilionCity_Gym/text.inc:25): The THUNDERBADGE proves you\ncleared VERMILION GYM!\pAnd with it, take this pass.\nIt'll get you special MART stock.$

## VermilionHarbor — KAI (TUBER_M)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberKai_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6134): Whoa! My float did a flip!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberKai_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6130): My float keeps bobbing when the\nships go by! Can your team stay\psteady?$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberKai_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6136): I count the ripples after each\nboat. The little ones always\pcatch up!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberKai_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6132): I stayed upright through three\nwakes! Now for your POKéMON!$

## VermilionHarbor — MINA (TUBER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberMina_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6142): My shell couldn't save us!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberMina_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6138): I found a shell shaped like a\ntiny shield! My team wants to\ptest it.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberMina_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6144): I put the shells back when I'm\ndone. Someone small might need a\phome.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTuberMina_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6140): Look, another shield shell! This\ntime we have a plan!$

## VermilionHarbor — OTTO (FISHERMAN)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanOtto_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6054): A fine catch, young one!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanOtto_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6050): I lower my line beside the\nmooring posts. No need for a\pfancy cast. Show me your\ntechnique!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanOtto_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6056): A still float isn't always a bad\nsign. Sometimes the fish are\pstudying you.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanOtto_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6052): Same old rod, new trick. Watch\nthe tip!$

## VermilionHarbor — VALE (FISHERMAN)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanVale_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6062): That's one for my records!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanVale_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6058): The forest runoff meets salt\nwater here. I never know what\pwill bite next!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanVale_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6064): I note each catch with the tide.\nEven familiar fish choose\pdifferent water.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborFishermanVale_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6060): I've filled another page with\ncatches. Your team deserves a\ppage too!$

## VermilionHarbor — DANE (SWIMMER_M)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerDane_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6094): Time for my shore break!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerDane_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6090): I swim short lengths between\nrests on shore. Fast isn't much\puse if you can't get back!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerDane_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6096): Boats need room to turn. I stay\nwell clear of their route, even\pon a good day.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerDane_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6092): Rested, warmed up, ready! Let's\nmake this a good length!$

## VermilionHarbor — LINA (SWIMMER_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerLina_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6102): So much for a quiet swim!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerLina_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6098): I can float without making a\nripple. My POKéMON aren't nearly\pso quiet!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerLina_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6104): Lie back and listen. The ropes\ncreak, the gulls call... The\pharbor has a rhythm.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSwimmerLina_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6100): I heard your splashes coming.\nOur quiet break is over!$

## VermilionHarbor — PAX (SAILOR)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorPax_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6078): You slipped every knot!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorPax_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6074): I tie knots aboard ship. In\nbattle, I like leaving my\popponent in a tangle!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorPax_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6080): A knot should hold under strain,\nthen loosen when you need it.\pThat's a useful lesson ashore.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorPax_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6076): No loose ends in our plan today!\nLet's see you untie it!$

## VermilionHarbor — MORGAN (SAILOR)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorMorgan_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6070): A change of wind caught me!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorMorgan_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6066): I watch the treeline to judge\nthe wind. A sailor needs more\pthan a view of the waves!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorMorgan_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6072): Those trees mark the eastern\nshore. After a long voyage,\pgreen is a welcome sight.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorMorgan_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6068): Leaves fluttering, pennants\nflying... A fine breeze for\panother match!$

## VermilionHarbor — RAFE (SAILOR)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorRafe_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6086): You carried that battle!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorRafe_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6082): Loading cargo taught me to share\nthe weight. My team knows how to\ppull together!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorRafe_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6088): The smallest deckhand can spot a\nslipping rope. I listen when\panyone calls out.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborSailorRafe_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6084): All hands ready! We've learned\nto answer each other faster!$

## VermilionHarbor — SKYE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteSkye_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6126): You caught me in transition!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteSkye_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6122): I practise changing from a swim\nto a run. The first few steps\palways feel strange!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteSkye_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6128): I lay out my shoes before\nswimming. Finding them shouldn't\pbe the hardest part of training.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteSkye_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6124): Shoes ready, breath steady. This\ntransition includes a battle!$

## VermilionHarbor — RORY (TRIATHLETE_M)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteRory_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6118): I spent too much too soon!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteRory_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6114): I'm working on even splits.\nStarting too fast cost me my\plast race. Race my team instead!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteRory_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6120): I measure progress by what I\nhave left at the finish, not how\pI feel at the start.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteRory_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6116): I've saved something for the\nfinal stretch this time!$

## VermilionHarbor — ELLE (TRIATHLETE_F)

- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteElle_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6110): A well-earned water break!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteElle_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6106): My partner sets our training\npace. Today it wants to stop and\pchallenge you!$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteElle_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6112): Some days we train hard. Other\ndays we walk along the trees.\pRest is part of getting\nstronger.$
- `WatersEdge_EventScript_HarborTriathleteElle_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:6108): My partner spotted you again. I\nthink it wants a rematch!$

## VictoryRoad_1F — ROLANDO (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_RolandoDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:17): I had a chance…$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_RolandoIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:13): I can see you're good.\nLet me see exactly how good!$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_RolandoPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:20): Tch!\nI concede, you are better than I!$

## VictoryRoad_1F — NAOMI (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_NaomiDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:5): I lost out…\nVICTORY ROAD chose you this time.$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_NaomiIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:1): I wonder if you are good enough\nfor me?$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_NaomiPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/text.inc:9): I never wanted to lose to anybody,\nespecially to a younger kid…$

## VictoryRoad_1F — IVO (EXPERT_M)

- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertIvoDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:54): Your judgment held under strain!$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertIvoIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:51): Strength brought you this far.\nNow show me your judgment.$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertIvoPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:57): The last climb can tempt you to\nrush. Save enough strength to\pgreet what waits beyond it.$

## VictoryRoad_1F — KARINA (EXPERT_F)

- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertKarinaDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:75): You carried your lessons well!$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertKarinaIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:72): The road ahead tests us all.\nShow me what you have learned.$
- `VictoryRoad_1F_Text_ExpertKarinaPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_1F/scripts.inc:78): I think of every teacher who\nhelped me reach this road.\pLosing gives me one more to\nremember.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — DAWSON (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DawsonDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:5): No!\nYou really do belong on this road.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DawsonIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:1): If you can get through here,\nyou can go meet the ELITE FOUR.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DawsonPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:9): I can beat you when it comes to\nknowledge about POKéMON!$

## VictoryRoad_2F — DAISUKE (BLACK_BELT_M)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DaisukeDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:17): Atcho!\nEven my focus cracked.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DaisukeIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:13): This here is VICTORY ROAD.\nIt's the final test for TRAINERS!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_DaisukePostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:21): If you get stuck, try moving some\nboulders around.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — NELSON (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_NelsonDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:29): You got me!\nThat's ELITE FOUR resolve.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_NelsonIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:25): Ah, so you wish to challenge the\nELITE FOUR?$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_NelsonPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:33): {RIVAL} also came through here.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — VINCENT (RUIN_MANIAC)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_VincentDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:40): I got whipped on the final road.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_VincentIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:36): Come on!\nI'll whip you!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_VincentPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:43): You earned the right to be on\nVICTORY ROAD…$

## VictoryRoad_2F — GREGORY (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_GregoryDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:50): Well done!\nYou didn't flinch from the climb.$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_GregoryIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:47): Is VICTORY ROAD too tough?$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_GregoryPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/text.inc:54): Many TRAINERS give up their\nchallenge here and go home.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — KAI & TESS (ACES)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_KaiTessDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:99): You found the gap between us!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_KaiTessIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:96): This cave tests your teamwork.\nShow us how your team works!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_KaiTessNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:105): Bring two ready POKéMON\nso we can battle as a pair!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_KaiTessPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:102): We cover each other when\nthe going gets tough.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — STEVIE (BLACK_BELT_F)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_StevieDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:123): My training is far from over!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_StevieIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:120): Every cave is a training ground.\nShow me your fighting spirit!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_SteviePostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:126): Rock Tunnel taught me patience.\nThis road will test my resolve.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — IRIS (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFIrisDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:144): You know your team well!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFIrisIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:141): This road tests every weakness.\nHow well do you know your team?$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFIrisPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:147): I want to reach the LEAGUE\nwith a team I can trust.$

## VictoryRoad_2F — THEA (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFTheaDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:165): You made those moves count!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFTheaIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:162): Power alone will not be enough.\nMake every move count!$
- `VictoryRoad_2F_Text_AceTrainerFTheaPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_2F/scripts.inc:168): The right move at the right time\ncan change the whole battle.$

## VictoryRoad_3F — GEORGE (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_GeorgeDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:27): The rumors were true!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_GeorgeIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:24): I heard rumors of a child prodigy.$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_GeorgePostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:30): So, it was you who beat GIOVANNI\nof TEAM ROCKET?$

## VictoryRoad_3F — ALEXA (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_AlexaDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:38): Oh!\nSo strong!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_AlexaIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:34): TRAINERS live to seek stronger\nopponents.$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_AlexaPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:42): By fighting tough battles, you get\nstronger.$

## VictoryRoad_3F — COLBY (ACE_TRAINER_M)

- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_ColbyDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:60): I don't believe it!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_ColbyIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:57): Only the chosen can pass here!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_ColbyPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:63): All TRAINERS here are headed to\nthe POKéMON LEAGUE.\pDon't let down your guard.$

## VictoryRoad_3F — CAROLINE (ACE_TRAINER_F)

- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_CarolineDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:50): I'm furious!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_CarolineIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:46): I'll show you just how good you\nare. Not!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_CarolinePostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:53): You showed me just how good\nI was…$

## VictoryRoad_3F — RAY & TYRA (ACES)

- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_RayDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:72): RAY: Ludicrous!\nThis can't be!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_RayIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:68): RAY: Together, the two of us are\ndestined for greatness!$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_RayNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:80): RAY: Together, the two of us are\nstriving for the pinnacle.\pWe need you to bring two POKéMON\ninto battle with us.$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_RayPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:76): RAY: You've beaten us.\nGreatness remains elusive…$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_TyraDefeat` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:90): TYRA: Oh, but…$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_TyraIntro` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:86): TYRA: We're trying to become\nchampions together.$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_TyraNotEnoughMons` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:97): TYRA: You can't battle with us if\nyou have only one POKéMON.$
- `VictoryRoad_3F_Text_TyraPostBattle` (data/maps/VictoryRoad_3F/text.inc:93): TYRA: You've taught me that power\ncan be infinite in shape and form.$

## ViridianChannel — FEN (FISHERMAN)

- `ViridianChannel_Text_FenAfter` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:8): ROUTE 2 is back to the west.\nI like to stop here before\pheading farther from home.$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_FenDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:5): That was quite a splash!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_FenIntro` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:1): The bridge shakes when people run.\nThe fish always notice first!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_FenRematch` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:13): Back across the CHANNEL?\nLet's see what you have learned!$

## ViridianChannel — HARLAN (FISHERMAN)

- `ViridianChannel_Text_HarlanAfter` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:24): Keep heading east for ROUTE 16.\nFollow the bridges and you can\pcross without entering the water.$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_HarlanDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:21): You reeled that one in!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_HarlanIntro` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:17): I fish from these wide platforms.\nThere is room to let others pass!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_HarlanRematch` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:29): Another crossing, another battle!$

## ViridianChannel — CORAL (SWIMMER_F)

- `ViridianChannel_Text_CoralAfter` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:39): The rocks narrow the swimming\nspace here. I stay close to\pthe crossing when I need a rest.$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_CoralDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:36): Time to catch my breath!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_CoralIntro` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:32): The water feels different from\nthe view up on the bridge!$
- `ViridianChannel_Text_CoralRematch` (data/maps/ViridianChannel/text.inc:44): Ready for another round\nout on the water?$

## ViridianCity_Gym — GIOVANNI (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `ViridianCity_Gym_Text_ExplainEarthBadgeTakeThis` (data/maps/ViridianCity_Gym/text.inc:45): The EARTHBADGE is evidence of\nyour mastery as a TRAINER.\pBut obedience is earned through\nthe trust recorded in your\lFIELD AIDE work.\pWith all eight BADGES, head west\nfrom VIRIDIAN onto ROUTE 22.\pThen go north through ROUTE 23\nand VICTORY ROAD to reach\lthe INDIGO LEAGUE.$
- `ViridianCity_Gym_Text_GiovanniDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianCity_Gym/text.inc:13): Ha!\nThat was a truly intense fight.\lYou have won!\pAs proof, here is the EARTHBADGE!\n{PAUSE_MUSIC}{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_OBTAIN_BADGE}{PAUSE 0xFE}{PAUSE 0x56}{RESUME_MUSIC}$
- `ViridianCity_Gym_Text_GiovanniIntro` (data/maps/ViridianCity_Gym/text.inc:1): GIOVANNI: You have seen me seek\npower in ruins, in industry, and\lbefore MEWTWO itself.\pEvery time, you stood in my way.\pVery well.\nNo machines. No inventions.\lNo stolen advantage.\pOnly a TRAINER and his team.\pFace GIOVANNI, LEADER of the\nVIRIDIAN GYM!{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_ENCOUNTER_ROCKET}$
- `ViridianCity_Gym_Text_GiovanniPostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianCity_Gym/text.inc:20): GIOVANNI: I chased absolute power\nbecause I mistook control for\lstrength.\pA creature in a vat.\nA BALL that never fails.\lA command no one could refuse.\pBut MEWTWO answered control with\ndefiance.\pMy own POKéMON answered trust.\pYou stood before power without\ntrying to own it.\pYou trusted your team, and they\ntrusted you.\pThat is what I had forgotten.\nThat is what TEAM ROCKET must\llearn.\pAs of today, TEAM ROCKET changes\ncourse.\pNo more shadows.\nNo more conquest.\pI will begin again, not as a tyrant,\nbut as a TRAINER.\pFarewell, {PLAYER}.$

## ViridianForest — RICK (BUG_CATCHER)

- `ViridianForest_Text_RickDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:12): No!\nCATERPIE got tangled up in the\nforest pace!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_RickIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:7): Hey! You have POKéMON!\nCome on!\lLet's battle 'em!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_RickPostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:17): Ssh! You'll scare the bugs away.\nAnother time, okay?$

## ViridianForest — DOUG (BUG_CATCHER)

- `ViridianForest_Text_DougDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:26): Huh?\nI ran out of POKéMON!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_DougIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:21): Yo!\nYou can't jam out if you're a\lPOKéMON TRAINER!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_DougPostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:30): That totally stinks! I'm going to\ncatch some stronger ones!$

## ViridianForest — SAMMY (BUG_CATCHER)

- `ViridianForest_Text_SammyDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:38): I give!\nYou're good at this!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_SammyIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:34): Hey, wait up!\nWhat's the hurry? Why the rush?$
- `ViridianForest_Text_SammyPostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:42): Sometimes, you can find stuff on\nthe ground.\pI'm looking for the stuff I\ndropped. Can you help me?$

## ViridianForest — ANTHONY (BUG_CATCHER)

- `ViridianForest_Text_AnthonyDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:52): Oh, boo.\nNothing went right.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_AnthonyIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:48): I might be little, but I won't like\nit if you go easy on me!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_AnthonyPostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:56): I lost some of my allowance…$

## ViridianForest — CHARLIE (BUG_CATCHER)

- `ViridianForest_Text_CharlieDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:62): Oh!\nGuess evolving fast isn't the same\nas battling well.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_CharlieIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:59): Did you know that POKéMON evolve?$
- `ViridianForest_Text_CharliePostBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:67): BUG POKéMON evolve quickly.\nThey're a lot of fun!$

## ViridianForest — ERIKA (LEADER)

- `Common_Text_NeedTwoPokemonForDoubleBattle` (data/scripts/trainer_battle.inc:102): You'll need two POKéMON with you\nfor a DOUBLE BATTLE.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaAfterBattle` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:133): ERIKA: You are remarkably strong.\pThis battle was witnessed, so it\nshall stand as your GYM challenge.\pI must confer on you the\nRAINBOWBADGE.\n{PAUSE_MUSIC}{PLAY_BGM}{MUS_OBTAIN_BADGE}{PAUSE 0xFE}{PAUSE 0x56}{RESUME_MUSIC}\pI saw trust in the way your\nPOKéMON stood with you.\pFriendship can guide evolution\nas surely as sunlight guides\la bud into bloom.\pPlease come to CELADON GYM.\nThere, I will give a reward for\lthe growth you have shown.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaBattleIntro` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:123): Very well.\pShow me how friendship has helped\nyour POKéMON grow.\pLet us begin.{PLAY_BGM MUS_ENCOUNTER_GYM_LEADER}$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaChallenge` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:110): ERIKA: How fortunate.\nThat is just the lesson I hoped\lthis forest would offer.\pSome POKéMON bloom only when\nfriendship has taken root.\pThat kind of evolution is a bond,\nnot merely a change in form.\pBefore I return, may I ask you for\na proper GYM challenge?\pWith my GYM GUIDE present, the\nmatch will be official.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaDefeat` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:129): Oh!\nI concede defeat.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaLookingForRoot` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:77): ERIKA: Oh, hello.\pI came to VIRIDIAN FOREST in\nsearch of an ENERGY ROOT.\pRoots are quiet things, yet they\nbind a whole garden together.\pA root grown here is said to heal\nbecause the forest shares its\lstrength as one family.\pWild ODDISH and BELLSPROUT\nsometimes carry ENERGY ROOTS.\pCatch one here and check its\nheld item. Bring a root to me.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ErikaShownRoot` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:91): ERIKA: Oh!\nThat ENERGY ROOT is splendid.\pSee how many tiny roots gather\ninto one strong whole?\pIt reminds me that families,\nfriends, and communities grow\lbest together.\pIf I could find one of my own, I\nwould gladly return to the GYM.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_ExplainRainbowKey` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:153): The RAINBOW KEY opens the\nBERRY PATCH next door to\lCELADON GYM.\pPlease feel free to pick the\nberries growing there.$
- `ViridianForest_Text_GymAideTypeAdvice` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:168): GYM AIDE: Before you battle,\nremember ERIKA uses GRASS types.\pFIRE, ICE, FLYING, BUG and POISON\nmoves are strong against GRASS.\pA second type can change that.\nWatch out for status moves, too!$
- `ViridianForest_Text_GymGuideFoundErika` (data/maps/ViridianForest/text.inc:102): ERIKA!\nI am so glad I found you!\pAnd look, I found an ENERGY ROOT\non the way here.\pIt was tucked among other roots,\nall holding the soil together.$

## ViridianForest — NIKKI (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4425): Your team made quite an\nimpression!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4417): Something sweet is drifting\nfrom deeper in the forest.\pFollow it after our battle!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4428): The flowers in VIRIDIAN FOREST\nare so beautiful.\pThey seem more vibrant and grow\nin number the closer you get to\lthe centre of the forest.\pAlmost like something is drawing\nthem inward.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_NIKKI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4421): That rich scent is here\nagain. Let us see what else\phas changed!$
- `Text_ApexRumorRecorded` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:102): Rumour recorded.\pCompare three accounts in your\nLOGBOOK to find an APEX POKéMON.\pIf you flee from its battle,\nit will leave for good.$
- `Text_ApexRumorUpdated` (data/scripts/static_pokemon.inc:109): Rumour updated.$

## ViridianForest — VIOLET (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4457): A stronger blend than mine!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4450): I blend scents a little at a\ntime. My battle plan needs\pjust the right balance!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4459): One strong scent can drown\nout the rest. I give each of\pmy POKéMON room to shine.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_VIOLET_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4454): I adjusted my formula. Would\nyou help me test it?$

## ViridianForest — ROSE (AROMA_LADY)

- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_ROSE_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4443): Our moment passed so quickly!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_ROSE_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4436): Some flowers open only in\nshade. My POKéMON are ready\pto come out!$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_ROSE_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4445): I leave woodland flowers\nwhere they grow. Their roots\pneed more than a vase can\noffer.$
- `EventScript_GymAdjacent_AROMA_LADY_ROSE_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4440): We have rested in the shade.\nTime to bloom again!$

## ViridianForest — NILES (BUG_CATCHER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_NILES_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4228): My surprise came unwrapped!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_NILES_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4224): See that curled leaf? Something\nbuilt a shelter inside it! My\pbugs have a surprise too!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_NILES_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4230): I leave curled leaves alone. I'd\nhate someone tearing down my\proof to say hello.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_CATCHER_NILES_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4226): I found more leaf shelters! My\nteam has been busy growing too!$

## ViridianForest — CEDAR (BUG_MANIAC)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_CEDAR_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4244): An unexpected result!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_CEDAR_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4240): I compare the patterns on shed\nskins. No two finds are quite\palike. Let me study your team!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_CEDAR_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4246): An empty shell isn't a dead bug.\nIt may be proof that something\pis still growing.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_BUG_MANIAC_CEDAR_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4242): New notes, new questions. Shall\nwe test another theory?$

## ViridianForest — HAZEL (POKÉMON_RANGER_F)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_HAZEL_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4405): You handled that with care!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_HAZEL_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4401): I keep fallen branches off the\ntrail. A clear path protects the\pplants beside it. Ready for a\nfield test?$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_HAZEL_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4407): I move a branch only as far as I\nneed to. Something may still be\pliving beneath it.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_RANGER_HAZEL_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4403): The trail is clear again. Let's\ncheck how your team is doing!$

## ViridianForest — ELIN (POKÉMON_BREEDER)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ELI_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4375): They stayed brave to the end!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ELI_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4371): My young POKéMON startle at\nrustling leaves. We take little\pwalks to build their confidence.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ELI_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4377): I let them investigate a sound\nbefore moving on. Confidence\pgrows faster than orders can\nforce it.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_PKMN_BREEDER_ELI_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4373): They recognised your footsteps!\nWould you help with another\plesson?$

## ViridianForest — PAX (SCOUT_M)

- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_PAX_Text_Defeat` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4321): I lost your trail!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_PAX_Text_Intro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4313): I follow tracks without\nstepping on them. Try to stay\pahead of my team!$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_PAX_Text_PostBattle` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4323): Bent grass shows a direction.\nA single print rarely tells\pthe whole story.$
- `EventScript_ForestTrainer_CAMPER_PAX_Text_RematchIntro` (data/scripts/trainers.inc:4317): Fresh tracks, familiar\ntrainer! Ready for another\pround?$
